```
1→"""Structural signal helpers shared across language phase implementations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import resolve_path
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import count_lines
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    11→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    12→
    13→
    14→def add_structural_signal(structural: dict, file: str, signal: str, detail: dict):
    15→    """Add a complexity signal to the per-file structural dict.
    16→
    17→    Accumulates signals per file so they can be merged into tiered issues.
    18→    """
    19→    f = resolve_path(file)
    20→    structural.setdefault(f, {"signals": [], "detail": {}})
    21→    structural[f]["signals"].append(signal)
    22→    structural[f]["detail"].update(detail)
    23→
    24→
    25→def merge_structural_signals(
    26→    structural: dict,
    27→    stderr_fn,
    28→    *,
    29→    complexity_only_min: int = 50,
    30→) -> list[Issue]:
    31→    """Convert per-file structural signals into tiered issues.
    32→
    33→    3+ signals -> T4/high (needs decomposition).
    34→    1-2 signals -> T3/medium.
    35→    Complexity-only files (no large/god signals) need score >= complexity_only_min
    36→    to be flagged — lower complexity in small files is normal, not decomposition-worthy.
    37→    """
    38→    results = []
    39→    suppressed = 0
    40→    for filepath, data in structural.items():
    41→        if "loc" not in data["detail"]:
    42→            try:
    43→                p = (
    44→                    Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
    45→                )
    46→                data["detail"]["loc"] = count_lines(p)
    47→            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
    48→                data["detail"]["loc"] = 0
    49→
    50→        # Suppress complexity-only issues below the elevated threshold.
    51→        signals = data["signals"]
    52→        is_complexity_only = all(s.startswith("complexity") for s in signals)
    53→        if is_complexity_only:
    54→            score = data["detail"].get("complexity_score", 0)
    55→            if score < complexity_only_min:
    56→                suppressed += 1
    57→                continue
    58→
    59→        signal_count = len(signals)
    60→        tier = 4 if signal_count >= 3 else 3
    61→        confidence = "high" if signal_count >= 3 else "medium"
    62→        summary = "Large file: " + " / ".join(signals)
    63→        results.append(
    64→            make_issue(
    65→                "structural",
    66→                filepath,
    67→                "",
    68→                tier=tier,
    69→                confidence=confidence,
    70→                summary=summary,
    71→                detail=data["detail"],
    72→            )
    73→        )
    74→    if suppressed:
    75→        stderr_fn(
    76→            "         "
    77→            f"{suppressed} complexity-only files below threshold (< {complexity_only_min})"
    78→        )
    79→    stderr_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} structural issues")
    80→    return results
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = ["add_structural_signal", "merge_structural_signals"]
    84→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Callable
     8→
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (

    13→
    14→if TYPE_CHECKING:

    16→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import TreeSitterLangSpec
    17→    from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    18→
    19→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→
    22→def _make_structural_phase(
    23→    treesitter_spec: TreeSitterLangSpec | None = None,
    24→) -> DetectorPhase:
    25→    """Create a structural analysis phase for generic plugins."""
    26→    from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
    27→    from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import ComplexitySignal
    28→
    29→    signals = [
    30→        ComplexitySignal(
    31→            "TODOs",
    32→            r"(?://|#|--|/\*)\s*(?:TODO|FIXME|HACK|XXX)",
    33→            weight=2,
    34→            threshold=0,
    35→        ),
    36→    ]
    37→
    38→    if treesitter_spec is not None:
    39→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import is_available
    40→
    41→        if is_available():
    42→            from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.complexity_function_metrics import (
    43→                make_cyclomatic_complexity_compute,
    44→                make_long_functions_compute,
    45→                make_max_params_compute,
    46→            )
    47→            from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.complexity_nesting import (
    48→                make_callback_depth_compute,
    49→                make_nesting_depth_compute,
    50→            )
    51→
    52→            signals.append(
    53→                ComplexitySignal(
    54→                    "nesting_depth",
    55→                    None,
    56→                    weight=3,
    57→                    threshold=4,
    58→                    compute=make_nesting_depth_compute(treesitter_spec),
    59→                )
    60→            )
    61→            signals.append(
    62→                ComplexitySignal(
    63→                    "long_functions",
    64→                    None,
    65→                    weight=3,
    66→                    threshold=80,
    67→                    compute=make_long_functions_compute(treesitter_spec),
    68→                )
    69→            )
    70→            signals.append(
    71→                ComplexitySignal(
    72→                    "cyclomatic_complexity",
    73→                    None,
    74→                    weight=2,
    75→                    threshold=15,
    76→                    compute=make_cyclomatic_complexity_compute(treesitter_spec),
    77→                )
    78→            )
    79→            signals.append(
    80→                ComplexitySignal(
    81→                    "many_params",
    82→                    None,
    83→                    weight=2,
    84→                    threshold=7,
    85→                    compute=make_max_params_compute(treesitter_spec),
    86→                )
    87→            )
    88→            signals.append(
    89→                ComplexitySignal(
    90→                    "callback_depth",
    91→                    None,
    92→                    weight=2,
    93→                    threshold=3,
    94→                    compute=make_callback_depth_compute(treesitter_spec),
    95→                )
    96→            )
    97→
    98→    god_rules = None
    99→    has_class_query = treesitter_spec is not None and treesitter_spec.class_query
   100→    if has_class_query:
   101→        from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import GodRule
   102→
   103→        god_rules = [
   104→            GodRule("methods", "methods", lambda c: len(c.methods), 15),
   105→            GodRule("loc", "LOC", lambda c: c.loc, 500),
   106→            GodRule("attributes", "attributes", lambda c: len(c.attributes), 10),
   107→        ]
   108→
   109→    def run(
   110→        path: Path,
   111→        lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   112→    ) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   113→        from desloppify.languages._framework.base.shared_phases import (
   114→            run_structural_phase,
   115→        )
   116→
   117→        god_extractor_fn = None
   118→        if god_rules and has_class_query:
   119→            god_extractor_fn = _make_god_extractor(treesitter_spec, lang.file_finder)
   120→
   121→        return run_structural_phase(
   122→            path,
   123→            lang,
   124→            complexity_signals=signals,
   125→            log_fn=log,
   126→            min_loc=40,
   127→            god_rules=god_rules,
   128→            god_extractor_fn=god_extractor_fn,
   129→        )
   130→
   131→    return DetectorPhase("Structural analysis", run)
   132→
   133→
   134→def _make_god_extractor(treesitter_spec, file_finder):
   135→    """Create a god-class extractor function bound to the given spec."""
   136→
   137→    def extractor(path: Path):
   138→        return _extract_ts_classes(path, treesitter_spec, file_finder)
   139→
   140→    return extractor
   141→
   142→
   143→def _extract_ts_classes(path, treesitter_spec, file_finder):
   144→    """Extract classes with methods populated via tree-sitter.
   145→
   146→    Returns [] on any error (graceful degradation).
   147→    """
   148→    try:
   149→        from collections import defaultdict
   150→
   151→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.extractors import (
   152→            ts_extract_classes,
   153→            ts_extract_functions,
   154→        )
   155→
   156→        file_list = file_finder(path)
   157→        classes = ts_extract_classes(path, treesitter_spec, file_list)
   158→        if not classes:
   159→            return classes
   160→
   161→        functions = ts_extract_functions(path, treesitter_spec, file_list)
   162→        by_file = defaultdict(list)
   163→        for function in functions:
   164→            by_file[function.file].append(function)
   165→        for cls in classes:
   166→            cls_end = cls.line + cls.loc
   167→            for function in by_file.get(cls.file, []):
   168→                if cls.line <= function.line <= cls_end:
   169→                    cls.methods.append(function)
   170→
   171→        return classes
   172→    except _TS_INIT_ERRORS as exc:
   173→        logger.debug("tree-sitter class extraction failed: %s", exc)
   174→        return []
   175→
   176→
   177→def _make_coupling_phase(
   178→    dep_graph_fn: Callable[[Path], dict[str, dict[str, object]]],
   179→) -> DetectorPhase:
   180→    """Create a coupling phase for generic plugins with a dep graph."""
   181→    from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
   182→
   183→    def run(
   184→        path: Path,
   185→        lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   186→    ) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   187→        from desloppify.languages._framework.base.shared_phases import (
   188→            run_coupling_phase,
   189→        )
   190→
   191→        return run_coupling_phase(
   192→            path,
   193→            lang,
   194→            build_dep_graph_fn=dep_graph_fn,
   195→            log_fn=log,
   196→        )
   197→
   198→    return DetectorPhase("Coupling + cycles + orphaned", run)
   199→
```

```
1→"""Structural/coupling phase runners shared by language runtimes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import ComplexitySignal
    10→from desloppify.engine.detectors.complexity import detect_complexity
    11→from desloppify.engine.detectors.flat_dirs import (
    12→    FlatDirDetectionConfig,
    13→    detect_flat_dirs,
    14→    format_flat_dir_summary,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine.detectors.graph import detect_cycles
    17→from desloppify.engine.detectors.large import detect_large_files
    18→from desloppify.engine.detectors.orphaned import (
    19→    OrphanedDetectionOptions,
    20→    detect_orphaned_files,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine.detectors.single_use import detect_single_use_abstractions
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    24→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import adjust_potential, filter_entries
    25→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.structural import (
    26→    add_structural_signal,
    27→    merge_structural_signals,
    28→)
    29→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangRuntimeContract
    30→from desloppify.languages._framework.issue_factories import (
    31→    make_cycle_issues,
    32→    make_orphaned_issues,
    33→    make_single_use_issues,
    34→)
    35→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    36→
    37→StructuralPhaseRunner = Callable[
    38→    [Path, LangRuntimeContract],
    39→    tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]],
    40→]
    41→
    42→
    43→def run_structural_phase(
    44→    path: Path,
    45→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
    46→    *,
    47→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
    48→    log_fn,
    49→    min_loc: int = 40,
    50→    god_rules=None,
    51→    god_extractor_fn=None,
    52→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    53→    """Run large/complexity/flat-directory detectors for a language."""
    54→    structural: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    55→
    56→    large_entries, file_count = detect_large_files(
    57→        path,
    58→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
    59→        threshold=lang.large_threshold,
    60→    )
    61→    for entry in large_entries:
    62→        add_structural_signal(
    63→            structural,
    64→            entry["file"],
    65→            f"large ({entry['loc']} LOC)",
    66→            {"loc": entry["loc"]},
    67→        )
    68→
    69→    complexity_entries, _ = detect_complexity(
    70→        path,
    71→        signals=complexity_signals,
    72→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
    73→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
    74→        min_loc=min_loc,
    75→    )
    76→    for entry in complexity_entries:
    77→        add_structural_signal(
    78→            structural,
    79→            entry["file"],
    80→            f"complexity score {entry['score']}",
    81→            {
    82→                "complexity_score": entry["score"],
    83→                "complexity_signals": entry["signals"],
    84→            },
    85→        )
    86→        lang.complexity_map[entry["file"]] = entry["score"]
    87→
    88→    if god_rules and god_extractor_fn:
    89→        from desloppify.engine.detectors.gods import detect_gods
    90→
    91→        god_entries, _ = detect_gods(god_extractor_fn(path), god_rules, min_reasons=2)
    92→        for entry in god_entries:
    93→            add_structural_signal(
    94→                structural,
    95→                entry["file"],
    96→                entry["signal_text"],
    97→                entry["detail"],
    98→            )
    99→        if god_entries:
   100→            log_fn(f"         god classes: {len(god_entries)}")
   101→
   102→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log_fn)
   103→    flat_entries, analyzed_dir_count = detect_flat_dirs(
   104→        path,
   105→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   106→        config=FlatDirDetectionConfig(),
   107→    )
   108→    for entry in flat_entries:
   109→        child_dir_count = int(entry.get("child_dir_count", 0))
   110→        combined_score = int(entry.get("combined_score", entry.get("file_count", 0)))
   111→        results.append(
   112→            make_issue(
   113→                "flat_dirs",
   114→                entry["directory"],
   115→                "",
   116→                tier=3,
   117→                confidence="medium",
   118→                summary=format_flat_dir_summary(entry),
   119→                detail={
   120→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
   121→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   122→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   123→                    "kind": entry.get("kind", "overload"),
   124→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(entry.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   125→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(entry.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   126→                    "sparse_child_count": int(entry.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   127→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(entry.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   128→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   129→                        entry.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   130→                    ),
   131→                },
   132→            )
   133→        )
   134→    if flat_entries:
   135→        log_fn(
   136→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   137→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    potentials = {
   141→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   142→        "flat_dirs": analyzed_dir_count,
   143→    }
   144→    return results, potentials
   145→
   146→
   147→def run_coupling_phase(
   148→    path: Path,
   149→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   150→    *,
   151→    build_dep_graph_fn,
   152→    log_fn,
   153→    post_process_fn=None,
   154→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   155→    """Run single-use/cycles/orphaned detectors against a language dep graph."""
   156→    graph = build_dep_graph_fn(path)
   157→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   158→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   159→    results: list[Issue] = []
   160→
   161→    single_entries, single_candidates = detect_single_use_abstractions(
   162→        path,
   163→        graph,
   164→        barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   165→    )
   166→    single_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, single_entries, "single_use")
   167→    single_issues = make_single_use_issues(single_entries, lang.get_area, stderr_fn=log_fn)
   168→    if post_process_fn:
   169→        post_process_fn(single_issues, single_entries, lang)
   170→    results.extend(single_issues)
   171→
   172→    cycle_entries, _ = detect_cycles(graph)
   173→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   174→    results.extend(make_cycle_issues(cycle_entries, log_fn))
   175→
   176→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = detect_orphaned_files(
   177→        path,
   178→        graph,
   179→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   180→        options=OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   181→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   182→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   183→        ),
   184→    )
   185→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   186→    orphan_issues = make_orphaned_issues(orphan_entries, log_fn)
   187→    if post_process_fn:
   188→        post_process_fn(orphan_issues, orphan_entries, lang)
   189→    results.extend(orphan_issues)
   190→
   191→    log_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} coupling/structural issues total")
   192→    potentials = {
   193→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zone_map, single_candidates),
   194→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   195→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   196→    }
   197→    return results, potentials
   198→
   199→
   200→def make_structural_coupling_phase_pair(
   201→    *,
   202→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
   203→    build_dep_graph_fn,
   204→    log_fn,
   205→) -> tuple[StructuralPhaseRunner, StructuralPhaseRunner]:
   206→    """Create default structural/coupling phase callables for a language."""
   207→
   208→    def phase_structural(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   209→        return run_structural_phase(
   210→            path,
   211→            lang,
   212→            complexity_signals=complexity_signals,
   213→            log_fn=log_fn,
   214→        )
   215→
   216→    def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   217→        return run_coupling_phase(
   218→            path,
   219→            lang,
   220→            build_dep_graph_fn=build_dep_graph_fn,
   221→            log_fn=log_fn,
   222→        )
   223→
   224→    return phase_structural, phase_coupling
   225→
   226→
   227→__all__ = [
   228→    "make_structural_coupling_phase_pair",
   229→    "run_coupling_phase",
   230→    "run_structural_phase",
   231→]
   232→
```

```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and issue pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import re
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "issue_in_scan_scope",

    19→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    21→    Issue,
    22→    StateModel,
    23→    ensure_state_defaults,
    24→    utc_now,
    25→    validate_state_invariants,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (
    28→    issue_in_scan_scope as _issue_in_scan_scope,

    30→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (
    31→    open_scope_breakdown as _open_scope_breakdown,

    34→    path_scoped_issues as _path_scoped_issues,
    35→)
    36→
    37→
    38→def path_scoped_issues(
    39→    issues: dict[str, Issue],
    40→    scan_path: str | None,
    41→) -> dict[str, Issue]:
    42→    """Filter issues to those within the given scan path."""
    43→    return _path_scoped_issues(issues, scan_path)
    44→
    45→
    46→def issue_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    47→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    48→    return _issue_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path)
    49→
    50→
    51→def open_scope_breakdown(
    52→    issues: dict[str, Issue],
    53→    scan_path: str | None,
    54→    *,
    55→    detector: str | None = None,
    56→) -> dict[str, int]:
    57→    """Return open-issue counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    58→    return _open_scope_breakdown(
    59→        issues,
    60→        scan_path,
    61→        detector=detector,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def is_ignored(issue_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    66→    """Check if a issue matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    67→    return matched_ignore_pattern(issue_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    68→
    69→
    70→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    71→    issue_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    72→) -> str | None:
    73→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    74→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    75→        if "*" in pattern:
    76→            target = issue_id if "::" in pattern else file
    77→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    78→                return pattern
    79→            continue
    80→
    81→        if "::" in pattern:
    82→            if issue_id.startswith(pattern):
    83→                return pattern
    84→            continue
    85→
    86→        raw_base = pattern.rstrip("/")
    87→        rel_base = rel(pattern).rstrip("/")
    88→        for base in (raw_base, rel_base):
    89→            if not base:
    90→                continue
    91→            if file == base or file.startswith(base + "/"):
    92→                return pattern
    93→
    94→    return None
    95→
    96→
    97→def remove_ignored_issues(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
    98→    """Suppress issues matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
    99→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   100→    matched_ids = [
   101→        issue_id
   102→        for issue_id, issue in state["issues"].items()
   103→        if is_ignored(issue_id, issue["file"], [pattern])
   104→    ]
   105→    now = utc_now()
   106→    for issue_id in matched_ids:
   107→        issue = state["issues"][issue_id]
   108→        issue["suppressed"] = True
   109→        issue["suppressed_at"] = now
   110→        issue["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   111→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_integration import (
   112→        recompute_stats as _recompute_stats,
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   116→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   117→    return len(matched_ids)
   118→
   119→
   120→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   121→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching issues."""
   122→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   123→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   124→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   125→    if pattern not in ignores:
   126→        ignores.append(pattern)
   127→    return remove_ignored_issues(state, pattern)
   128→
   129→
   130→def make_issue(
   131→    detector: str,
   132→    file: str,
   133→    name: str,
   134→    *,
   135→    tier: int,
   136→    confidence: str,
   137→    summary: str,
   138→    detail: dict | None = None,
   139→) -> Issue:
   140→    """Create a normalized issue dict with a stable ID."""
   141→    rfile = rel(file)
   142→    issue_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    return {
   145→        "id": issue_id,
   146→        "detector": detector,
   147→        "file": rfile,
   148→        "tier": tier,
   149→        "confidence": confidence,
   150→        "summary": summary,
   151→        "detail": detail or {},
   152→        "status": "open",
   153→        "note": None,
   154→        "first_seen": now,
   155→        "last_seen": now,
   156→        "resolved_at": None,
   157→        "reopen_count": 0,
   158→    }
   159→
   160→
   161→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   162→
   163→
   164→def _matches_issue_path(issue: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   165→    """Match against the issue's detector name or file path."""
   166→    return (
   167→        issue.get("detector") == pattern
   168→        or issue["file"] == pattern
   169→        or issue["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   170→    )
   171→
   172→
   173→def _matches_issue_name_segment(issue_id: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
   174→    """Match against the name segment of the issue ID.
   175→
   176→    For hashed IDs (detector::path::name::hex8), also match the descriptive
   177→    name (second-to-last segment).  Returns False for IDs without :: or
   178→    patterns containing ::.
   179→    """
   180→    if "::" in pattern or "::" not in issue_id:
   181→        return False
   182→    segments = issue_id.split("::")
   183→    name_segment = segments[-1]
   184→    if name_segment == pattern:
   185→        return True
   186→    if len(segments) < 3 or not _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment):
   187→        return False
   188→    return segments[-2] == pattern
   189→
   190→
   191→def _matches_pattern(issue_id: str, issue: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   192→    """Check if a issue matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   193→    if issue_id == pattern:
   194→        return True
   195→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(issue_id, pattern):
   196→        return True
   197→    if "::" in pattern and issue_id.startswith(pattern):
   198→        return True
   199→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and issue_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   200→        return True
   201→    if _matches_issue_path(issue, pattern):
   202→        return True
   203→    if _matches_issue_name_segment(issue_id, pattern):
   204→        return True
   205→
   206→    return False
   207→
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me create a summary of my findings:

## Summary: How the `structural` Detector Assigns Confidence Levels

The structural detector assigns confidence levels based on the **number of signals (complexity issues) detected** in a file. Here's exactly how it works:

### Confidence Assignment Logic

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/structural.py` (lines 59-61):

```python
signal_count = len(signals)
tier = 4 if signal_count >= 3 else 3
confidence = "high" if signal_count >= 3 else "medium"
```

**The rules are simple:**
- **3+ signals → HIGH confidence (Tier 4)** — "needs decomposition"
- **1-2 signals → MEDIUM confidence (Tier 3)** — "needs refactoring"

### What Counts as a Signal?

Signals are accumulated from multiple detectors via the `add_structural_signal()` function. The structural phase combines:

1. **Large files** — from `detect_large_files()` (LOC exceeds language threshold, typically 100+ lines)
2. **Complexity issues** — from `detect_complexity()`, which evaluates:
   - TODOs/FIXMEs (weight 2)
   - Nesting depth > 4 (weight 3)
   - Long functions > 80 LOC (weight 3)
   - Cyclomatic complexity > 15 (weight 2)
   - Functions with > 7 parameters (weight 2)
   - Callback nesting depth > 3 (weight 2)
3. **God classes** — from `detect_gods()` (classes with 15+ methods, 500+ LOC, or 10+ attributes)

### Additional Filter: Complexity-Only Suppression

Files with **only complexity signals** (no "large" or "god" signals) have a secondary threshold: they're suppressed if their `complexity_score < 50` (line 55). This prevents flagging normal small files with minor complexity issues.

### Full Workflow

The `merge_structural_signals()` function (lines 25-80 in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/structural.py`) orchestrates this:
1. Collects all signals per file
2. Suppresses complexity-only files below threshold
3. Assigns tier/confidence based on signal count
4. Creates a single "Large file" issue with summary like: `"Large file: large (200 LOC) / complexity score 21 / god class (500 LOC)"`

### Example

- A file with **large size + complexity issue + god class methods** = 3 signals = **HIGH confidence, Tier 4**
- A file with **just complexity issue** = 1 signal = **MEDIUM confidence, Tier 3** (unless suppressed if score < 50)

```
    42→        if not isinstance(raw, str):
    43→            continue
    44→        file_path = raw.strip().replace("\\", "/")
    45→        if file_path:
    46→            out.add(file_path)
    47→    return out
    48→
    49→
    50→def gather_mechanical_evidence(
    51→    state: StateModel,
    52→    *,
    53→    allowed_files: set[str] | list[str] | tuple[str, ...] | None = None,
    54→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    55→    """Aggregate open issues into evidence clusters for holistic review."""
    56→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    57→    if not issues:
    58→        return {}
    59→    allowed_scope = _normalize_allowed_files(allowed_files)
    60→
    61→    by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    62→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    63→    smell_counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
    64→    smell_files: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    65→
    66→    for issue in issues.values():
    67→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    68→            continue
    69→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    70→            continue
    71→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    72→        normalized_file = filepath.strip().replace("\\", "/") if isinstance(filepath, str) else ""
    73→        if allowed_scope is not None and normalized_file not in allowed_scope:
    74→            continue
    75→        det = issue.get("detector", "")
    76→        if det:
    77→            by_detector[det].append(issue)
    78→        if normalized_file and normalized_file != ".":
    79→            by_file[normalized_file].append(issue)
    80→        if det == "smells":
    81→            detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    82→            smell_id = detail.get("smell_id", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
    83→            if smell_id:
    84→                smell_counter[smell_id] += 1
    85→                smell_files[smell_id].append(normalized_file)
    86→
    87→    if not by_detector:
    88→        return {}
    89→
    90→    evidence: dict[str, Any] = {}
    91→
    92→    complexity_hotspots = _build_complexity_hotspots(by_detector, by_file)
    93→    if complexity_hotspots:
    94→        evidence["complexity_hotspots"] = complexity_hotspots
    95→
    96→    error_hotspots = _build_error_hotspots(by_detector)
    97→    if error_hotspots:
    98→        evidence["error_hotspots"] = error_hotspots
    99→
   100→    mutable_globals = _build_mutable_globals(by_detector)
   101→    if mutable_globals:
   102→        evidence["mutable_globals"] = mutable_globals
   103→
   104→    boundary_violations = _build_boundary_violations(by_detector)
   105→    if boundary_violations:
   106→        evidence["boundary_violations"] = boundary_violations
   107→
   108→    dead_code = _build_dead_code(by_detector)
   109→    if dead_code:
   110→        evidence["dead_code"] = dead_code
   111→
   112→    private_crossings = _build_private_crossings(by_detector)
   113→    if private_crossings:
   114→        evidence["private_crossings"] = private_crossings
   115→
   116→    deferred = _build_deferred_import_density(by_file)
   117→    if deferred:
   118→        evidence["deferred_import_density"] = deferred
   119→
   120→    duplicate_clusters = _build_duplicate_clusters(by_detector)
   121→    if duplicate_clusters:
   122→        evidence["duplicate_clusters"] = duplicate_clusters
   123→
   124→    naming_drift = _build_naming_drift(by_detector)
   125→    if naming_drift:
   126→        evidence["naming_drift"] = naming_drift
   127→
   128→    flat_dir_issues = _build_flat_dir_issues(by_detector)
   129→    if flat_dir_issues:
   130→        evidence["flat_dir_issues"] = flat_dir_issues
   131→
   132→    large_dist = _build_large_file_distribution(by_detector)
   133→    if large_dist:
   134→        evidence["large_file_distribution"] = large_dist
   135→
   136→    security_hotspots = _build_security_hotspots(by_detector)
   137→    if security_hotspots:
   138→        evidence["security_hotspots"] = security_hotspots
   139→
   140→    signal_density = _build_signal_density(by_file)
   141→    if signal_density:
   142→        evidence["signal_density"] = signal_density
   143→
   144→    systemic = _build_systemic_patterns(smell_counter, smell_files)
   145→    if systemic:
   146→        evidence["systemic_patterns"] = systemic
   147→
   148→    pkg_census = _build_package_size_census(by_file)
   149→    if pkg_census:
   150→        evidence["package_size_census"] = pkg_census
   151→
   152→    return evidence
   153→
   154→
   155→def _build_package_size_census(
   156→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]],
   157→) -> list[dict]:
   158→    """Compute LOC per top-level package.  Flag packages >15% of codebase."""
   159→    pkg_loc: dict[str, int] = {}
   160→    for filepath, issues in by_file.items():
   161→        parts = filepath.replace("\\", "/").split("/")
   162→        pkg = parts[0] if parts else filepath
   163→        # Sum file LOC from structural detail or count issues as proxy.
   164→        file_loc = 0
   165→        for issue in issues:
   166→            if issue.get("detector") == "structural":
   167→                detail = issue.get("detail", {})
   168→                if isinstance(detail, dict):
   169→                    loc_val = detail.get("loc", 0)
   170→                    if isinstance(loc_val, int | float) and loc_val > file_loc:
   171→                        file_loc = int(loc_val)
   172→        if file_loc == 0:
   173→            file_loc = 1  # Count the file at minimum
   174→        pkg_loc[pkg] = pkg_loc.get(pkg, 0) + file_loc
   175→
   176→    total_loc = sum(pkg_loc.values())
   177→    if total_loc == 0:
   178→        return []
   179→
   180→    results = []
   181→    for pkg, loc in sorted(pkg_loc.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1]):
   182→        pct = round(100 * loc / total_loc, 1)
   183→        results.append({
   184→            "package": pkg,
   185→            "loc": loc,
   186→            "pct_of_total": pct,
   187→            "disproportionate": pct > 15,
   188→        })
   189→    return results
   190→
   191→
   192→__all__ = ["gather_mechanical_evidence"]
   193→
```

```
    38→def parse_holistic_import_payload(
    39→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
    40→) -> tuple[list[ReviewIssuePayload], dict[str, Any] | None, list[str]]:
    41→    """Parse strict holistic import payload object."""

    45→
    46→def import_holistic_issues(
    47→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
    48→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    49→    lang_name: str,
    50→    *,
    51→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
    52→    utc_now_fn=state_mod.utc_now,
    53→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    54→    """Import holistic (codebase-wide) issues into state."""
    55→    payload: ReviewImportEnvelope = parse_review_import_payload(
    56→        issues_data,

    58→    )
    59→    issues_list = payload.issues
    60→    assessments = payload.assessments
    61→    reviewed_files = payload.reviewed_files
    62→    dimension_judgment = payload.dimension_judgment
    63→    review_scope = issues_data.get("review_scope", {})
    64→    if not isinstance(review_scope, dict):
    65→        review_scope = {}
    66→    review_scope.setdefault("full_sweep_included", None)
    67→    scope_full_sweep = review_scope.get("full_sweep_included")
    68→    if not isinstance(scope_full_sweep, bool):
    69→        scope_full_sweep = None
    70→
    71→    if assessments:
    72→        store_assessments(
    73→            state,
    74→            assessments,
    75→            source="holistic",
    76→            utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
    77→            dimension_judgment=dimension_judgment,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→    _, holistic_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
    81→    valid_dimensions = {
    82→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
    83→        for dim in holistic_prompts
    84→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    85→    }
    86→    review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns = _validate_and_build_issues(
    87→        issues_list,
    88→        holistic_prompts,
    89→        lang_name,
    90→    )
    91→    imported_dimensions = _collect_imported_dimensions(
    92→        issues_list=issues_list,
    93→        review_issues=review_issues,
    94→        assessments=assessments if isinstance(assessments, dict) else None,
    95→        review_scope=review_scope,
    96→        valid_dimensions=valid_dimensions,
    97→    )
    98→
    99→    if dismissed_concerns:
   100→        store = state.setdefault("concern_dismissals", {})
   101→        now = utc_now_fn()
   102→        current_concerns = generate_concerns(state)
   103→        concern_sources = {
   104→            concern.fingerprint: list(concern.source_issues)
   105→            for concern in current_concerns
   106→        }
   107→        for dismissal in dismissed_concerns:
   108→            fingerprint = dismissal["fingerprint"]
   109→            store[fingerprint] = {
   110→                "dismissed_at": now,
   111→                "reasoning": dismissal.get("reasoning", ""),
   112→                "concern_type": dismissal.get("concern_type", ""),
   113→                "concern_file": dismissal.get("concern_file", ""),
   114→                "source_issue_ids": concern_sources.get(fingerprint, []),
   115→            }
   116→
   117→    potentials = ensure_lang_potentials(state, lang_name)
   118→    existing_review = potentials.get("review", 0)
   119→    potentials["review"] = max(existing_review, HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL)
   120→
   121→    concern_count = sum(1 for issue in review_issues if issue.get("detector") == "concerns")
   122→    if concern_count:
   123→        potentials["concerns"] = max(potentials.get("concerns", 0), concern_count)
   124→
   125→    merge_potentials_dict: dict[str, int] = {"review": potentials.get("review", 0)}
   126→    if potentials.get("concerns", 0) > 0:
   127→        merge_potentials_dict["concerns"] = potentials["concerns"]
   128→
   129→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   130→        state,
   131→        review_issues,
   132→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   133→            lang=lang_name,
   134→            potentials=merge_potentials_dict,
   135→            merge_potentials=True,
   136→        ),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→    new_ids = {issue["id"] for issue in review_issues}
   140→    _auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   141→        state,
   142→        new_ids,
   143→        diff,
   144→        utc_now_fn,
   145→        imported_dimensions=imported_dimensions,
   146→        full_sweep_included=scope_full_sweep,
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    if skipped:
   150→        diff["skipped"] = len(skipped)
   151→        diff["skipped_details"] = skipped
   152→
   153→    update_reviewed_file_cache(
   154→        state,
   155→        reviewed_files,
   156→        project_root=project_root,
   157→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   158→    )
   159→    resolve_reviewed_file_coverage_issues(

   161→        diff,
   162→        reviewed_files,

   164→    )
   165→    update_holistic_review_cache(

   171→    )
   172→    resolve_holistic_coverage_issues(state, diff, utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn)
   173→
   174→    cleanup_stale_dismissals(state)
   175→
   176→    return diff
```

```
1→"""Shared prompt rendering sections used by both batch and external review paths."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (

    10→
    11→
    12→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    13→class PromptBatchContext:
    14→    name: str
    15→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...]
    16→    rationale: str
    17→    seed_files: tuple[str, ...]
    18→    issues_cap: int
    19→
    20→    @property
    21→    def dimension_set(self) -> set[str]:
    22→        return set(self.dimensions)
    23→
    24→    @property
    25→    def dimensions_text(self) -> str:
    26→        return ", ".join(self.dimensions) if self.dimensions else "(none)"
    27→
    28→    @property
    29→    def seed_files_text(self) -> str:
    30→        return "\n".join(f"- {path}" for path in self.seed_files) if self.seed_files else "- (none)"
    31→
    32→
    33→def coerce_string_list(raw: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    34→    if not isinstance(raw, list | tuple):
    35→        return ()
    36→    return tuple(str(item) for item in raw if isinstance(item, str) and item)
    37→
    38→
    39→def build_batch_context(batch: dict[str, object], batch_index: int) -> PromptBatchContext:
    40→    dimensions = coerce_string_list(batch.get("dimensions", []))
    41→    return PromptBatchContext(
    42→        name=str(batch.get("name", f"Batch {batch_index + 1}")),
    43→        dimensions=dimensions,
    44→        rationale=str(batch.get("why", "")).strip(),
    45→        seed_files=coerce_string_list(batch.get("files_to_read", [])),
    46→        issues_cap=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dimensions)),
    47→    )

    86→def render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
    87→    """Render dimension-specific scan_evidence guidance."""
    88→    return "".join(
    89→        text
    90→        for dim, text in SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION.items()
    91→        if dim in dim_set

    94→
    95→def render_historical_focus(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
    96→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
    97→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
    98→        return ""
    99→
   100→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
   101→    try:
   102→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
   103→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   104→        selected_count = 0
   105→
   106→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])
   107→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   108→        issues = []
   109→
   110→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
   111→        return ""
   112→
   113→    lines: list[str] = []
   114→    lines.append(
   115→        "Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:"
   116→    )
   117→    lines.append(
   118→        "Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report"
   119→        " issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or"
   120→        " what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out."
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    for entry in issues:
   124→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   125→            continue
   126→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   127→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   128→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
   129→
   130→        line = f"  - [{status}] {summary}"
   131→        if note:
   132→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   133→        lines.append(line)
   134→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   135→
   136→
   137→def _concern_signal_lines(entry: dict[str, object]) -> list[str]:
   138→    """Render one concern signal entry into prompt lines."""
   139→    file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown file)"
   140→    concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   141→    summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   142→    question = str(entry.get("question", "")).strip()
   143→    evidence_raw = entry.get("evidence", [])
   144→    evidence = (
   145→        [str(item).strip() for item in evidence_raw if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
   146→        if isinstance(evidence_raw, list)
   147→        else []
   148→    )
   149→    lines = [f"  - [{concern_type}] {file}"]
   150→    if summary:
   151→        lines.append(f"    summary: {summary}")
   152→    if question:
   153→        lines.append(f"    question: {question}")
   154→    lines.extend(f"    evidence: {snippet}" for snippet in evidence[:2])
   155→    return lines
   156→
   157→
   158→def _iter_valid_concern_signals(
   159→    signals: list[object],
   160→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   161→    """Filter signal entries to mapping payloads only."""
   162→    return [entry for entry in signals if isinstance(entry, dict)]
   163→
   164→
   165→def render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
   166→    """Render mechanically-generated concern hypotheses for this batch."""
   167→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")
   168→    if not isinstance(signals, list) or not signals:
   169→        return ""
   170→
   171→    lines: list[str] = []
   172→    lines.append("Mechanical concern signals — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:")
   173→    lines.append(
   174→        "Confirm or refute each with your own code reading. Report only confirmed defects."
   175→    )
   176→
   177→    valid_signals = _iter_valid_concern_signals(signals)
   178→    capped_signals = valid_signals[:8]
   179→    for entry in capped_signals:
   180→        lines.extend(_concern_signal_lines(entry))
   181→
   182→    extra = max(0, len(valid_signals) - len(capped_signals))
   183→    if extra:
   184→        lines.append(f"  - (+{extra} more concern signals)")
   185→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   186→
   187→
   188→def render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   189→    """Render workflow integrity checks for architecture/integration dimensions."""
   190→    if not dim_set.intersection(
   191→        {
   192→            "cross_module_architecture",
   193→            "high_level_elegance",
   194→            "mid_level_elegance",
   195→            "design_coherence",
   196→            "initialization_coupling",
   197→        }
   198→    ):
   199→        return ""
   200→    return (
   201→        "9j. Workflow integrity checks: when reviewing orchestration/queue/review flows,\n"
   202→        "    explicitly look for loop-prone patterns and blind spots:\n"
   203→        "    - repeated stale/reopen churn without clear exit criteria or gating,\n"
   204→        "    - packet/batch data being generated but dropped before prompt execution,\n"
   205→        "    - ranking/triage logic that can starve target-improving work,\n"
   206→        "    - reruns happening before existing open review work is drained.\n"
   207→        "    If found, propose concrete guardrails and where to implement them.\n"
   208→    )
   209→
   210→
   211→def render_package_org_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   212→    if "package_organization" not in dim_set:
   213→        return ""
   214→    return (
   215→        "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   216→        "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   217→        "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   218→        "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   219→        "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   220→        "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   221→        "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_issues` for directories "
   222→        "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   223→    )
   224→
   225→
   226→def render_abstraction_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   227→    if "abstraction_fitness" not in dim_set:
   228→        return ""
   229→    return (
   230→        "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   231→        "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   232→        "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   233→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   234→        "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   235→        "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   236→        "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   237→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   238→        "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   239→        "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   240→        "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   241→    )
   242→
   243→
   244→def render_dimension_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   245→    return (
   246→        render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   247→        + render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   248→        + render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   249→        + render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   250→    )
   251→
   252→
   253→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   254→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   255→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   256→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   257→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   258→
   259→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   260→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension.  When *dimension_prompts*
   261→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a ``_dimension_prompt`` key with the
   262→    prompt for its single dimension so that downstream renderers can use it
   263→    without extra parameter threading.
   264→    """
   265→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   266→    result: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   267→    for batch in batches:
   268→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   269→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   270→            result.append(batch)
   271→            continue
   272→        for dim in dims:
   273→            exploded: dict[str, object] = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   274→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   275→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   276→                exploded["_dimension_prompt"] = dim_prompt
   277→            result.append(exploded)
   278→    return result
   279→
   280→
   281→def render_dimension_prompts_block(
   282→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],
   283→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   284→) -> str:
   285→    """Render inline dimension guidance so the reviewer sees the full rubric."""
   286→    if not dimensions or not dimension_prompts:
   287→        return ""
   288→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   289→    for dim in dimensions:
   290→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   291→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   292→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   293→            continue
   294→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   295→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   296→        if description:
   297→            lines.append(description)
   298→
   299→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   300→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   301→            lines.append("Look for:")
   302→            for item in look_for:
   303→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   304→
   305→        skip = prompt.get("skip")
   306→        if isinstance(skip, list) and skip:
   307→            lines.append("Skip:")
   308→            for item in skip:
   309→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   310→        lines.append("")
   311→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   312→
   313→
   314→def render_scoring_frame() -> str:
   315→    return (
   316→        "YOUR TASK: Read the code for this batch's dimension. Judge "
   317→        "how well the codebase serves a developer from that perspective. The dimension "
   318→        "rubric above defines what good looks like. "
   319→        "Cite specific observations that explain your judgment.\n\n"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def render_scan_evidence_note() -> str:
   324→    return (
   325→        "Mechanical scan evidence — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\n"
   326→        "The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` with aggregated signals "
   327→        "from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, error hotspots, signal "
   328→        "density index, boundary violations, and systemic patterns. Use these as starting "
   329→        "points for where to look beyond the seed files.\n\n"
   330→    )
   331→
   332→
   333→def render_seed_files_block(context: PromptBatchContext) -> str:
   334→    return f"Seed files (start here):\n{context.seed_files_text}\n\n"
   335→
   336→
   337→def render_task_requirements(*, issues_cap: int, dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   338→    dim_focus = render_dimension_focus(dim_set)
   339→    # Build numbered items; dimension focus items get renumbered dynamically.
   340→    lines = [
   341→        "Task requirements:",
   342→        "1. Read the blind packet's `system_prompt` — it contains scoring rules and calibration.",
   343→        "2. Start from the seed files, then freely explore the repository to build your understanding.",
   344→        "3. Keep issues and scoring scoped to this batch's dimension.",
   345→        "4. Respect scope controls: do not include files/directories marked by `exclude`, `suppress`, or non-production zone overrides.",
   346→        f"5. Return 0-{issues_cap} issues for this batch (empty array allowed).",
   347→    ]
   348→    next_num = 6
   349→    if dim_focus:
   350→        for focus_line in dim_focus.rstrip("\n").split("\n"):
   351→            lines.append(f"{next_num}. {focus_line.lstrip('0123456789abcdefghij. ')}")
   352→            next_num += 1
   353→    lines.append(
   354→        f"{next_num}. Complete `dimension_judgment` for your dimension — all three fields "
   355→        "(strengths, issue_character, score_rationale) are required. Write the judgment BEFORE setting the score."
   356→    )
   357→    next_num += 1
   358→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Do not edit repository files.")
   359→    next_num += 1
   360→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.")
   361→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   362→
   363→
   364→def render_scope_enums() -> str:
   365→    return (
   366→        "Scope enums:\n"
   367→        '- impact_scope: "local" | "module" | "subsystem" | "codebase"\n'
   368→        '- fix_scope: "single_edit" | "multi_file_refactor" | "architectural_change"\n\n'
   369→    )
   370→
   371→
   372→def join_non_empty_sections(*sections: str) -> str:
   373→    return "".join(section for section in sections if section)
   374→
   375→
   376→__all__ = [
   377→    "PromptBatchContext",
   378→    "coerce_string_list",
   379→    "build_batch_context",
   380→    "explode_to_single_dimension",
   381→    "render_dimension_prompts_block",
   382→    "SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION",
   383→    "render_scan_evidence_focus",
   384→    "render_historical_focus",
   385→    "render_mechanical_concern_signals",
   386→    "render_workflow_integrity_focus",
   387→    "render_package_org_focus",
   388→    "render_abstraction_focus",
   389→    "render_dimension_focus",
   390→    "render_scoring_frame",
   391→    "render_scan_evidence_note",
   392→    "render_seed_files_block",
   393→    "render_task_requirements",
   394→    "render_scope_enums",
   395→    "join_non_empty_sections",
   396→]
   397→
```

```
    59→        "    }\n"

    77→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    78→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name_when_supported_by_history"\n'

    89→def _extract_dimension_prompts(batch: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    90→    """Extract dimension prompts embedded by explode_to_single_dimension."""
    91→    prompt = batch.get("_dimension_prompt")
    92→    if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
    93→        return {}
    94→    dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
    95→    if isinstance(dims, list) and len(dims) == 1:
    96→        return {str(dims[0]): prompt}
    97→    return {}
    98→
    99→
   100→def render_batch_prompt(
   101→    *,
   102→    repo_root: Path,
   103→    packet_path: Path,
   104→    batch_index: int,
   105→    batch: dict[str, object],
   106→    policy_block: str = "",
   107→) -> str:
   108→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   109→    context = build_batch_context(batch, batch_index)
   110→    dim_prompts = _extract_dimension_prompts(batch)
   111→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   112→        _render_metadata_block(
   113→            repo_root=repo_root,
   114→            packet_path=packet_path,
   115→            batch_index=batch_index,
   116→            context=context,
   117→        ),
   118→        render_dimension_prompts_block(context.dimensions, dim_prompts),
   119→        policy_block,
   120→        render_scoring_frame(),
   121→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   122→        render_seed_files_block(context),
   123→        render_historical_focus(batch),
   124→        render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   125→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=context.issues_cap, dim_set=context.dimension_set),
   126→        render_scope_enums(),
   127→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   128→    )
   129→
   130→
   131→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]
```

```
    99→def _batch_live_log_interval_seconds(heartbeat_seconds: float) -> float:
   100→    """Clamp the live log polling interval derived from the heartbeat."""
   101→    if heartbeat_seconds <= 0:
   102→        return 5.0
   103→    return max(1.0, min(heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))
   104→
   105→
   106→def _prompt_fn_with_policy(*, policy_block: str, **kwargs):
   107→    """Render one batch prompt with the active queue policy block."""
   108→    return render_batch_prompt(**kwargs, policy_block=policy_block)
   109→
   110→
   111→def _selected_batch_indexes_for_args(args, *, batch_count: int) -> list[int]:
   112→    """Resolve the subset of investigation batches selected by CLI args."""
   113→    return selected_batch_indexes(
   114→        raw_selection=getattr(args, "only_batches", None),
   115→        batch_count=batch_count,
   116→        parse_fn=parse_batch_selection,
   117→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def _build_codex_batch_runner_deps(policy) -> CodexBatchRunnerDeps:
   122→    """Build shared subprocess deps for one local codex batch run."""
   123→    return CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
   124→        timeout_seconds=policy.batch_timeout_seconds,
   125→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   126→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   127→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   128→        use_popen_runner=(getattr(subprocess.run, "__module__", "") == "subprocess"),
   129→        subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
   130→        live_log_interval_seconds=_batch_live_log_interval_seconds(

   168→
   169→    policy_result = load_policy_result()
   170→    policy_block = render_policy_block(policy_result.policy)
   171→    if not policy_result.ok:
   172→        print(
   173→            colorize(
   174→                f"  Warning: ignoring malformed project policy ({policy_result.message or 'unknown error'}).",
   175→                "yellow",
   176→            )
   177→        )
   178→    codex_batch_deps = _build_codex_batch_runner_deps(policy)
   179→    followup_scan_deps = _build_followup_scan_deps(project_root=project_root)
   180→    return review_batches_mod.BatchRunDeps(
   181→        run_stamp_fn=run_stamp,
   182→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=_load_or_prepare_packet,
   183→        selected_batch_indexes_fn=_selected_batch_indexes_for_args,
   184→        prepare_run_artifacts_fn=partial(
   185→            prepare_run_artifacts,
   186→            build_prompt_fn=partial(_prompt_fn_with_policy, policy_block=policy_block),
   187→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   188→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   189→        ),
   190→        run_codex_batch_fn=partial(

   194→        execute_batches_fn=lambda **kwargs: execute_batches(
```

```
    64→def _utc_now() -> datetime:

    72→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    73→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    74→        return None
    75→    try:
    76→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    77→    except ValueError:
    78→        return None
    79→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    80→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    81→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)

    95→
    96→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    97→    if not session_id.strip():
    98→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-id is required.", exit_code=2)
    99→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
   100→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
   101→        raise CommandError("Error: invalid --session-id value.", exit_code=2)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   105→    if not path.exists():
   106→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
   107→    try:
   108→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   109→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   110→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
   111→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   112→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
   113→    return payload
   114→
   115→
   116→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   117→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   118→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   119→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   120→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   121→    if payload_id != session_id:
   122→        raise CommandError(
   123→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   124→        )
   125→    return path, payload

   138→    try:

   140→            state=state,

   149→        if not msg:

   151→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1) from exc
   152→    write_query(packet)

   154→    stamp = run_stamp()
   155→    blind_packet_path = _blind_packet_path()
   156→    packet_path, blind_path = write_review_packet_snapshot(

   160→        blind_path_override=blind_packet_path,
   161→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,

   163→    return packet, packet_path, blind_path
```

```
1→"""Complexity-focused mechanical evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num
     8→
     9→
    10→def _build_complexity_hotspots(
    11→    by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]],
    12→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]],
    13→) -> list[dict]:
    14→    """Top 20 files by composite complexity score."""
    15→    del by_file
    16→    file_data: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    17→
    18→    for issue in by_detector.get("structural", []):
    19→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    20→        if not filepath:
    21→            continue
    22→        signals = _get_signals(issue)
    23→        entry = file_data.setdefault(
    24→            filepath,
    25→            {
    26→                "file": filepath,
    27→                "loc": 0,
    28→                "complexity_score": 0,
    29→                "signals": [],
    30→                "component_count": 0,
    31→                "function_count": 0,
    32→                "monster_functions": 0,
    33→                "cyclomatic_hotspots": 0,
    34→            },
    35→        )
    36→        entry["loc"] = max(entry["loc"], _safe_num(signals.get("loc")))
    37→        entry["function_count"] = max(
    38→            entry["function_count"], _safe_num(signals.get("function_count"))
    39→        )
    40→        entry["component_count"] = max(
    41→            entry["component_count"], _safe_num(signals.get("component_count"))
    42→        )
    43→
    44→        max_params = _safe_num(signals.get("max_params"))
    45→        if max_params >= 5:
    46→            entry["signals"].append(f"{int(max_params)} params")
    47→        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get("max_nesting"))
    48→        if max_nesting >= 4:
    49→            entry["signals"].append(f"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}")
    50→        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get("complexity_score"))
    51→        entry["complexity_score"] = max(entry["complexity_score"], complexity)
    52→
    53→    for issue in by_detector.get("smells", []):
    54→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    55→        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, "smell_id", "")
    56→        if filepath in file_data:
    57→            if smell_id == "monster_function":
    58→                file_data[filepath]["monster_functions"] += 1
    59→            elif smell_id in ("cyclomatic_complexity", "high_cyclomatic"):
    60→                file_data[filepath]["cyclomatic_hotspots"] += 1
    61→
    62→    for issue in by_detector.get("responsibility_cohesion", []):
    63→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    64→        if filepath in file_data:
    65→            clusters = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, "cluster_count"))
    66→            file_data[filepath]["component_count"] = max(
    67→                file_data[filepath]["component_count"], clusters
    68→            )
    69→
    70→    for entry in file_data.values():
    71→        entry["_score"] = (
    72→            entry["loc"] / 100
    73→            + entry["complexity_score"]
    74→            + entry["component_count"] * 3
    75→            + entry["monster_functions"] * 10
    76→        )
    77→        entry["signals"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry["signals"]))
    78→
    79→    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e["_score"])[:20]
    80→    for entry in ranked:
    81→        del entry["_score"]
    82→    return ranked
    83→
    84→
    85→__all__ = ["_build_complexity_hotspots"]
    86→
```

```
1→"""Organization and layout evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num
     6→
     7→
     8→def _build_flat_dir_issues(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
     9→    """From flat_dirs detector."""
    10→    results: list[dict] = []
    11→    for issue in by_detector.get("flat_dirs", []):
    12→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    13→        kind = _get_detail(issue, "kind") or _get_detail(issue, "reason", "")
    14→        raw_score = _get_detail(issue, "score")
    15→        if raw_score is None:
    16→            raw_score = _get_detail(issue, "combined_score", 0)
    17→        results.append(
    18→            {
    19→                "directory": filepath,
    20→                "kind": kind,
    21→                "file_count": int(_safe_num(_get_detail(issue, "file_count", 0))),
    22→                "combined_score": int(_safe_num(raw_score)),
    23→            }
    24→        )
    25→    results.sort(key=lambda e: -e["combined_score"])
    26→    return results[:20]
    27→
    28→
    29→def _build_large_file_distribution(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> dict | None:
    30→    """Distribution stats from structural issues."""
    31→    locs: list[float] = []
    32→    for issue in by_detector.get("structural", []):
    33→        signals = _get_signals(issue)
    34→        loc = _safe_num(signals.get("loc"))
    35→        if loc > 0:
    36→            locs.append(loc)
    37→    if not locs:
    38→        return None
    39→    locs.sort()
    40→    n = len(locs)
    41→    return {
    42→        "count": n,
    43→        "median_loc": int(locs[n // 2]),
    44→        "p90_loc": int(locs[int(n * 0.9)]) if n >= 10 else int(locs[-1]),
    45→        "p99_loc": int(locs[int(n * 0.99)]) if n >= 100 else int(locs[-1]),
    46→    }
    47→
    48→
    49→__all__ = ["_build_flat_dir_issues", "_build_large_file_distribution"]
    50→
```

```
    27→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (

    29→)
    30→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (
    31→    open_scope_breakdown as _open_scope_breakdown,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (
    34→    path_scoped_issues as _path_scoped_issues,
    35→)
    36→
    37→
    38→def path_scoped_issues(
    39→    issues: dict[str, Issue],
    40→    scan_path: str | None,
    41→) -> dict[str, Issue]:
    42→    """Filter issues to those within the given scan path."""
    43→    return _path_scoped_issues(issues, scan_path)
    44→
    45→
    46→def issue_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    47→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    48→    return _issue_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path)
    49→
    50→
    51→def open_scope_breakdown(
    52→    issues: dict[str, Issue],
    53→    scan_path: str | None,
    54→    *,
    55→    detector: str | None = None,
    56→) -> dict[str, int]:
    57→    """Return open-issue counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    58→    return _open_scope_breakdown(
    59→        issues,
    60→        scan_path,
    61→        detector=detector,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def is_ignored(issue_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    66→    """Check if a issue matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    67→    return matched_ignore_pattern(issue_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    68→
    69→
    70→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    71→    issue_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    72→) -> str | None:
    73→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    74→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    75→        if "*" in pattern:
    76→            target = issue_id if "::" in pattern else file
    77→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    78→                return pattern
    79→            continue
    80→
    81→        if "::" in pattern:
    82→            if issue_id.startswith(pattern):
    83→                return pattern
    84→            continue
    85→
    86→        raw_base = pattern.rstrip("/")
    87→        rel_base = rel(pattern).rstrip("/")
    88→        for base in (raw_base, rel_base):
    89→            if not base:
    90→                continue
    91→            if file == base or file.startswith(base + "/"):
    92→                return pattern
    93→
    94→    return None
    95→
    96→
    97→def remove_ignored_issues(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
    98→    """Suppress issues matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
    99→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   100→    matched_ids = [
   101→        issue_id
   102→        for issue_id, issue in state["issues"].items()
   103→        if is_ignored(issue_id, issue["file"], [pattern])
   104→    ]
   105→    now = utc_now()
   106→    for issue_id in matched_ids:
   107→        issue = state["issues"][issue_id]
   108→        issue["suppressed"] = True
   109→        issue["suppressed_at"] = now
   110→        issue["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   111→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_integration import (
   112→        recompute_stats as _recompute_stats,
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   116→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   117→    return len(matched_ids)
   118→
   119→
   120→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   121→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching issues."""
   122→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   123→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   124→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   125→    if pattern not in ignores:
   126→        ignores.append(pattern)
   127→    return remove_ignored_issues(state, pattern)
   128→
   129→
   130→def make_issue(
   131→    detector: str,
   132→    file: str,
   133→    name: str,
   134→    *,
   135→    tier: int,
   136→    confidence: str,
   137→    summary: str,
   138→    detail: dict | None = None,
   139→) -> Issue:
   140→    """Create a normalized issue dict with a stable ID."""
   141→    rfile = rel(file)
   142→    issue_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    return {
   145→        "id": issue_id,
   146→        "detector": detector,
   147→        "file": rfile,
   148→        "tier": tier,
   149→        "confidence": confidence,
   150→        "summary": summary,
   151→        "detail": detail or {},
   152→        "status": "open",
   153→        "note": None,
   154→        "first_seen": now,
   155→        "last_seen": now,
   156→        "resolved_at": None,
   157→        "reopen_count": 0,
   158→    }
   159→
   160→
   161→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   162→
   163→
   164→def _matches_issue_path(issue: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   165→    """Match against the issue's detector name or file path."""
   166→    return (
   167→        issue.get("detector") == pattern
   168→        or issue["file"] == pattern
   169→        or issue["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   170→    )
   171→
   172→
   173→def _matches_issue_name_segment(issue_id: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
   174→    """Match against the name segment of the issue ID.
   175→
   176→    For hashed IDs (detector::path::name::hex8), also match the descriptive
   177→    name (second-to-last segment).  Returns False for IDs without :: or
   178→    patterns containing ::.
   179→    """
   180→    if "::" in pattern or "::" not in issue_id:
   181→        return False
   182→    segments = issue_id.split("::")
   183→    name_segment = segments[-1]
   184→    if name_segment == pattern:
   185→        return True
   186→    if len(segments) < 3 or not _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment):
   187→        return False
   188→    return segments[-2] == pattern
   189→
   190→
   191→def _matches_pattern(issue_id: str, issue: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   192→    """Check if a issue matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   193→    if issue_id == pattern:
   194→        return True
   195→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(issue_id, pattern):
   196→        return True
   197→    if "::" in pattern and issue_id.startswith(pattern):
   198→        return True
   199→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and issue_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   200→        return True
   201→    if _matches_issue_path(issue, pattern):
   202→        return True
   203→    if _matches_issue_name_segment(issue_id, pattern):
   204→        return True
   205→
   206→    return False
   207→
```
