1→"""Suppress command handler — permanently silence issues matching a pattern."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.attestation import (
     9→    show_attestation_requirement,
    10→    validate_attestation,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.persist import (
    14→    save_config_or_exit,
    15→    save_state_or_exit,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import show_score_with_plan_context
    19→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    20→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
    21→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    22→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    23→from desloppify.base.tooling import check_config_staleness
    24→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    25→import desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core as narrative_mod
    26→
    27→
    28→def cmd_suppress(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    29→    """Suppress issues matching a pattern."""
    30→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    31→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    32→        show_attestation_requirement("Suppress", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
    33→        raise CommandError("Suppress requires a valid attestation.")
    34→
    35→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    36→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    37→    state = runtime.state
    38→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    39→
    40→    config = runtime.config
    41→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
    42→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
    43→    save_config_or_exit(config)
    44→
    45→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_issues(state, args.pattern)
    46→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
    47→        {
    48→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
    49→            "command": "suppress",
    50→            "pattern": args.pattern,
    51→            "attestation": attestation,
    52→            "affected": removed,
    53→        }
    54→    )
    55→    save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    56→
    57→    print(colorize(f"Added suppress pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
    58→    if removed:
    59→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching issues from state.")
    60→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    61→    if config_warning:
    62→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    63→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
    64→
    65→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    66→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    67→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    68→        state,
    69→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="suppress"),
    70→    )
    71→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    72→    write_query(
    73→        {
    74→            "command": "suppress",
    75→            "pattern": args.pattern,
    76→            "removed": removed,
    77→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    78→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    79→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    80→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    81→            "attestation": attestation,
    82→            "narrative": narrative,
    83→        }
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→__all__ = ["cmd_suppress"]
    88→

1→"""Stale-wontfix scan augmentation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import resolve_path
    10→
    11→_STRUCTURAL_COMPLEXITY_GROWTH_THRESHOLD = 10
    12→_STRUCTURAL_LOC_GROWTH_THRESHOLD = 50
    13→
    14→
    15→def _in_scan_scope(filepath: str, scan_path: Path, *, project_root: Path) -> bool:
    16→    if scan_path.resolve() == project_root.resolve():
    17→        return True
    18→    full = Path(resolve_path(filepath))
    19→    root = scan_path.resolve()
    20→    return full == root or root in full.parents
    21→
    22→
    23→def _to_float(value: object) -> float | None:
    24→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    25→        return None
    26→    if isinstance(value, int | float):
    27→        return float(value)
    28→    if isinstance(value, str):
    29→        try:
    30→            return float(value)
    31→        except ValueError:
    32→            return None
    33→    return None
    34→
    35→
    36→def _structural_growth_details(
    37→    snapshot: dict[str, Any],
    38→    current: dict[str, Any],
    39→) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
    40→    """Return structural drift details for wontfix issues."""
    41→    snapshot_detail = (
    42→        snapshot.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(snapshot.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    43→    )
    44→    current_detail = (
    45→        current.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(current.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    46→    )
    47→    drift: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    48→
    49→    old_complexity = _to_float(snapshot_detail.get("complexity_score"))
    50→    new_complexity = _to_float(current_detail.get("complexity_score"))
    51→    if (
    52→        old_complexity is not None
    53→        and new_complexity is not None
    54→        and new_complexity >= old_complexity + _STRUCTURAL_COMPLEXITY_GROWTH_THRESHOLD
    55→    ):
    56→        drift["complexity_score"] = {"from": old_complexity, "to": new_complexity}
    57→
    58→    old_loc = _to_float(snapshot_detail.get("loc"))
    59→    new_loc = _to_float(current_detail.get("loc"))
    60→    if (
    61→        old_loc is not None
    62→        and new_loc is not None
    63→        and new_loc >= old_loc + _STRUCTURAL_LOC_GROWTH_THRESHOLD
    64→    ):
    65→        drift["loc"] = {"from": old_loc, "to": new_loc}
    66→
    67→    return drift
    68→
    69→
    70→def _wontfix_staleness_reasons(
    71→    previous: dict[str, Any],
    72→    current: dict[str, Any],
    73→    *,
    74→    since_scan: int,
    75→    decay_scans: int,
    76→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, float]]]:
    77→    """Determine why a wontfix issue is stale and compute structural drift."""
    78→    reasons: list[str] = []
    79→    if decay_scans > 0 and since_scan >= decay_scans:
    80→        reasons.append("scan_decay")
    81→
    82→    drift: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    83→    if previous.get("detector") == "structural":
    84→        snapshot = previous.get("wontfix_snapshot")
    85→        if isinstance(snapshot, dict):
    86→            drift = _structural_growth_details(snapshot, current)
    87→            if drift:
    88→                reasons.append("severity_drift")
    89→
    90→    return reasons, drift
    91→
    92→
    93→def _format_drift_summary(drift: dict[str, dict[str, float]]) -> str:
    94→    """Format structural drift metrics into a human-readable suffix."""
    95→    parts = []
    96→    if "complexity_score" in drift:
    97→        comp = drift["complexity_score"]
    98→        parts.append(f"complexity {comp['from']:.0f}->{comp['to']:.0f}")
    99→    if "loc" in drift:
   100→        loc = drift["loc"]
   101→        parts.append(f"loc {loc['from']:.0f}->{loc['to']:.0f}")
   102→    return f"; drift: {', '.join(parts)}" if parts else ""
   103→
   104→
   105→def _build_stale_wontfix_issue(
   106→    issue_id: str,
   107→    previous: dict[str, Any],
   108→    *,
   109→    reasons: list[str],
   110→    drift: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
   111→    since_scan: int,
   112→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   113→    """Construct a stale_wontfix issue from staleness metadata."""
   114→    tier = 4 if "severity_drift" in reasons else 3
   115→    confidence = "high" if "severity_drift" in reasons else "medium"
   116→    reason_text = " + ".join(reasons)
   117→    summary = (
   118→        f"Stale wontfix ({reason_text}): re-triage `{issue_id}` "
   119→        f"(last reviewed {since_scan} scans ago)"
   120→    )
   121→    summary += _format_drift_summary(drift)
   122→
   123→    return state_mod.make_issue(
   124→        "stale_wontfix",
   125→        previous.get("file", ""),
   126→        issue_id,
   127→        tier=tier,
   128→        confidence=confidence,
   129→        summary=summary,
   130→        detail={
   131→            "subtype": "stale_wontfix",
   132→            "original_issue_id": issue_id,
   133→            "original_detector": previous.get("detector"),
   134→            "reasons": reasons,
   135→            "scans_since_wontfix": since_scan,
   136→            "drift": drift,
   137→        },
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→def augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   142→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   143→    *,
   144→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   145→    scan_path: Path,
   146→    project_root: Path,
   147→    decay_scans: int,
   148→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   149→    """Append re-triage issues for stale or worsening wontfix debt."""
   150→    existing = state.get("issues", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(existing, dict):
   152→        return issues, 0
   153→
   154→    current_by_id = {
   155→        issue.get("id"): issue
   156→        for issue in issues
   157→        if issue.get("id")
   158→    }
   159→    augmented = list(issues)
   160→    monitored = 0
   161→
   162→    for issue_id, previous in existing.items():
   163→        if not isinstance(previous, dict):
   164→            continue
   165→        if previous.get("status") != "wontfix":
   166→            continue
   167→        if issue_id not in current_by_id:
   168→            continue
   169→        if not _in_scan_scope(
   170→            str(previous.get("file", "")),
   171→            scan_path,
   172→            project_root=project_root,
   173→        ):
   174→            continue
   175→
   176→        monitored += 1
   177→        scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   178→        since_scan = scan_count - int(previous.get("wontfix_scan_count", scan_count) or 0)
   179→        since_scan = max(since_scan, 0)
   180→
   181→        reasons, drift = _wontfix_staleness_reasons(
   182→            previous,
   183→            current_by_id[issue_id],
   184→            since_scan=since_scan,
   185→            decay_scans=decay_scans,
   186→        )
   187→        if not reasons:
   188→            continue
   189→
   190→        augmented.append(
   191→            _build_stale_wontfix_issue(
   192→                issue_id,
   193→                previous,
   194→                reasons=reasons,
   195→                drift=drift,
   196→                since_scan=since_scan,
   197→            )
   198→        )
   199→
   200→    return augmented, monitored
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = ["augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues"]
   204→
```

1→"""Issue and score-related TypedDict models for persisted state payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, NotRequired, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
     8→
     9→
    10→class Issue(TypedDict):
    11→    """The central data structure: a normalized issue from any detector."""
    12→
    13→    id: str
    14→    detector: str
    15→    file: str
    16→    tier: int
    17→    confidence: str
    18→    summary: str
    19→    # Known detail shapes per detector (non-exhaustive, for reference):
    20→    #
    21→    # structural:      {loc, complexity_score?, complexity_signals?: list[str],
    22→    #                   name? (god class), ...god_class_metrics}
    23→    # smells:          {smell_id, severity, count, lines: list[int]}
    24→    # dupes:           {fn_a: dict, fn_b: dict, similarity, kind, cluster_size,
    25→    #                   cluster: list}
    26→    # coupling:        {target, tool?, direction, sole_tool?, importer_count?,
    27→    #                   loc?, source_tool?, target_tool?}
    28→    # single_use:      {loc, sole_importer}
    29→    # orphaned:        {loc}
    30→    # facade:          {loc, importers, imports_from: list[str], kind}
    31→    # review:          {holistic?: bool, dimension?, related_files?: list[str],
    32→    #                   suggestion?, evidence?: list[str], investigation?,
    33→    #                   merged_at?}
    34→    # review_coverage: {reason, loc?, age_days?, old_files?, new_files?}
    35→    # security:        {kind, severity, line, content, remediation}
    36→    # test_coverage:   {kind, loc?, importer_count?, loc_weight?,
    37→    #                   test_file?, test_functions?, assertions?, mocks?,
    38→    #                   snapshots?}
    39→    # props:           {passthrough entry fields minus "file"}
    40→    # subjective_assessment (synthetic): {dimension_name, dimension, failing,
    41→    #                   strict_score, open_review_issues?}
    42→    # workflow (synthetic): {stage?, strict?, plan_start_strict?, delta?,
    43→    #                   total_review_issues?, explanation?}
    44→    detail: dict[str, Any]
    45→    status: Status
    46→    note: str | None
    47→    first_seen: str
    48→    last_seen: str
    49→    resolved_at: str | None
    50→    reopen_count: int
    51→    suppressed: NotRequired[bool]
    52→    suppressed_at: NotRequired[str | None]
    53→    suppression_pattern: NotRequired[str | None]
    54→    resolution_attestation: NotRequired[dict[str, str | bool | None]]
    55→    lang: NotRequired[str]
    56→    zone: NotRequired[str]
    57→
    58→
    59→class TierStats(TypedDict, total=False):
    60→    open: int
    61→    fixed: int
    62→    auto_resolved: int
    63→    wontfix: int
    64→    false_positive: int
    65→
    66→
    67→class StateStats(TypedDict, total=False):
    68→    total: int
    69→    open: int
    70→    fixed: int
    71→    auto_resolved: int
    72→    wontfix: int
    73→    false_positive: int
    74→    by_tier: dict[str, TierStats]
    75→
    76→
    77→class DimensionScore(TypedDict, total=False):
    78→    score: float
    79→    strict: float
    80→    verified_strict_score: float
    81→    checks: int
    82→    failing: int
    83→    tier: int
    84→    carried_forward: bool
    85→    detectors: dict[str, Any]

1→"""Dismissal helpers for cluster triage plan mutations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .prompt import TriageResult
     6→
     7→
     8→def _triaged_out_payload(
     9→    *,
    10→    issue_id: str,
    11→    reason: str,
    12→    note: str | None,
    13→    now: str,
    14→    scan_count: int,
    15→) -> dict:
    16→    return {
    17→        "issue_id": issue_id,
    18→        "kind": "triaged_out",
    19→        "reason": reason,
    20→        "note": note,
    21→        "attestation": None,
    22→        "created_at": now,
    23→        "review_after": None,
    24→        "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def dismiss_triage_issues(
    29→    *,
    30→    triage: TriageResult,
    31→    order: list[str],
    32→    skipped: dict,
    33→    now: str,
    34→    version: int,
    35→    scan_count: int,
    36→) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
    37→    """Move triage-dismissed issues out of queue and into skipped metadata."""
    38→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
    39→    dismiss_count = 0
    40→    for dismissed in triage.dismissed_issues:
    41→        issue_id = dismissed.issue_id
    42→        dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    43→        if issue_id in order:
    44→            order.remove(issue_id)
    45→        skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    46→            issue_id=issue_id,
    47→            reason=dismissed.reason,
    48→            note=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    49→            now=now,
    50→            scan_count=scan_count,
    51→        )
    52→        dismiss_count += 1
    53→
    54→    for epic_data in triage.clusters:
    55→        for issue_id in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
    56→            if issue_id in dismissed_ids or issue_id not in order:
    57→                continue
    58→            order.remove(issue_id)
    59→            dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    60→            skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    61→                issue_id=issue_id,
    62→                reason=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    63→                note=None,
    64→                now=now,
    65→                scan_count=scan_count,
    66→            )
    67→            dismiss_count += 1
    68→
    69→    return dismissed_ids, dismiss_count
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = ["dismiss_triage_issues"]
    73→
```

1→"""Issue validation and stale-resolution helpers for holistic review imports."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Issue, StateModel
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions import normalize_dimension_name
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts_types import (
    12→    ReviewIssuePayload,
    13→    ReviewScopePayload,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts_validation import (
    16→    validate_review_issue_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.payload import (
    19→    normalize_review_confidence,
    20→    review_tier,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.resolution import (
    23→    auto_resolve_review_issues,
    24→)
    25→
    26→_POSITIVE_PREFIXES = (
    27→    "good ",
    28→    "well ",
    29→    "strong ",
    30→    "clean ",
    31→    "excellent ",
    32→    "nice ",
    33→    "solid ",
    34→)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _build_holistic_detail(
    38→    *,
    39→    content_hash: str,
    40→    dimension: str,
    41→    related_files: list[str],
    42→    evidence: list[str],
    43→    suggestion: str,
    44→    reasoning: str,
    45→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    46→    detail: dict[str, Any] = {
    47→        "holistic": True,
    48→        "dimension": dimension,
    49→        "related_files": related_files,
    50→        "evidence": evidence,
    51→        "suggestion": suggestion,
    52→        "reasoning": reasoning,
    53→    }
    54→    detail["summary_hash"] = content_hash
    55→    return detail
    56→
    57→
    58→def validate_and_build_issues(
    59→    issues_list: list[ReviewIssuePayload],
    60→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    61→    lang_name: str,
    62→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    63→    """Validate raw holistic issues and build state-ready issue dicts.
    64→
    65→    Returns (review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns).
    66→    """
    67→    review_issues: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    68→    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    69→    dismissed_concerns: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    70→    allowed_dimensions = {
    71→        dim for dim in holistic_prompts if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    for idx, raw_issue in enumerate(issues_list):
    75→        issue, issue_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
    76→            raw_issue,
    77→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
    78→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions,
    79→            allow_dismissed=True,
    80→        )
    81→        if issue_errors:
    82→            skipped.append(
    83→                {
    84→                    "index": idx,
    85→                    "missing": issue_errors,
    86→                    "identifier": (
    87→                        raw_issue.get("identifier", "<none>")
    88→                        if isinstance(raw_issue, dict)
    89→                        else "<none>"
    90→                    ),
    91→                }
    92→            )
    93→            continue
    94→        if issue is None:
    95→            raise ValueError(
    96→                "review issue payload missing after validation succeeded"
    97→            )
    98→
    99→        if issue.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed":
   100→            fingerprint = issue.get("concern_fingerprint", "")
   101→            if fingerprint:
   102→                dismissed_concerns.append(
   103→                    {
   104→                        "fingerprint": fingerprint,
   105→                        "concern_type": issue.get("concern_type", ""),
   106→                        "concern_file": issue.get("concern_file", ""),
   107→                        "reasoning": issue.get("reasoning", ""),
   108→                    }
   109→                )
   110→            continue
   111→
   112→        summary_text = str(issue.get("summary", ""))
   113→        if summary_text.lower().startswith(_POSITIVE_PREFIXES):
   114→            skipped.append(
   115→                {
   116→                    "index": idx,
   117→                    "missing": ["positive observation (not a defect)"],
   118→                    "identifier": issue.get("identifier", "<none>"),
   119→                }
   120→            )
   121→            continue
   122→
   123→        dimension = issue["dimension"]
   124→
   125→        is_confirmed_concern = issue.get("concern_verdict") == "confirmed"
   126→        detector = "concerns" if is_confirmed_concern else "review"
   127→
   128→        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(summary_text.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
   129→        detail = _build_holistic_detail(
   130→            content_hash=content_hash,
   131→            dimension=dimension,
   132→            related_files=issue["related_files"],
   133→            evidence=issue["evidence"],
   134→            suggestion=issue.get("suggestion", ""),
   135→            reasoning=issue.get("reasoning", ""),
   136→        )
   137→        if is_confirmed_concern:
   138→            detail["concern_type"] = issue.get("concern_type", "")
   139→            detail["concern_verdict"] = "confirmed"
   140→
   141→        prefix = "concern" if is_confirmed_concern else "holistic"
   142→        issue_file = issue.get("concern_file", "") if is_confirmed_concern else ""
   143→        confidence = normalize_review_confidence(issue.get("confidence", "low"))
   144→        imported = make_issue(
   145→            detector=detector,
   146→            file=issue_file,
   147→            name=f"{prefix}::{dimension}::{issue['identifier']}",
   148→            tier=review_tier(confidence, holistic=True),
   149→            confidence=confidence,
   150→            summary=summary_text,
   151→            detail=detail,
   152→        )
   153→        imported["lang"] = lang_name
   154→        review_issues.append(imported)
   155→
   156→    return review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns
   157→
   158→
   159→def collect_imported_dimensions(
   160→    *,
   161→    issues_list: list[ReviewIssuePayload],
   162→    review_issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   163→    assessments: dict[str, Any] | None,
   164→    review_scope: ReviewScopePayload | dict[str, Any] | None,
   165→    valid_dimensions: set[str],
   166→) -> set[str]:
   167→    """Return normalized dimensions this import explicitly covered."""
   168→    imported_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   169→
   170→    if isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   171→        scope_dims = review_scope.get("imported_dimensions")
   172→        if isinstance(scope_dims, list):
   173→            for raw_dim in scope_dims:
   174→                normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   175→                if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   176→                    imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   177→
   178→    for issue in issues_list:
   179→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   180→            continue
   181→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(issue.get("dimension", "")))
   182→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   183→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   184→
   185→    for issue in review_issues:
   186→        detail = issue.get("detail")
   187→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   188→            continue
   189→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   190→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   191→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   192→
   193→    for raw_dim in (assessments or {}):
   194→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   195→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   196→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   197→
   198→    return imported_dimensions
   199→
   200→
   201→def auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   202→    state: StateModel,
   203→    new_ids: set[str],
   204→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   205→    utc_now_fn,
   206→    *,
   207→    imported_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   208→    full_sweep_included: bool | None = None,
   209→) -> None:
   210→    """Auto-resolve open holistic issues not present in the latest import."""
   211→    scope_dimensions = {
   212→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   213→        for dim in (imported_dimensions or set())
   214→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   215→    }
   216→    scoped_reimport = full_sweep_included is False
   217→    if scoped_reimport and not scope_dimensions:
   218→        return
   219→
   220→    def _should_resolve(issue: Issue) -> bool:
   221→        if issue.get("detector") not in ("review", "concerns"):
   222→            return False
   223→        detail = issue.get("detail")
   224→        if not isinstance(detail, dict) or not detail.get("holistic"):
   225→            return False
   226→        if not scoped_reimport:
   227→            return True
   228→        dimension = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   229→        return dimension in scope_dimensions
   230→
   231→    auto_resolve_review_issues(
   232→        state,
   233→        new_ids=new_ids,
   234→        diff=diff,
   235→        note="not reported in latest holistic re-import",
   236→        should_resolve=_should_resolve,
   237→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "auto_resolve_stale_holistic",
   243→    "collect_imported_dimensions",
   244→    "validate_and_build_issues",
   245→]
   246→
```

     2→

     4→
     5→from typing import Any, NotRequired, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.enums import Status

    10→class Issue(TypedDict):
    11→    """The central data structure: a normalized issue from any detector."""
    12→
    13→    id: str
    14→    detector: str
    15→    file: str
    16→    tier: int
    17→    confidence: str
    18→    summary: str
    19→    # Known detail shapes per detector (non-exhaustive, for reference):
    20→    #
    21→    # structural:      {loc, complexity_score?, complexity_signals?: list[str],
    22→    #                   name? (god class), ...god_class_metrics}
    23→    # smells:          {smell_id, severity, count, lines: list[int]}
    24→    # dupes:           {fn_a: dict, fn_b: dict, similarity, kind, cluster_size,
    25→    #                   cluster: list}
    26→    # coupling:        {target, tool?, direction, sole_tool?, importer_count?,
    27→    #                   loc?, source_tool?, target_tool?}
    28→    # single_use:      {loc, sole_importer}
    29→    # orphaned:        {loc}
    30→    # facade:          {loc, importers, imports_from: list[str], kind}
    31→    # review:          {holistic?: bool, dimension?, related_files?: list[str],
    32→    #                   suggestion?, evidence?: list[str], investigation?,
    33→    #                   merged_at?}
    34→    # review_coverage: {reason, loc?, age_days?, old_files?, new_files?}
    35→    # security:        {kind, severity, line, content, remediation}
    36→    # test_coverage:   {kind, loc?, importer_count?, loc_weight?,
    37→    #                   test_file?, test_functions?, assertions?, mocks?,
    38→    #                   snapshots?}
    39→    # props:           {passthrough entry fields minus "file"}
    40→    # subjective_assessment (synthetic): {dimension_name, dimension, failing,
    41→    #                   strict_score, open_review_issues?}
    42→    # workflow (synthetic): {stage?, strict?, plan_start_strict?, delta?,
    43→    #                   total_review_issues?, explanation?}
    44→    detail: dict[str, Any]
    45→    status: Status
    46→    note: str | None
    47→    first_seen: str
    48→    last_seen: str
    49→    resolved_at: str | None
    50→    reopen_count: int
    51→    suppressed: NotRequired[bool]
    52→    suppressed_at: NotRequired[str | None]
    53→    suppression_pattern: NotRequired[str | None]
    54→    resolution_attestation: NotRequired[dict[str, str | bool | None]]
    55→    lang: NotRequired[str]
    56→    zone: NotRequired[str]

    73→    false_positive: int

    77→class DimensionScore(TypedDict, total=False):
    78→    score: float
    79→    strict: float
    80→    verified_strict_score: float
    81→    checks: int
    82→    failing: int

    85→    detectors: dict[str, Any]
    86→    coverage_status: str

1→"""Canonical enums for issue attributes.
     2→
     3→StrEnum values compare equal to their string values (Confidence.HIGH == "high"),
     4→so existing code using raw strings continues to work during gradual migration.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import enum
    10→
    11→
    12→class Confidence(enum.StrEnum):
    13→    HIGH = "high"
    14→    MEDIUM = "medium"
    15→    LOW = "low"
    16→
    17→
    18→class Status(enum.StrEnum):
    19→    OPEN = "open"
    20→    FIXED = "fixed"
    21→    WONTFIX = "wontfix"
    22→    FALSE_POSITIVE = "false_positive"
    23→    AUTO_RESOLVED = "auto_resolved"
    24→    RESOLVED = "resolved"  # Legacy on-disk value; migrated to FIXED on load.
    25→
    26→
    27→_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES = frozenset(
    28→    {
    29→        Status.OPEN.value,
    30→        Status.FIXED.value,
    31→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    32→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    33→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    34→    }
    35→)
    36→_RESOLVED_STATUSES = frozenset(
    37→    {
    38→        Status.FIXED.value,
    39→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    40→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    41→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    42→    }
    43→)
    44→_LEGACY_STATUS_ALIASES = {
    45→    Status.RESOLVED.value: Status.FIXED.value,
    46→}
    47→
    48→
    49→class Tier(enum.IntEnum):
    50→    AUTO_FIX = 1
    51→    QUICK_FIX = 2
    52→    JUDGMENT = 3
    53→    MAJOR_REFACTOR = 4
    54→
    55→
    56→def canonical_issue_status(
    57→    value: object, *, default: Status = Status.OPEN
    58→) -> Status:
    59→    """Normalize legacy/unknown issue status values to a canonical enum."""
    60→    token = str(value).strip().lower()
    61→    [REDACTED](token, token)
    62→    if token in _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES:
    63→        return Status(token)
    64→    return default
    65→
    66→
    67→def issue_status_tokens(*, include_all: bool = False) -> frozenset[str]:
    68→    """Return canonical issue-status tokens, optionally including `all`."""
    69→    if include_all:
    70→        return frozenset({*_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES, "all"})
    71→    return _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES
    72→
    73→
    74→def resolved_statuses() -> frozenset[str]:
    75→    """Return the set of statuses that mean an issue is no longer open."""
    76→    return _RESOLVED_STATUSES
    77→
    78→
    79→__all__ = [
    80→    "Confidence",
    81→    "Status",
    82→    "Tier",
    83→    "canonical_issue_status",
    84→    "issue_status_tokens",
    85→    "resolved_statuses",
    86→]
    87→

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_issue_inventory
    12→from desloppify.app.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.base.tooling import check_config_staleness
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    16→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_execution_queue
    17→
    18→from .options import NextOptions
    19→from .queue_flow import build_and_render_execution_queue
    20→from .subjective import _low_subjective_dimensions
    21→
    22→# Backward-compatible test seam: `next` now uses the execution queue wrapper.
    23→build_work_queue = build_execution_queue
    24→
    25→
    26→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    27→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    28→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    29→    state = runtime.state
    30→    config = runtime.config
    31→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    35→    if skill_warning:
    36→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    37→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    38→    if config_warning:
    39→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    40→
    41→    if getattr(args, "format", "terminal") == "terminal":
    42→        print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    43→    build_and_render_execution_queue(
    44→        args,
    45→        state,
    46→        config,
    47→        resolve_lang_fn=resolve_lang,
    48→        load_plan_fn=load_plan,
    49→        build_work_queue_fn=build_work_queue,
    50→        write_query_fn=write_query,
    51→    )
    52→
    53→

1→"""Validation and normalization helpers for review issue import entries."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .contracts_types import (
     6→    REVIEW_ISSUE_REQUIRED_FIELDS,
     7→    VALID_REVIEW_CONFIDENCE,
     8→    ReviewIssuePayload,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def _normalized_non_empty_text(value: object) -> str | None:
    13→    if not isinstance(value, str):
    14→        return None
    15→    text = value.strip()
    16→    return text if text else None
    17→
    18→
    19→def _normalized_non_empty_text_list(value: object) -> list[str] | None:
    20→    if not isinstance(value, list):
    21→        return None
    22→    cleaned = [str(item).strip() for item in value if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
    23→    return cleaned if cleaned else None
    24→
    25→
    26→def validate_review_issue_payload(
    27→    issue: object,
    28→    *,
    29→    label: str,
    30→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    31→    allow_dismissed: bool = True,
    32→) -> tuple[ReviewIssuePayload | None, list[str]]:
    33→    """Validate and normalize one review issue payload entry."""
    34→    if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    35→        return None, [f"{label} must be an object"]
    36→
    37→    dismissed = issue.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed"
    38→    if dismissed and not allow_dismissed:
    39→        return None, [f"{label}.concern_verdict='dismissed' is not allowed here"]
    40→
    41→    if dismissed:
    42→        fingerprint = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_fingerprint"))
    43→        if fingerprint is None:
    44→            return (
    45→                None,
    46→                [
    47→                    f"{label}.concern_fingerprint is required when concern_verdict='dismissed'"
    48→                ],
    49→            )
    50→        normalized: ReviewIssuePayload = {
    51→            "concern_verdict": "dismissed",
    52→            "concern_fingerprint": fingerprint,
    53→        }
    54→        concern_type = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_type"))
    55→        if concern_type is not None:
    56→            normalized["concern_type"] = concern_type
    57→        concern_file = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_file"))
    58→        if concern_file is not None:
    59→            normalized["concern_file"] = concern_file
    60→        reasoning = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("reasoning"))
    61→        if reasoning is not None:
    62→            normalized["reasoning"] = reasoning
    63→        return normalized, []
    64→
    65→    errors: list[str] = []
    66→    missing = [field for field in REVIEW_ISSUE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if field not in issue]
    67→    if missing:
    68→        errors.append(f"{label} missing required fields: {', '.join(missing)}")
    69→        return None, errors
    70→
    71→    dimension = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("dimension"))
    72→    if dimension is None:
    73→        errors.append(f"{label}.dimension must be a non-empty string")
    74→    elif allowed_dimensions is not None and dimension not in allowed_dimensions:
    75→        errors.append(f"{label}.dimension '{dimension}' is not allowed")
    76→
    77→    identifier = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("identifier"))
    78→    if identifier is None:
    79→        errors.append(f"{label}.identifier must be a non-empty string")
    80→
    81→    summary = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("summary"))
    82→    if summary is None:
    83→        errors.append(f"{label}.summary must be a non-empty string")
    84→
    85→    suggestion = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("suggestion"))
    86→    if suggestion is None:
    87→        errors.append(f"{label}.suggestion must be a non-empty string")
    88→
    89→    confidence = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("confidence"))
    90→    confidence_text = confidence.lower() if confidence is not None else ""
    91→    if confidence_text not in VALID_REVIEW_CONFIDENCE:
    92→        errors.append(f"{label}.confidence must be one of: high, medium, low")
    93→
    94→    related_files = _normalized_non_empty_text_list(issue.get("related_files"))
    95→    if related_files is None:
    96→        errors.append(f"{label}.related_files must contain at least one file path string")
    97→
    98→    evidence = _normalized_non_empty_text_list(issue.get("evidence"))
    99→    if evidence is None:
   100→        errors.append(f"{label}.evidence must contain at least one concrete evidence string")
   101→
   102→    if errors:
   103→        return None, errors
   104→
   105→    normalized_payload: ReviewIssuePayload = {
   106→        "dimension": dimension or "",
   107→        "identifier": identifier or "",
   108→        "summary": summary or "",
   109→        "confidence": confidence_text,
   110→        "suggestion": suggestion or "",
   111→        "related_files": related_files or [],
   112→        "evidence": evidence or [],
   113→    }
   114→    reasoning = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("reasoning"))
   115→    if reasoning is not None:
   116→        normalized_payload["reasoning"] = reasoning
   117→    concern_type = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_type"))
   118→    if concern_type is not None:
   119→        normalized_payload["concern_type"] = concern_type
   120→    concern_file = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_file"))
   121→    if concern_file is not None:
   122→        normalized_payload["concern_file"] = concern_file
   123→    file_path = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("file"))
   124→    if file_path is not None:
   125→        normalized_payload["file"] = file_path
   126→    evidence_lines = issue.get("evidence_lines")
   127→    if isinstance(evidence_lines, list):
   128→        normalized_lines = [line for line in evidence_lines if isinstance(line, int)]
   129→        if normalized_lines:
   130→            normalized_payload["evidence_lines"] = normalized_lines
   131→    return normalized_payload, []
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = ["validate_review_issue_payload"]
   135→

    42→        return True
    43→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    44→    if validate_note_length(note):
    45→        return True

    50→def _load_state_with_guards(
    51→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    52→    *,
    53→    attestation: str | None,
    54→) -> tuple[str, dict] | None:
    55→    """Validate inputs, apply guardrails, and load state for resolve."""
    56→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)

    64→        return None
    65→

    67→        require_triage_current_or_exit(

    69→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
    70→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
    71→        )
    72→
    73→    return state_file, state

    75→
    76→def _resolve_ids_with_snapshots(
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    79→    *,
    80→    attestation: str | None,
    81→) -> tuple[object, dict[str, float], list[str]]:

    91→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    92→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    93→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    94→        if isinstance(dim, str)

   100→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   101→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
   102→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   103→    loaded = _load_state_with_guards(args, attestation=attestation)
   104→    if loaded is None:
   105→        return
   106→    state_file, state = loaded
   107→    prev, prev_subjective_scores, all_resolved = _resolve_ids_with_snapshots(
   108→        state,
   109→        args,
   110→        attestation=attestation,
   111→    )
   112→    if not all_resolved:
   113→        print_no_match_warning(args)
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   117→
   118→    plan, cluster_ctx = update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   119→        args=args,
   120→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   121→        attestation=attestation,
   122→        state_file=state_file,
   123→    )
   124→    mid_cluster = (
   125→        cluster_ctx.cluster_name is not None and not cluster_ctx.cluster_completed
   126→    )
   127→
   128→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   129→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   130→        state,
   131→        status=args.status,
   132→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   133→    )
   134→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   135→    if not mid_cluster:
   136→        render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   137→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   138→        args=args,
   139→        state=state,
   140→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   141→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   145→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   146→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   147→        state,
   148→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   149→    )
   150→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   151→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   152→
   153→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   154→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   155→        ResolveQueryContext(
   156→            patterns=args.patterns,
   157→            status=args.status,
   158→            resolved=all_resolved,
   159→            next_command=next_command,
   160→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   161→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   162→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   163→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   164→            attestation=attestation,
   165→            narrative=narrative,
   166→            state=state,
   167→        )
   168→    )
   169→    print_fixed_next_user_message(
   170→        args=args,
   171→        plan=plan,
   172→        next_command=next_command,
   173→        mid_cluster=mid_cluster,
   174→        cluster_ctx=cluster_ctx,
   175→    )

1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_issues",
    10→    "resolve_issues",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→from desloppify.engine._state import _recompute_stats
    24→
    25→
    26→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    27→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    28→
    29→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    30→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    31→    """
    32→    if is_numeric(value):
    33→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    34→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    35→        raw = value.get("score")
    36→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    37→            return None
    38→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    39→    return None
    40→
    41→
    42→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    43→    state: StateModel,
    44→    *,
    45→    status: str,
    46→    resolved_issues: list[dict],
    47→    now: str,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review issues are resolved.
    50→
    51→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    52→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    53→    for a re-review.
    54→    """
    55→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    56→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    60→    for issue in resolved_issues:
    61→        if issue.get("detector") != "review":
    62→            continue
    63→        dimension = str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    64→        if dimension:
    65→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    66→
    67→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    68→        if dimension not in assessments:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    72→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    73→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    74→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_issue_{status}"
    75→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    76→        else:
    77→            assessments[dimension] = {
    78→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    79→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    80→                "refresh_reason": f"review_issue_{status}",
    81→                "stale_since": now,
    82→            }
    83→
    84→
    85→def match_issues(
    86→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    87→) -> list[dict]:
    88→    """Return issues matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    89→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    90→    return [
    91→        issue
    92→        for issue_id, issue in state["issues"].items()
    93→        if not issue.get("suppressed")
    94→        if (status_filter == "all" or issue["status"] == status_filter)
    95→        and _matches_pattern(issue_id, issue, pattern)
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→
    99→def resolve_issues(
   100→    state: StateModel,
   101→    pattern: str,
   102→    status: str,
   103→    note: str | None = None,
   104→    attestation: str | None = None,
   105→) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Set issue status for matches and return affected issue IDs."""
   107→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   108→    now = utc_now()
   109→    resolved: list[str] = []
   110→    resolved_issues: list[dict] = []
   111→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   112→    for issue in match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   113→        previous_status = str(issue.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   114→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   115→            continue
   116→
   117→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   118→        if status == "wontfix":
   119→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   120→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   121→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   122→                "captured_at": now,
   123→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   124→                "tier": issue.get("tier"),
   125→                "confidence": issue.get("confidence"),
   126→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(issue.get("detail", {})),
   127→            }
   128→        if status == "open":
   129→            issue["reopen_count"] = int(issue.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   130→            issue.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   131→            issue.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   132→            previous_note = issue.get("note")
   133→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   135→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   136→            reopen_attestation = {
   137→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   138→                "text": attestation or note,
   139→                "attested_at": now,
   140→                "scan_verified": False,
   141→            }
   142→            reopen_attestation["previous_status"] = previous_status
   143→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = reopen_attestation
   144→
   145→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   146→            "status": status,
   147→            "note": note,
   148→            "resolved_at": now,
   149→            "suppressed": False,
   150→            "suppressed_at": None,
   151→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   152→            "resolution_attestation": {
   153→                "kind": "manual",
   154→                "text": attestation,
   155→                "attested_at": now,
   156→                "scan_verified": False,
   157→            },
   158→        }
   159→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   160→        issue.update(updates)
   161→        resolved.append(issue["id"])
   162→        resolved_issues.append(issue)
   163→
   164→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   165→        state,
   166→        status=status,
   167→        resolved_issues=resolved_issues,
   168→        now=now,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   172→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   173→    return resolved
   174→

1→"""Complexity-focused mechanical evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num
     8→
     9→
    10→def _build_complexity_hotspots(
    11→    by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]],
    12→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]],
    13→) -> list[dict]:
    14→    """Top 20 files by composite complexity score."""
    15→    del by_file
    16→    file_data: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    17→
    18→    for issue in by_detector.get("structural", []):
    19→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    20→        if not filepath:
    21→            continue
    22→        signals = _get_signals(issue)
    23→        entry = file_data.setdefault(
    24→            filepath,
    25→            {
    26→                "file": filepath,
    27→                "loc": 0,
    28→                "complexity_score": 0,
    29→                "signals": [],
    30→                "component_count": 0,
    31→                "function_count": 0,
    32→                "monster_functions": 0,
    33→                "cyclomatic_hotspots": 0,
    34→            },
    35→        )
    36→        entry["loc"] = max(entry["loc"], _safe_num(signals.get("loc")))
    37→        entry["function_count"] = max(
    38→            entry["function_count"], _safe_num(signals.get("function_count"))
    39→        )
    40→        entry["component_count"] = max(
    41→            entry["component_count"], _safe_num(signals.get("component_count"))
    42→        )
    43→
    44→        max_params = _safe_num(signals.get("max_params"))
    45→        if max_params >= 5:
    46→            entry["signals"].append(f"{int(max_params)} params")
    47→        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get("max_nesting"))
    48→        if max_nesting >= 4:
    49→            entry["signals"].append(f"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}")
    50→        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get("complexity_score"))
    51→        entry["complexity_score"] = max(entry["complexity_score"], complexity)
    52→
    53→    for issue in by_detector.get("smells", []):
    54→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    55→        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, "smell_id", "")
    56→        if filepath in file_data:
    57→            if smell_id == "monster_function":
    58→                file_data[filepath]["monster_functions"] += 1
    59→            elif smell_id in ("cyclomatic_complexity", "high_cyclomatic"):
    60→                file_data[filepath]["cyclomatic_hotspots"] += 1
    61→
    62→    for issue in by_detector.get("responsibility_cohesion", []):
    63→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    64→        if filepath in file_data:
    65→            clusters = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, "cluster_count"))
    66→            file_data[filepath]["component_count"] = max(
    67→                file_data[filepath]["component_count"], clusters
    68→            )
    69→
    70→    for entry in file_data.values():
    71→        entry["_score"] = (
    72→            entry["loc"] / 100
    73→            + entry["complexity_score"]
    74→            + entry["component_count"] * 3
    75→            + entry["monster_functions"] * 10
    76→        )
    77→        entry["signals"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry["signals"]))
    78→
    79→    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e["_score"])[:20]
    80→    for entry in ranked:
    81→        del entry["_score"]
    82→    return ranked
    83→
    84→
    85→__all__ = ["_build_complexity_hotspots"]
    86→
```

1→"""Organization and layout evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num
     6→
     7→
     8→def _build_flat_dir_issues(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
     9→    """From flat_dirs detector."""
    10→    results: list[dict] = []
    11→    for issue in by_detector.get("flat_dirs", []):
    12→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    13→        kind = _get_detail(issue, "kind") or _get_detail(issue, "reason", "")
    14→        raw_score = _get_detail(issue, "score")
    15→        if raw_score is None:
    16→            raw_score = _get_detail(issue, "combined_score", 0)
    17→        results.append(
    18→            {
    19→                "directory": filepath,
    20→                "kind": kind,
    21→                "file_count": int(_safe_num(_get_detail(issue, "file_count", 0))),
    22→                "combined_score": int(_safe_num(raw_score)),
    23→            }
    24→        )
    25→    results.sort(key=lambda e: -e["combined_score"])
    26→    return results[:20]
    27→
    28→
    29→def _build_large_file_distribution(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> dict | None:
    30→    """Distribution stats from structural issues."""
    31→    locs: list[float] = []
    32→    for issue in by_detector.get("structural", []):
    33→        signals = _get_signals(issue)
    34→        loc = _safe_num(signals.get("loc"))
    35→        if loc > 0:
    36→            locs.append(loc)
    37→    if not locs:
    38→        return None
    39→    locs.sort()
    40→    n = len(locs)
    41→    return {
    42→        "count": n,
    43→        "median_loc": int(locs[n // 2]),
    44→        "p90_loc": int(locs[int(n * 0.9)]) if n >= 10 else int(locs[-1]),
    45→        "p99_loc": int(locs[int(n * 0.99)]) if n >= 100 else int(locs[-1]),
    46→    }
    47→
    48→
    49→__all__ = ["_build_flat_dir_issues", "_build_large_file_distribution"]
    50→

1→"""Error and mutable-state evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from ._accessors import _get_detail, _safe_num
     8→
     9→
    10→def _build_error_hotspots(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    11→    """Files with 3+ exception handling issues from smells detector."""
    12→    error_smell_ids = frozenset(
    13→        {
    14→            "broad_except",
    15→            "silent_except",
    16→            "empty_except",
    17→            "swallowed_error",
    18→            "bare_except",
    19→        }
    20→    )
    21→    file_counts: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int))
    22→
    23→    for issue in by_detector.get("smells", []):
    24→        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, "smell_id", "")
    25→        if smell_id not in error_smell_ids:
    26→            continue
    27→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    28→        if filepath:
    29→            file_counts[filepath][smell_id] += 1
    30→
    31→    results = []
    32→    for filepath, counts in file_counts.items():
    33→        total = sum(counts.values())
    34→        if total < 3:
    35→            continue
    36→        entry = {"file": filepath, "total": total}
    37→        for sid in sorted(error_smell_ids):
    38→            entry[sid] = counts.get(sid, 0)
    39→        results.append(entry)
    40→    results.sort(key=lambda e: -e["total"])
    41→    return results[:20]
    42→
    43→
    44→def _build_mutable_globals(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    45→    """All global_mutable_config issues."""
    46→    results: list[dict] = []
    47→    file_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    48→
    49→    for issue in by_detector.get("global_mutable_config", []):
    50→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    51→        if not filepath:
    52→            continue
    53→        entry = file_data.setdefault(
    54→            filepath,
    55→            {
    56→                "file": filepath,
    57→                "names": [],
    58→                "total_mutations": 0,
    59→            },
    60→        )
    61→        name = _get_detail(issue, "name", "")
    62→        if name and name not in entry["names"]:
    63→            entry["names"].append(name)
    64→        mutations = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, "mutations"))
    65→        entry["total_mutations"] += int(mutations) if mutations else 1
    66→
    67→    results = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e["total_mutations"])
    68→    return results[:20]
    69→
    70→
    71→__all__ = ["_build_error_hotspots", "_build_mutable_globals"]
    72→

1→"""Dependency architecture evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num
     8→
     9→
    10→def _build_boundary_violations(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    11→    """From coupling and layer_violation detectors."""
    12→    results: list[dict] = []
    13→    for det_name in ("coupling", "layer_violation"):
    14→        for issue in by_detector.get(det_name, []):
    15→            filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    16→            target = _get_detail(issue, "target") or _get_detail(
    17→                issue, "imported_from", ""
    18→            )
    19→            direction = _get_detail(issue, "direction") or _get_detail(
    20→                issue, "violation", ""
    21→            )
    22→            results.append(
    23→                {
    24→                    "file": filepath,
    25→                    "target": target,
    26→                    "direction": direction,
    27→                }
    28→            )
    29→    return results[:30]
    30→
    31→
    32→def _build_dead_code(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Orphaned files + uncalled functions."""
    34→    results: list[dict] = []
    35→    for issue in by_detector.get("orphaned", []):
    36→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    37→        signals = _get_signals(issue)
    38→        loc = _safe_num(signals.get("loc", _get_detail(issue, "loc")))
    39→        results.append({"file": filepath, "kind": "orphaned", "loc": int(loc)})
    40→    for issue in by_detector.get("uncalled_functions", []):
    41→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    42→        detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    43→        loc = _safe_num(detail.get("loc", 0)) if isinstance(detail, dict) else 0
    44→        results.append({"file": filepath, "kind": "uncalled", "loc": int(loc)})
    45→    return results[:30]
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_private_crossings(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    49→    """From private_imports detector."""
    50→    results: list[dict] = []
    51→    for issue in by_detector.get("private_imports", []):
    52→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    53→        symbol = _get_detail(issue, "symbol") or _get_detail(issue, "name", "")
    54→        source = _get_detail(issue, "source") or _get_detail(
    55→            issue, "imported_from", ""
    56→        )
    57→        target = _get_detail(issue, "target") or filepath
    58→        results.append(
    59→            {
    60→                "file": filepath,
    61→                "symbol": symbol,
    62→                "source": source,
    63→                "target": target,
    64→            }
    65→        )
    66→    return results[:30]
    67→
    68→
    69→def _build_deferred_import_density(by_file: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    70→    """Files with 2+ deferred_import smells (proxy for cycle pressure)."""
    71→    file_counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    72→    for filepath, file_issues in by_file.items():
    73→        for issue in file_issues:
    74→            if issue.get("detector") != "smells":
    75→                continue
    76→            if _get_detail(issue, "smell_id") == "deferred_import":
    77→                file_counts[filepath] += 1
    78→
    79→    results = [
    80→        {"file": filepath, "count": count}
    81→        for filepath, count in file_counts.items()
    82→        if count >= 2
    83→    ]
    84→    results.sort(key=lambda e: -e["count"])
    85→    return results[:20]
    86→
    87→
    88→__all__ = [
    89→    "_build_boundary_violations",
    90→    "_build_dead_code",
    91→    "_build_deferred_import_density",
    92→    "_build_private_crossings",
    93→]
    94→

1→"""Issue upsert/verification helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import matches_exclusion
     6→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open issues but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for issue in existing.values():
    21→        if issue["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = issue.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' issues enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them — regardless
    30→    # of current issue status (open, wontfix, etc.).
    31→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    32→    suspect: set[str] = set(import_only_detectors)
    33→
    34→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    35→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def _mark_scan_verified(
    50→    issue: dict,
    51→    now: str,
    52→    *,
    53→    note: str,
    54→    attestation_text: str,
    55→) -> None:
    56→    """Record scan corroboration without changing the manual disposition."""
    57→    issue["suppressed"] = False
    58→    issue["suppressed_at"] = None
    59→    issue["suppression_pattern"] = None
    60→    issue["note"] = note
    61→    existing = issue.get("resolution_attestation")
    62→    if not isinstance(existing, dict):
    63→        existing = {}
    64→        issue["resolution_attestation"] = existing
    65→    existing["scan_verified"] = True
    66→    existing["scan_verified_at"] = now
    67→    existing["scan_verification_text"] = attestation_text
    68→
    69→
    70→def verify_disappeared(
    71→    existing: dict,
    72→    current_ids: set[str],
    73→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    74→    now: str,
    75→    *,
    76→    lang: str | None,
    77→    scan_path: str | None,
    78→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    79→) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
    80→    """Update scan corroboration for issues absent from scan.
    81→
    82→    Returns (resolved_count, skipped_other_lang, resolved_out_of_scope, changed_detectors).
    83→    Queue-tracked work stays user-controlled: disappearing from scan does not
    84→    change an open issue to resolved. Manually resolved items can be marked as
    85→    scan-verified when they remain absent.
    86→    """
    87→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = resolved_out_of_scope = 0
    88→    resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
    89→
    90→    for issue_id, previous in existing.items():
    91→        previous_status = previous.get("status")
    92→        if issue_id in current_ids or previous_status not in (
    93→            "open",
    94→            "wontfix",
    95→            "fixed",
    96→            "false_positive",
    97→        ):
    98→            continue
    99→
   100→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
   101→            skipped_other_lang += 1
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        # Suspect detectors (e.g. 'review') are import-only and must never
   105→        # be auto-resolved by a scan — check this BEFORE the scope filter
   106→        # so that review issues with file="." aren't wrongly resolved as
   107→        # "out of current scan scope".
   108→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
   109→            continue
   110→
   111→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   112→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
   113→            if (
   114→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
   115→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
   116→            ):
   117→                if previous_status != "open":
   118→                    scope_note = f"Still absent in current scan scope ({scan_path})"
   119→                    _mark_scan_verified(
   120→                        previous,
   121→                        now,
   122→                        note=scope_note,
   123→                        attestation_text=scope_note,
   124→                    )
   125→                    resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   126→                    resolved_out_of_scope += 1
   127→                continue
   128→
   129→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
   130→            continue
   131→
   132→        if previous_status == "open":
   133→            continue
   134→
   135→        verification_note = (
   136→            "Still absent from scan after manual wontfix"
   137→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   138→            else "Still absent from scan after manual resolution"
   139→        )
   140→        _mark_scan_verified(
   141→            previous,
   142→            now,
   143→            note=verification_note,
   144→            attestation_text="Absent from detector output in latest scan",
   145→        )
   146→        resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   147→        resolved += 1
   148→
   149→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, resolved_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors
   150→
   151→
   152→def upsert_issues(
   153→    existing: dict,
   154→    current_issues: list[dict],
   155→    ignore: list[str],
   156→    now: str,
   157→    *,
   158→    lang: str | None,
   159→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
   160→    """Insert new issues and update existing ones.
   161→
   162→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
   163→    """
   164→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   165→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   166→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   167→    changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
   168→
   169→    for issue in current_issues:
   170→        issue_id = issue["id"]
   171→        detector = issue.get("detector", "unknown")
   172→        current_ids.add(issue_id)
   173→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   174→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(issue_id, issue["file"], ignore)
   175→        if matched_ignore:
   176→            ignored_count += 1
   177→
   178→        if lang:
   179→            issue["lang"] = lang
   180→
   181→        if issue_id not in existing:
   182→            existing[issue_id] = dict(issue)
   183→            if matched_ignore:
   184→                existing[issue_id]["suppressed"] = True
   185→                existing[issue_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   186→                existing[issue_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   187→                continue
   188→            new_count += 1
   189→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   190→            continue
   191→
   192→        previous = existing[issue_id]
   193→        previous.update(
   194→            last_seen=now,
   195→            tier=issue["tier"],
   196→            confidence=issue["confidence"],
   197→            summary=issue["summary"],
   198→            detail=issue.get("detail", {}),
   199→        )
   200→        if "zone" in issue:
   201→            previous["zone"] = issue["zone"]
   202→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   203→            previous["lang"] = lang
   204→
   205→        if matched_ignore:
   206→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   207→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   208→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   209→            continue
   210→
   211→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   212→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   213→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   214→
   215→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   216→            # subjective_review issues are condition-based.  When just
   217→            # completed by an agent import, skip reopening to avoid a
   218→            # resolve-then-reopen loop on the same scan cycle.
   219→            if (
   220→                detector == "subjective_review"
   221→                and previous["status"] in {"fixed", "auto_resolved"}
   222→                and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
   223→            ):
   224→                continue
   225→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   226→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   227→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   228→            previous.update(
   229→                status="open",
   230→                resolved_at=None,
   231→                note=(
   232→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   233→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   234→                ),
   235→            )
   236→            reopened_count += 1
   237→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   238→
   239→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "verify_disappeared",
   244→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   245→    "upsert_issues",
   246→]
   247→
