```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and issue pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import re
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "issue_in_scan_scope",
    10→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    11→    "path_scoped_issues",
    12→    "is_ignored",
    13→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    14→    "remove_ignored_issues",
    15→    "add_ignore",
    16→    "make_issue",
    17→]
    18→
    19→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    21→    Issue,
    22→    StateModel,
    23→    ensure_state_defaults,
    24→    utc_now,
    25→    validate_state_invariants,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (
    28→    issue_in_scan_scope as _issue_in_scan_scope,
    29→)
    30→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (
    31→    open_scope_breakdown as _open_scope_breakdown,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import (
    34→    path_scoped_issues as _path_scoped_issues,
    35→)
    36→
    37→
    38→def path_scoped_issues(
    39→    issues: dict[str, Issue],
    40→    scan_path: str | None,
    41→) -> dict[str, Issue]:
    42→    """Filter issues to those within the given scan path."""
    43→    return _path_scoped_issues(issues, scan_path)
    44→
    45→
    46→def issue_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    47→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    48→    return _issue_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path)
    49→
    50→
    51→def open_scope_breakdown(
    52→    issues: dict[str, Issue],
    53→    scan_path: str | None,
    54→    *,
    55→    detector: str | None = None,
    56→) -> dict[str, int]:
    57→    """Return open-issue counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    58→    return _open_scope_breakdown(
    59→        issues,
    60→        scan_path,
    61→        detector=detector,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def is_ignored(issue_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    66→    """Check if a issue matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    67→    return matched_ignore_pattern(issue_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    68→
    69→
    70→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    71→    issue_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    72→) -> str | None:
    73→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    74→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    75→        if "*" in pattern:
    76→            target = issue_id if "::" in pattern else file
    77→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    78→                return pattern
    79→            continue
    80→
    81→        if "::" in pattern:
    82→            if issue_id.startswith(pattern):
    83→                return pattern
    84→            continue
    85→
    86→        raw_base = pattern.rstrip("/")
    87→        rel_base = rel(pattern).rstrip("/")
    88→        for base in (raw_base, rel_base):
    89→            if not base:
    90→                continue
    91→            if file == base or file.startswith(base + "/"):
    92→                return pattern
    93→
    94→    return None
    95→
    96→
    97→def remove_ignored_issues(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
    98→    """Suppress issues matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
    99→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   100→    matched_ids = [
   101→        issue_id
   102→        for issue_id, issue in state["issues"].items()
   103→        if is_ignored(issue_id, issue["file"], [pattern])
   104→    ]
   105→    now = utc_now()
   106→    for issue_id in matched_ids:
   107→        issue = state["issues"][issue_id]
   108→        issue["suppressed"] = True
   109→        issue["suppressed_at"] = now
   110→        issue["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   111→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_integration import (
   112→        recompute_stats as _recompute_stats,
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   116→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   117→    return len(matched_ids)
   118→
   119→
   120→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   121→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching issues."""
   122→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   123→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   124→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   125→    if pattern not in ignores:
   126→        ignores.append(pattern)
   127→    return remove_ignored_issues(state, pattern)
   128→
   129→
   130→def make_issue(
   131→    detector: str,
   132→    file: str,
   133→    name: str,
   134→    *,
   135→    tier: int,
   136→    confidence: str,
   137→    summary: str,
   138→    detail: dict | None = None,
   139→) -> Issue:
   140→    """Create a normalized issue dict with a stable ID."""
   141→    rfile = rel(file)
   142→    issue_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    return {
   145→        "id": issue_id,
   146→        "detector": detector,
   147→        "file": rfile,
   148→        "tier": tier,
   149→        "confidence": confidence,
   150→        "summary": summary,
   151→        "detail": detail or {},
   152→        "status": "open",
   153→        "note": None,
   154→        "first_seen": now,
   155→        "last_seen": now,
   156→        "resolved_at": None,
   157→        "reopen_count": 0,
   158→    }
   159→
   160→
   161→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   162→
   163→
   164→def _matches_issue_path(issue: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   165→    """Match against the issue's detector name or file path."""
   166→    return (
   167→        issue.get("detector") == pattern
   168→        or issue["file"] == pattern
   169→        or issue["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   170→    )
   171→
   172→
   173→def _matches_issue_name_segment(issue_id: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
   174→    """Match against the name segment of the issue ID.
   175→
   176→    For hashed IDs (detector::path::name::hex8), also match the descriptive
   177→    name (second-to-last segment).  Returns False for IDs without :: or
   178→    patterns containing ::.
   179→    """
   180→    if "::" in pattern or "::" not in issue_id:
   181→        return False
   182→    segments = issue_id.split("::")
   183→    name_segment = segments[-1]
   184→    if name_segment == pattern:
   185→        return True
   186→    if len(segments) < 3 or not _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment):
   187→        return False
   188→    return segments[-2] == pattern
   189→
   190→
   191→def _matches_pattern(issue_id: str, issue: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   192→    """Check if a issue matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   193→    if issue_id == pattern:
   194→        return True
   195→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(issue_id, pattern):
   196→        return True
   197→    if "::" in pattern and issue_id.startswith(pattern):
   198→        return True
   199→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and issue_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   200→        return True
   201→    if _matches_issue_path(issue, pattern):
   202→        return True
   203→    if _matches_issue_name_segment(issue_id, pattern):
   204→        return True
   205→
   206→    return False
   207→
```
```

1→"""Consistency and duplication evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def _build_duplicate_clusters(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
     7→    """From dupes and boilerplate_duplication detectors."""
     8→    results: list[dict] = []
     9→    for det_name in ("dupes", "boilerplate_duplication"):
    10→        for issue in by_detector.get(det_name, []):
    11→            detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    12→            if not isinstance(detail, dict):
    13→                detail = {}
    14→            detail.setdefault("kind", det_name)
    15→            detail.setdefault("name", "")
    16→            detail.setdefault("function", "")
    17→            detail.setdefault("files", [])
    18→            kind = detail.get("kind", det_name)
    19→            name = detail.get(
    20→                "name", detail.get("function", issue.get("summary", "")[:60])
    21→            )
    22→            files = detail.get("files", [])
    23→            if not isinstance(files, list) or not files:
    24→                fallback = issue.get("file", "")
    25→                files = [fallback] if fallback else []
    26→            results.append(
    27→                {
    28→                    "kind": kind,
    29→                    "cluster_size": len(files) if files else 1,
    30→                    "name": name,
    31→                    "files": files[:10],
    32→                }
    33→            )
    34→    return results[:20]
    35→
    36→
    37→def _build_naming_drift(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    38→    """From naming detector."""
    39→    dir_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    40→    for issue in by_detector.get("naming", []):
    41→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    42→        detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    43→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
    44→            detail = {}
    45→        detail.setdefault("expected_convention", "")
    46→        parts = filepath.rsplit("/", 1)
    47→        directory = parts[0] + "/" if len(parts) > 1 else "./"
    48→        entry = dir_data.setdefault(
    49→            directory,
    50→            {
    51→                "directory": directory,
    52→                "majority": detail.get("expected_convention", ""),
    53→                "minority_count": 0,
    54→                "outliers": [],
    55→            },
    56→        )
    57→        entry["minority_count"] += 1
    58→        if filepath not in entry["outliers"]:
    59→            entry["outliers"].append(filepath)
    60→        if not entry["majority"] and detail.get("expected_convention"):
    61→            entry["majority"] = detail["expected_convention"]
    62→
    63→    results = sorted(dir_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e["minority_count"])
    64→    return results[:20]
    65→
    66→
    67→__all__ = ["_build_duplicate_clusters", "_build_naming_drift"]
    68→
```

```
1→"""Security and cross-cutting evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import Counter, defaultdict
     6→
     7→
     8→def _build_security_hotspots(by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
     9→    """Files with 3+ security issues grouped by severity."""
    10→    file_counts: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int))
    11→    for issue in by_detector.get("security", []):
    12→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    13→        if not filepath:
    14→            continue
    15→        detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    16→        severity = detail.get("severity", "medium") if isinstance(detail, dict) else "medium"
    17→        file_counts[filepath][severity] += 1
    18→
    19→    results = []
    20→    for filepath, counts in file_counts.items():
    21→        total = sum(counts.values())
    22→        if total < 3:
    23→            continue
    24→        results.append(
    25→            {
    26→                "file": filepath,
    27→                "high_severity": counts.get("high", 0),
    28→                "medium_severity": counts.get("medium", 0),
    29→                "total": total,
    30→            }
    31→        )
    32→    results.sort(key=lambda e: (-e["high_severity"], -e["total"]))
    33→    return results[:20]
    34→
    35→
    36→def _build_signal_density(by_file: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:
    37→    """Top 20 files by number of distinct detectors firing."""
    38→    results: list[dict] = []
    39→    for filepath, file_issues in by_file.items():
    40→        detectors = set()
    41→        for issue in file_issues:
    42→            det = issue.get("detector", "")
    43→            if det:
    44→                detectors.add(det)
    45→        if len(detectors) >= 2:
    46→            results.append(
    47→                {
    48→                    "file": filepath,
    49→                    "detector_count": len(detectors),
    50→                    "issue_count": len(file_issues),
    51→                    "detectors": sorted(detectors),
    52→                }
    53→            )
    54→    results.sort(key=lambda e: (-e["detector_count"], -e["issue_count"]))
    55→    return results[:20]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _build_systemic_patterns(
    59→    smell_counter: Counter[str],
    60→    smell_files: dict[str, list[str]],
    61→) -> list[dict]:
    62→    """Smell subtypes appearing in 5+ files."""
    63→    results: list[dict] = []
    64→    for smell_id, _count in smell_counter.most_common():
    65→        unique_files = sorted(set(smell_files.get(smell_id, [])))
    66→        if len(unique_files) < 5:
    67→            continue
    68→        file_counts = Counter(smell_files[smell_id])
    69→        hotspots = [f"{f} ({c})" for f, c in file_counts.most_common(5)]
    70→        results.append(
    71→            {
    72→                "pattern": smell_id,
    73→                "file_count": len(unique_files),
    74→                "hotspots": hotspots,
    75→            }
    76→        )
    77→    return results[:20]
    78→
    79→
    80→__all__ = ["_build_security_hotspots", "_build_signal_density", "_build_systemic_patterns"]
    81→
```

```
1→"""Orchestration helpers for holistic review payload preparation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.models import HolisticContext
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.review._prepare.helpers import HOLISTIC_WORKFLOW
    11→
    12→from .prepare_holistic_payload_parts import (
    13→    _attach_issue_history_context,
    14→    _build_selected_prompts,
    15→)
    16→from .prepare_holistic_scope import (
    17→    collect_allowed_review_files,
    18→    file_in_allowed_scope,
    19→    filter_batches_to_file_scope,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→def _resolve_review_files(
    24→    path: Path,
    25→    lang: object,
    26→    options: object,
    27→) -> tuple[list[str], set[str]]:
    28→    """Resolve scoped review files and the allowed-review-file set."""
    29→    discovered_files = (
    30→        options.files
    31→        if options.files is not None
    32→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
    33→    )
    34→    allowed = collect_allowed_review_files(discovered_files, lang, base_path=path)
    35→    scoped_files = [
    36→        filepath
    37→        for filepath in discovered_files
    38→        if file_in_allowed_scope(filepath, allowed)
    39→    ]
    40→    return scoped_files, allowed
    41→
    42→
    43→def _build_review_contexts(
    44→    path: Path,
    45→    lang: object,
    46→    state: dict,
    47→    review_files: list[str],
    48→    *,
    49→    is_file_cache_enabled_fn,
    50→    enable_file_cache_fn,
    51→    disable_file_cache_fn,
    52→    build_holistic_context_fn,
    53→    build_review_context_fn,
    54→) -> tuple[HolisticContext, object]:
    55→    """Build holistic and review contexts, managing the file cache lifecycle."""
    56→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled_fn()
    57→    if not already_cached:
    58→        enable_file_cache_fn()
    59→    try:
    60→        context = HolisticContext.from_raw(
    61→            build_holistic_context_fn(path, lang, state, files=review_files)
    62→        )
    63→        review_ctx = build_review_context_fn(path, lang, state, files=review_files)
    64→    finally:
    65→        if not already_cached:
    66→            disable_file_cache_fn()
    67→    return context, review_ctx
    68→
    69→
    70→@dataclass
    71→class _DimensionContext:
    72→    """Resolved dimension configuration for holistic review."""
    73→
    74→    dims: list[str]
    75→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any]
    76→    per_file_prompts: dict[str, Any]
    77→    system_prompt: str
    78→    lang_guide: str
    79→    invalid_requested: list[str]
    80→    invalid_default: list[str]
    81→
    82→
    83→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    84→class HolisticPrepareDependencies:
    85→    """Injected collaborators used by holistic payload orchestration."""
    86→
    87→    is_file_cache_enabled_fn: object
    88→    enable_file_cache_fn: object
    89→    disable_file_cache_fn: object
    90→    build_holistic_context_fn: object
    91→    build_review_context_fn: object
    92→    load_dimensions_for_lang_fn: object
    93→    resolve_dimensions_fn: object
    94→    get_lang_guidance_fn: object
    95→    build_investigation_batches_fn: object
    96→    batch_concerns_fn: object
    97→    filter_batches_to_dimensions_fn: object
    98→    append_full_sweep_batch_fn: object
    99→    serialize_context_fn: object
   100→    log_best_effort_failure_fn: object
   101→    logger: object
   102→
   103→
   104→def _resolve_dimension_context(
   105→    lang_name: str,
   106→    options: object,
   107→    *,
   108→    load_dimensions_for_lang_fn,
   109→    resolve_dimensions_fn,
   110→    get_lang_guidance_fn,
   111→) -> _DimensionContext:
   112→    """Load, resolve, and validate dimensions for the review."""
   113→    default_dims, holistic_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang_fn(lang_name)
   114→    _, per_file_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang_fn(lang_name)
   115→    dims = resolve_dimensions_fn(
   116→        cli_dimensions=options.dimensions,
   117→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   118→    )
   119→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance_fn(lang_name)
   120→    valid_dims = set(holistic_prompts) | set(per_file_prompts)
   121→    invalid_requested = [
   122→        dim for dim in (options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   123→    ]
   124→    invalid_default = [dim for dim in default_dims if dim not in valid_dims]
   125→    return _DimensionContext(
   126→        dims=dims,
   127→        holistic_prompts=holistic_prompts,
   128→        per_file_prompts=per_file_prompts,
   129→        system_prompt=system_prompt,
   130→        lang_guide=lang_guide,
   131→        invalid_requested=invalid_requested,
   132→        invalid_default=invalid_default,
   133→    )
   134→
   135→
   136→def _append_concerns_batch(
   137→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   138→    state: dict,
   139→    dims: list[str],
   140→    allowed_review_files: set[str],
   141→    max_files_per_batch: int,
   142→    *,
   143→    batch_concerns_fn,
   144→    log_best_effort_failure_fn,
   145→    log: object,
   146→) -> None:
   147→    """Generate concern signals and append as a batch (best-effort)."""
   148→    try:
   149→        from desloppify.engine._concerns.generators import generate_concerns
   150→
   151→        concerns = generate_concerns(state)
   152→        concerns = [
   153→            concern
   154→            for concern in concerns
   155→            if file_in_allowed_scope(getattr(concern, "file", ""), allowed_review_files)
   156→        ]
   157→        concerns_batch = batch_concerns_fn(
   158→            concerns,
   159→            max_files=max_files_per_batch,
   160→            active_dimensions=dims,
   161→        )
   162→        if concerns_batch:
   163→            concern_dim = concerns_batch["dimensions"][0]
   164→            merged = False
   165→            for existing in batches:
   166→                if existing.get("dimensions") == [concern_dim]:
   167→                    existing_files = set(existing.get("files_to_read", []))
   168→                    for filepath in concerns_batch.get("files_to_read", []):
   169→                        if filepath not in existing_files:
   170→                            existing["files_to_read"].append(filepath)
   171→                            existing_files.add(filepath)
   172→                    existing["concern_signals"] = concerns_batch.get("concern_signals", [])
   173→                    existing["concern_signal_count"] = concerns_batch.get("concern_signal_count", 0)
   174→                    merged = True
   175→                    break
   176→            if not merged:
   177→                batches.append(concerns_batch)
   178→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   179→        log_best_effort_failure_fn(log, "generate review concern batch", exc)
   180→
   181→
   182→def prepare_holistic_review_payload(
   183→    path: Path,
   184→    lang: object,
   185→    state: dict,
   186→    options,
   187→    *,
   188→    deps: HolisticPrepareDependencies,
   189→) -> dict[str, object]:
   190→    """Prepare holistic review payload with injected dependencies for patchability."""
   191→    scoped_files, allowed_review_files = _resolve_review_files(path, lang, options)
   192→
   193→    context, review_ctx = _build_review_contexts(
   194→        path,
   195→        lang,
   196→        state,
   197→        scoped_files,
   198→        is_file_cache_enabled_fn=deps.is_file_cache_enabled_fn,
   199→        enable_file_cache_fn=deps.enable_file_cache_fn,
   200→        disable_file_cache_fn=deps.disable_file_cache_fn,
```

```
    38→    def __contains__(self, key: object) -> bool:

    43→
    44→    def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: list[str]) -> None:

    47→    def pop(self, key: str, default: list[str] | None = None) -> list[str] | None:

    79→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(
    80→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    81→    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    82→) -> None:
    83→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    84→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    85→    persisted_raw = config.get("exclude", [])
    86→    persisted = (
    87→        [entry for entry in persisted_raw if isinstance(entry, str)]
    88→        if isinstance(persisted_raw, list)
    89→        else []
    90→    )
    91→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    92→    if not combined:
    93→        return
    94→    set_exclusions(combined)
    95→    if cli_exclusions:
    96→        print(
    97→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    98→            file=sys.stderr,
    99→        )
   100→        return
```

```
1→"""Issue validation and stale-resolution helpers for holistic review imports."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Issue, StateModel
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions import normalize_dimension_name
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts_types import (
    12→    ReviewIssuePayload,
    13→    ReviewScopePayload,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts_validation import (
    16→    validate_review_issue_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.payload import (
    19→    normalize_review_confidence,
    20→    review_tier,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.resolution import (
    23→    auto_resolve_review_issues,
    24→)
    25→
    26→_POSITIVE_PREFIXES = (
    27→    "good ",
    28→    "well ",
    29→    "strong ",
    30→    "clean ",
    31→    "excellent ",
    32→    "nice ",
    33→    "solid ",
    34→)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _build_holistic_detail(
    38→    *,
    39→    content_hash: str,
    40→    dimension: str,
    41→    related_files: list[str],
    42→    evidence: list[str],
    43→    suggestion: str,
    44→    reasoning: str,
    45→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    46→    detail: dict[str, Any] = {
    47→        "holistic": True,
    48→        "dimension": dimension,
    49→        "related_files": related_files,
    50→        "evidence": evidence,
    51→        "suggestion": suggestion,
    52→        "reasoning": reasoning,
    53→    }
    54→    detail["summary_hash"] = content_hash
    55→    return detail
    56→
    57→
    58→def validate_and_build_issues(
    59→    issues_list: list[ReviewIssuePayload],
    60→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    61→    lang_name: str,
    62→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    63→    """Validate raw holistic issues and build state-ready issue dicts.
    64→
    65→    Returns (review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns).
    66→    """
    67→    review_issues: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    68→    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    69→    dismissed_concerns: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    70→    allowed_dimensions = {
    71→        dim for dim in holistic_prompts if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    for idx, raw_issue in enumerate(issues_list):
    75→        issue, issue_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
    76→            raw_issue,
    77→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
    78→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions,
    79→            allow_dismissed=True,
    80→        )
    81→        if issue_errors:
    82→            skipped.append(
    83→                {
    84→                    "index": idx,
    85→                    "missing": issue_errors,
    86→                    "identifier": (
    87→                        raw_issue.get("identifier", "<none>")
    88→                        if isinstance(raw_issue, dict)
    89→                        else "<none>"
    90→                    ),
    91→                }
    92→            )
    93→            continue
    94→        if issue is None:
    95→            raise ValueError(
    96→                "review issue payload missing after validation succeeded"
    97→            )
    98→
    99→        if issue.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed":
   100→            fingerprint = issue.get("concern_fingerprint", "")
   101→            if fingerprint:
   102→                dismissed_concerns.append(
   103→                    {
   104→                        "fingerprint": fingerprint,
   105→                        "concern_type": issue.get("concern_type", ""),
   106→                        "concern_file": issue.get("concern_file", ""),
   107→                        "reasoning": issue.get("reasoning", ""),
   108→                    }
   109→                )
   110→            continue
   111→
   112→        summary_text = str(issue.get("summary", ""))
   113→        if summary_text.lower().startswith(_POSITIVE_PREFIXES):
   114→            skipped.append(
   115→                {
   116→                    "index": idx,
   117→                    "missing": ["positive observation (not a defect)"],
   118→                    "identifier": issue.get("identifier", "<none>"),
   119→                }
   120→            )
   121→            continue
   122→
   123→        dimension = issue["dimension"]
   124→
   125→        is_confirmed_concern = issue.get("concern_verdict") == "confirmed"
   126→        detector = "concerns" if is_confirmed_concern else "review"
   127→
   128→        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(summary_text.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
   129→        detail = _build_holistic_detail(
   130→            content_hash=content_hash,
   131→            dimension=dimension,
   132→            related_files=issue["related_files"],
   133→            evidence=issue["evidence"],
   134→            suggestion=issue.get("suggestion", ""),
   135→            reasoning=issue.get("reasoning", ""),
   136→        )
   137→        if is_confirmed_concern:
   138→            detail["concern_type"] = issue.get("concern_type", "")
   139→            detail["concern_verdict"] = "confirmed"
   140→
   141→        prefix = "concern" if is_confirmed_concern else "holistic"
   142→        issue_file = issue.get("concern_file", "") if is_confirmed_concern else ""
   143→        confidence = normalize_review_confidence(issue.get("confidence", "low"))
   144→        imported = make_issue(
   145→            detector=detector,
   146→            file=issue_file,
   147→            name=f"{prefix}::{dimension}::{issue['identifier']}",
   148→            tier=review_tier(confidence, holistic=True),
   149→            confidence=confidence,
   150→            summary=summary_text,
   151→            detail=detail,
   152→        )
   153→        imported["lang"] = lang_name
   154→        review_issues.append(imported)
   155→
   156→    return review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns
   157→
   158→
   159→def collect_imported_dimensions(
   160→    *,
   161→    issues_list: list[ReviewIssuePayload],
   162→    review_issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   163→    assessments: dict[str, Any] | None,
   164→    review_scope: ReviewScopePayload | dict[str, Any] | None,
   165→    valid_dimensions: set[str],
   166→) -> set[str]:
   167→    """Return normalized dimensions this import explicitly covered."""
   168→    imported_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   169→
   170→    if isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   171→        scope_dims = review_scope.get("imported_dimensions")
   172→        if isinstance(scope_dims, list):
   173→            for raw_dim in scope_dims:
   174→                normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   175→                if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   176→                    imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   177→
   178→    for issue in issues_list:
   179→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   180→            continue
   181→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(issue.get("dimension", "")))
   182→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   183→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   184→
   185→    for issue in review_issues:
   186→        detail = issue.get("detail")
   187→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   188→            continue
   189→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   190→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   191→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   192→
   193→    for raw_dim in (assessments or {}):
   194→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   195→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   196→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   197→
   198→    return imported_dimensions
   199→
   200→
   201→def auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   202→    state: StateModel,
   203→    new_ids: set[str],
   204→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   205→    utc_now_fn,
   206→    *,
   207→    imported_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   208→    full_sweep_included: bool | None = None,
   209→) -> None:
   210→    """Auto-resolve open holistic issues not present in the latest import."""
   211→    scope_dimensions = {
   212→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   213→        for dim in (imported_dimensions or set())
   214→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   215→    }
   216→    scoped_reimport = full_sweep_included is False
   217→    if scoped_reimport and not scope_dimensions:
   218→        return
   219→
   220→    def _should_resolve(issue: Issue) -> bool:
   221→        if issue.get("detector") not in ("review", "concerns"):
   222→            return False
   223→        detail = issue.get("detail")
   224→        if not isinstance(detail, dict) or not detail.get("holistic"):
   225→            return False
   226→        if not scoped_reimport:
   227→            return True
   228→        dimension = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   229→        return dimension in scope_dimensions
   230→
   231→    auto_resolve_review_issues(
   232→        state,
   233→        new_ids=new_ids,
   234→        diff=diff,
   235→        note="not reported in latest holistic re-import",
   236→        should_resolve=_should_resolve,
   237→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "auto_resolve_stale_holistic",
   243→    "collect_imported_dimensions",
   244→    "validate_and_build_issues",
   245→]
   246→
```

```
1→"""Prompt/data contracts for whole-plan triage orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→    triage_clusters,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data needed to produce/update triage clusters."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # compatibility alias; values are clusters
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→    @property
    33→    def existing_clusters(self) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
    34→        """Canonical cluster view used by staged triage docs."""
    35→        return self.existing_epics
    36→
    37→@dataclass
    38→class DismissedIssue:
    39→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    40→
    41→    issue_id: str
    42→    reason: str
    43→
    44→@dataclass
    45→class ContradictionNote:
    46→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    47→
    48→    kept: str
    49→    dismissed: str
    50→    reason: str
    51→
    52→@dataclass
    53→class TriageResult:
    54→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    55→
    56→    strategy_summary: str
    57→    epics: list[dict]
    58→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    59→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    60→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    61→
    62→    @property
    63→    def clusters(self) -> list[dict]:
    64→        """Canonical cluster view for triage results."""
    65→        return self.epics
    66→
    67→
    68→def _issue_dimension(issue: dict) -> str:
    69→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    70→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    71→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    72→        if isinstance(dimension, str):
    73→            return dimension
    74→    return ""
    75→
    76→
    77→def _recurring_dimensions(
    78→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    79→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    80→) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]]:
    81→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    82→    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():
    83→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    84→        if dimension:
    85→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    86→
    87→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    88→    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():
    89→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    90→        if dimension:
    91→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    92→
    93→    recurring: dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]] = {}
    94→    for dimension in sorted(set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim)):
    95→        recurring[dimension] = {
    96→            "open": sorted(open_by_dim[dimension]),
    97→            "resolved": sorted(resolved_by_dim[dimension]),
    98→        }
    99→    return recurring
   100→
```

```
1→"""Data models for review-context construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Mapping
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→from typing import Any, TypeAlias
     8→
     9→
    10→class ReviewContextSchemaError(ValueError):
    11→    """Raised when contextual review payloads violate section-shape contracts."""
    12→
    13→
    14→SectionPayload: TypeAlias = dict[str, Any]
    15→
    16→
    17→def _empty_section(name: str) -> SectionPayload:
    18→    _ = name
    19→    return {}
    20→
    21→
    22→def _coerce_section(
    23→    *,
    24→    section: str,
    25→    value: object,
    26→    strict: bool,
    27→) -> SectionPayload:
    28→    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
    29→        return dict(value)
    30→    if value is None:
    31→        return {}
    32→    if strict:
    33→        raise ReviewContextSchemaError(
    34→            f"review context section '{section}' must be an object, got {type(value).__name__}"
    35→        )
    36→    return {}
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ReviewContext:
    41→    """Codebase-wide context for contextual file evaluation."""
    42→
    43→    naming_vocabulary: SectionPayload = field(
    44→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("naming_vocabulary")
    45→    )
    46→    error_conventions: SectionPayload = field(
    47→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("error_conventions")
    48→    )
    49→    module_patterns: SectionPayload = field(
    50→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("module_patterns")
    51→    )

    82→        "zone_distribution",

    91→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:

    93→
    94→    def normalize_sections(self, *, strict: bool) -> None:
    95→        for section in self._SECTION_NAMES:
    96→            setattr(
    97→                self,
    98→                section,
    99→                _coerce_section(section=section, value=getattr(self, section), strict=strict),
   100→            )
   101→
   102→    @classmethod
   103→    def from_raw(cls, payload: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> ReviewContext:
   104→        if payload is not None and not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
   105→            raise ReviewContextSchemaError(
   106→                f"review context payload must be an object, got {type(payload).__name__}"
   107→            )
   108→        raw = payload if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else {}
   109→        return cls(**{section: raw.get(section) for section in cls._SECTION_NAMES})
   110→

   112→@dataclass
   113→class HolisticContext:
   114→    """Typed seam contract for holistic review context pipelines."""
   115→
   116→    architecture: SectionPayload = field(
   117→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("architecture")
   118→    )
   119→    coupling: SectionPayload = field(default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("coupling"))
   120→    conventions: SectionPayload = field(
   121→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("conventions")

   124→    abstractions: SectionPayload = field(

   126→    )
   127→    dependencies: SectionPayload = field(
   128→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("dependencies")
   129→    )
   130→    testing: SectionPayload = field(default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("testing"))
   131→    api_surface: SectionPayload = field(
   132→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("api_surface")
   133→    )
   134→    structure: SectionPayload = field(default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("structure"))
   135→    codebase_stats: SectionPayload = field(
   136→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("codebase_stats")
   137→    )
   138→    authorization: SectionPayload = field(
   139→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("authorization")
   140→    )
   141→    ai_debt_signals: SectionPayload = field(
   142→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("ai_debt_signals")
   143→    )
   144→    migration_signals: SectionPayload = field(
   145→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("migration_signals")
   146→    )
   147→    scan_evidence: SectionPayload = field(
   148→        default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("scan_evidence")
   149→    )
   150→
   151→    _SECTION_NAMES = (
   152→        "architecture",
   153→        "coupling",
   154→        "conventions",
   155→        "errors",
   156→        "abstractions",
   157→        "dependencies",
   158→        "testing",

   162→        "authorization",
   163→        "ai_debt_signals",
   164→        "migration_signals",
   165→        "scan_evidence",
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   169→        self.normalize_sections(strict=True)
   170→
   171→    def normalize_sections(self, *, strict: bool) -> None:
   172→        for section in self._SECTION_NAMES:
   173→            setattr(
   174→                self,
   175→                section,
   176→                _coerce_section(section=section, value=getattr(self, section), strict=strict),
   177→            )
   178→
   179→    @classmethod
   180→    def from_raw(cls, payload: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> HolisticContext:
   181→        if payload is not None and not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
   182→            raise ReviewContextSchemaError(
   183→                f"holistic review context payload must be an object, got {type(payload).__name__}"
   184→            )
   185→        raw = payload if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else {}
   186→        return cls(**{section: raw.get(section) for section in cls._SECTION_NAMES})
   187→
   188→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
   189→        return {section: dict(getattr(self, section)) for section in self._SECTION_NAMES}
```

```
     2→

    27→    validate_and_build_issues as _validate_and_build_issues,
    28→)

    37→
    38→def parse_holistic_import_payload(
    39→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
    40→) -> tuple[list[ReviewIssuePayload], dict[str, Any] | None, list[str]]:
    41→    """Parse strict holistic import payload object."""
    42→    payload = parse_review_import_payload(data, mode_name="Holistic")
    43→    return payload.issues, payload.assessments, payload.reviewed_files
    44→
    45→
    46→def import_holistic_issues(
    47→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
    48→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    49→    lang_name: str,
    50→    *,
    51→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
    52→    utc_now_fn=state_mod.utc_now,
    53→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    54→    """Import holistic (codebase-wide) issues into state."""
    55→    payload: ReviewImportEnvelope = parse_review_import_payload(
    56→        issues_data,
    57→        mode_name="Holistic",
    58→    )
    59→    issues_list = payload.issues
    60→    assessments = payload.assessments
    61→    reviewed_files = payload.reviewed_files
    62→    dimension_judgment = payload.dimension_judgment
    63→    review_scope = issues_data.get("review_scope", {})
    64→    if not isinstance(review_scope, dict):
    65→        review_scope = {}
    66→    review_scope.setdefault("full_sweep_included", None)
    67→    scope_full_sweep = review_scope.get("full_sweep_included")
    68→    if not isinstance(scope_full_sweep, bool):
    69→        scope_full_sweep = None
    70→
    71→    if assessments:
    72→        store_assessments(
    73→            state,
    74→            assessments,
    75→            source="holistic",
    76→            utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
    77→            dimension_judgment=dimension_judgment,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→    _, holistic_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
    81→    valid_dimensions = {
    82→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
    83→        for dim in holistic_prompts
    84→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    85→    }
    86→    review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns = _validate_and_build_issues(
    87→        issues_list,
    88→        holistic_prompts,
    89→        lang_name,
    90→    )
    91→    imported_dimensions = _collect_imported_dimensions(
    92→        issues_list=issues_list,
    93→        review_issues=review_issues,
    94→        assessments=assessments if isinstance(assessments, dict) else None,
    95→        review_scope=review_scope,
    96→        valid_dimensions=valid_dimensions,
    97→    )
    98→
    99→    if dismissed_concerns:
   100→        store = state.setdefault("concern_dismissals", {})
   101→        now = utc_now_fn()
   102→        current_concerns = generate_concerns(state)
   103→        concern_sources = {
   104→            concern.fingerprint: list(concern.source_issues)
   105→            for concern in current_concerns
   106→        }
   107→        for dismissal in dismissed_concerns:
   108→            fingerprint = dismissal["fingerprint"]
   109→            store[fingerprint] = {
   110→                "dismissed_at": now,
   111→                "reasoning": dismissal.get("reasoning", ""),
   112→                "concern_type": dismissal.get("concern_type", ""),
   113→                "concern_file": dismissal.get("concern_file", ""),
   114→                "source_issue_ids": concern_sources.get(fingerprint, []),
   115→            }
   116→
   117→    potentials = ensure_lang_potentials(state, lang_name)
   118→    existing_review = potentials.get("review", 0)
   119→    potentials["review"] = max(existing_review, HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL)
   120→
   121→    concern_count = sum(1 for issue in review_issues if issue.get("detector") == "concerns")
   122→    if concern_count:
   123→        potentials["concerns"] = max(potentials.get("concerns", 0), concern_count)
   124→
   125→    merge_potentials_dict: dict[str, int] = {"review": potentials.get("review", 0)}
   126→    if potentials.get("concerns", 0) > 0:
   127→        merge_potentials_dict["concerns"] = potentials["concerns"]
   128→
   129→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   130→        state,
   131→        review_issues,
   132→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   133→            lang=lang_name,
   134→            potentials=merge_potentials_dict,
   135→            merge_potentials=True,
   136→        ),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→    new_ids = {issue["id"] for issue in review_issues}
   140→    _auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   141→        state,
   142→        new_ids,
   143→        diff,
   144→        utc_now_fn,
   145→        imported_dimensions=imported_dimensions,
   146→        full_sweep_included=scope_full_sweep,
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    if skipped:
   150→        diff["skipped"] = len(skipped)
```

```
    65→def _handle_special_entity_views(
    66→    *,
    67→    entity,
    68→    state: dict,
    69→    config: dict,
    70→    lang_name: str | None,
    71→    pattern_raw: str,
    72→) -> bool:
    73→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    74→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    75→        return True
    76→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    77→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    78→        return True
    79→    return False
    80→
    81→
    82→def _load_entity_matches(
    83→    *,
    84→    state: dict,
    85→    entity,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    scope: str | None,
    89→    chronic: bool,
    90→) -> tuple[str, list[dict]] | None:
    91→    if entity.kind != "dimension" or entity.is_subjective:
    92→        return pattern, load_matches(
    93→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    94→        )
    95→    matches = _load_dimension_issues(state, entity, status_filter)
    96→    if not matches:
    97→        _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    98→        return None
    99→    return entity.display_name, matches

   101→
   102→def _active_plan_or_none() -> dict | None:
   103→    try:
   104→        plan = load_plan()
   105→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   106→        logger.debug("Show command skipped active-plan lookup.", exc_info=exc)
   107→        return None
   108→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"):
   109→        return plan
   110→    return None
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Show all issues for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   115→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   116→    state = runtime.state
   117→    config = runtime.config
   118→
   119→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   123→    if skill_warning:
   124→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   125→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   126→    if config_warning:
   127→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   128→
   129→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   130→
   131→    opts = ShowOptions.from_args(args)
   132→
   133→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   134→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   135→        return
   136→
   137→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   138→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   139→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   140→        state,
   141→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   145→    if _handle_special_entity_views(
   146→        entity=entity,
   147→        state=state,
   148→        config=config,
   149→        lang_name=lang_name,
   150→        pattern_raw=opts.pattern_raw,
   151→    ):
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    match_result = _load_entity_matches(
   155→        state=state,
   156→        entity=entity,
   157→        pattern=pattern,
   158→        status_filter=status_filter,
   159→        scope=scope,
   160→        chronic=opts.chronic,
   161→    )
   162→    if match_result is None:
   163→        return
   164→    pattern, matches = match_result
   165→
   166→    if not matches:
   167→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    (
   171→        surfaced_matches,
   172→        hidden_by_detector,
   173→        noise_budget,
   174→        global_noise_budget,
   175→        budget_warning,
   176→    ) = resolve_noise(
   177→        config,
   178→        matches,
   179→        no_budget=opts.no_budget,
   180→    )
   181→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   182→
   183→    payload = build_show_payload(
   184→        surfaced_matches,
   185→        pattern,
   186→        status_filter,
   187→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   188→            total_matches=len(matches),
   189→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   190→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   191→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   192→        ),
   193→    )
   194→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   195→
   196→    if opts.output_file:
   197→        if write_show_output_file(opts.output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   198→            return
   199→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   200→
   201→    render_issues(
   202→        surfaced_matches,
   203→        pattern=pattern,
   204→        status_filter=status_filter,
   205→        show_code=opts.show_code,
   206→        top=opts.top,
   207→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   208→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   209→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   210→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   211→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   212→    )
   213→    plan_active = _active_plan_or_none()
   214→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   215→    show_subjective_followup(
   216→        state,
   217→        target_strict_score_from_config(config),
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   221→
   222→
   223→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   224→
```

```
    28→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    29→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    30→    try:
    31→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    32→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} issues to {output_file}", "green"))
    33→    except OSError as exc:
    34→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    35→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    36→        return False
    37→    return True
    38→
    39→
    40→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    41→    """Group issues by file and sort by descending count."""
    42→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    43→    for issue in matches:
    44→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    45→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    46→
    47→
    48→def _print_single_issue(issue: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    49→    """Render a single issue to terminal."""
    50→    normalized_status = canonical_issue_status(issue.get("status"))
    51→    status_icon = {
    52→        "open": "○",
    53→        "fixed": "✓",
    54→        "wontfix": "—",
    55→        "false_positive": "✗",
    56→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    57→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    58→    zone = issue.get("zone", "production")
    59→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    60→    print(
    61→        f"    {status_icon} T{issue['tier']} [{issue['confidence']}] {issue['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    62→    )
    63→
    64→    detail_parts = format_detail(issue.get("detail", {}))
    65→    if detail_parts:
    66→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    67→    if show_code:
    68→        detail_raw = issue.get("detail", {})
    69→        detail = detail_raw if isinstance(detail_raw, dict) else {}
    70→        target_line = (
    71→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    72→        )
    73→        if target_line and issue["file"] not in (".", ""):
    74→            snippet = read_code_snippet(issue["file"], target_line)
    75→            if snippet:
    76→                print(snippet)
    77→    if issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"      ⟳ reopened {issue['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    81→                "red",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→    if issue.get("note"):
    85→        print(colorize(f"      note: {issue['note']}", "dim"))
    86→    print(colorize(f"      {issue['id']}", "dim"))
    87→
    88→
    89→def render_issues(
    90→    matches: list[dict],
    91→    *,
    92→    pattern: str,
    93→    status_filter: str,
    94→    show_code: bool,
    95→    top: int,
    96→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    97→    hidden_total: int,
    98→    noise_budget: int,
    99→    global_noise_budget: int,
   100→    budget_warning: str | None,
   101→) -> None:
   102→    """Render grouped issues and rollup summary to terminal."""
   103→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
   104→    print(
   105→        colorize(
   106→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} issues matching '{pattern}'\n",
   107→            "bold",
   108→        )
   109→    )
   110→    if budget_warning:
   111→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   112→    if hidden_total:
   113→        global_label = (
   114→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   115→        )
   116→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   117→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   118→        )
   119→        print(

   124→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   125→                ),
   126→                "dim",
   127→            )
   128→        )
   129→
   130→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   131→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   132→    remaining_issues = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   133→
   134→    for filepath, issues in shown_files:
   135→        issues.sort(
   136→            key=lambda issue: (
   137→                issue["tier"],
   138→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue["confidence"], 9),
   139→            )
   140→        )
   141→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   142→        print(

   144→            + colorize(f"  ({len(issues)} issues)", "dim")
   145→        )
   146→
   147→        for issue in issues:

   158→                ),

   165→    for issue in matches:

   167→        by_tier[issue["tier"]] += 1

   211→def show_agent_plan(
   212→    narrative: dict, matches: list[dict], *, plan: dict | None = None
   213→) -> None:
   214→    """Render a compact plan from current issues and narrative actions.
   215→
   216→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   217→    """
   218→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
   219→        render_plan_with_living_plan(
   220→            [],
   221→            plan=plan,
   222→            header="  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next` to see your next task):",
   223→        )
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   227→    if not actions and not matches:
   228→        return
   229→
   230→    print(
   231→        colorize(
   232→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   233→            "yellow",
   234→        )
   235→    )
   236→    if actions:
   237→        top = actions[0]
   238→        print(
   239→            colorize(
   240→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}",
   241→                "cyan",
   242→            )
   243→        )
   244→    elif matches:
   245→        first = matches[0]
   246→        print(
   247→            colorize(
   248→                "  Agent focus: `desloppify next --count 20` — "
   249→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   250→                "cyan",
   251→            )
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   255→        print()
   256→
   257→
   258→def show_subjective_followup(
   259→    state: dict, target_strict_score: float, *, objective_backlog: int = 0,
   260→) -> None:
   261→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   262→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   263→    if not dim_scores:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   267→        state,

   269→    )
   270→    if not subjective:
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   274→        state,
   275→        subjective,
   276→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   277→        max_quality_items=3,
   278→        max_integrity_items=5,
   279→    )
   280→    if print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=objective_backlog):
   281→        print()
```

```
1→"""Shared holistic review packet construction and next-command helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.coercions import coerce_positive_int
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    11→import desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core as narrative_mod
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.review.prepare import (
    13→    HolisticReviewPrepareOptions,
    14→    prepare_holistic_review,
    15→)
    16→
    17→from .. import runner_packets as runner_packets_mod
    18→from ..helpers import parse_dimensions
    19→from ..runtime_paths import (
    20→    blind_packet_path,
    21→    review_packet_dir,
    22→    runtime_project_root,
    23→)
    24→from .policy import coerce_review_batch_file_limit, redacted_review_config
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class ReviewPacketContext:
    29→    """Normalized review-packet CLI options shared across entrypoints."""
    30→
    31→    path: Path
    32→    dimensions: list[str] | None
    33→    retrospective: bool
    34→    retrospective_max_issues: int
    35→    retrospective_max_batch_items: int
    36→
    37→
    38→def resolve_review_packet_context(args: Any) -> ReviewPacketContext:
    39→    """Parse shared packet options from CLI args."""
    40→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
    41→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
    42→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
    43→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
    44→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
    45→        default=30,
    46→        minimum=1,
    47→    )
    48→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
    49→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
    50→        default=20,
    51→        minimum=1,
    52→    )
    53→    return ReviewPacketContext(
    54→        path=Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    55→        dimensions=dimensions,
    56→        retrospective=retrospective,
    57→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    58→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→def build_holistic_packet(
    63→    *,
    64→    state: StateModel,
    65→    lang: Any,
    66→    config: dict[str, Any],
    67→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
    68→    setup_lang_fn,
    69→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
    70→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
    71→    """Build the canonical holistic review packet payload and lang name."""
    72→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_fn(lang, context.path, config)
    73→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    74→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    75→        state,
    76→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    77→    )
    78→    prepare_fn = prepare_holistic_review_fn or prepare_holistic_review
    79→    packet = prepare_fn(
    80→        context.path,
    81→        lang_run,
    82→        state,
    83→        options=HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    84→            dimensions=context.dimensions,
    85→            files=found_files or None,
    86→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    87→            include_issue_history=context.retrospective,
    88→            issue_history_max_issues=context.retrospective_max_issues,
    89→            issue_history_max_batch_items=context.retrospective_max_batch_items,
    90→        ),
    91→    )
    92→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
    93→    return packet, lang_name
    94→
    95→
    96→def build_run_batches_next_command(context: ReviewPacketContext) -> str:
    97→    """Return the canonical next command for local batch-based review."""
    98→    parts: list[str] = [
    99→        "desloppify",
   100→        "review",
   101→        "--run-batches",
   102→        "--runner",
   103→        "codex",
   104→        "--parallel",
   105→        "--scan-after-import",
   106→    ]
   107→    if context.dimensions:
   108→        parts.extend(["--dimensions", ",".join(context.dimensions)])
   109→    if context.retrospective:
   110→        parts.extend(
   111→            [
   112→                "--retrospective",
   113→                "--retrospective-max-issues",
   114→                str(context.retrospective_max_issues),
   115→                "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   116→                str(context.retrospective_max_batch_items),
   117→            ]
   118→        )
   119→    return " ".join(parts)
   120→
   121→
   122→def build_external_submit_next_command(context: ReviewPacketContext) -> str:
   123→    """Return the canonical next command for external-session submit."""
   124→    parts: list[str] = [
   125→        "desloppify",
   126→        "review",
   127→        "--external-submit",
   128→        "--session-id",
   129→        "<id>",
   130→        "--import",
   131→        "<file>",
   132→    ]
   133→    if context.retrospective:
   134→        parts.extend(
   135→            [
   136→                "--retrospective",
   137→                "--retrospective-max-issues",
   138→                str(context.retrospective_max_issues),
   139→                "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   140→                str(context.retrospective_max_batch_items),
   141→            ]
   142→        )
   143→    return " ".join(parts)
   144→
   145→
   146→def require_non_empty_packet(packet: dict[str, Any], *, path: Path) -> int:
   147→    """Return packet total_files, raising ValueError when no reviewable files exist."""
   148→    total = packet.get("total_files", 0)
   149→    if isinstance(total, bool) or not isinstance(total, int):
   150→        raise ValueError(
   151→            f"invalid review packet shape for path '{path}': total_files must be an integer"
   152→        )
   153→    if total <= 0:
   154→        raise ValueError(f"no files found at path '{path}'. Nothing to review.")
   155→    return total
   156→
   157→
   158→def build_review_packet_payload(
   159→    *,
   160→    state: StateModel,
   161→    lang: Any,
   162→    config: dict[str, Any],
   163→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
   164→    next_command: str,
   165→    setup_lang_fn,
   166→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
   167→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   168→    """Build and validate a holistic review packet without persisting artifacts."""
   169→    packet, _lang_name = build_holistic_packet(
   170→        state=state,
   171→        lang=lang,
   172→        config=config,
   173→        context=context,
   174→        setup_lang_fn=setup_lang_fn,
   175→        prepare_holistic_review_fn=prepare_holistic_review_fn,
   176→    )
   177→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   178→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   179→    require_non_empty_packet(packet, path=context.path)
   180→    return packet
   181→
   182→
   183→def write_review_packet_snapshot(
   184→    packet: dict[str, Any],
   185→    *,
   186→    stamp: str,
   187→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
   188→    review_packet_dir_override: Path | None = None,
   189→    blind_path_override: Path | None = None,
   190→    safe_write_text_fn,
   191→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
   192→    """Persist immutable + blind packet snapshots and return their paths."""
   193→    runtime_root = runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override)
   194→    blind_path = blind_path_override or blind_packet_path(
   195→        project_root_override=runtime_root,
   196→        stamp=stamp,
   197→    )
   198→    packet_dir = review_packet_dir(
   199→        project_root_override=runtime_root,
   200→        review_packet_dir_override=review_packet_dir_override,
   201→    )
   202→    return runner_packets_mod.write_packet_snapshot(
   203→        packet,
   204→        stamp=stamp,
   205→        review_packet_dir=packet_dir,
   206→        blind_path=blind_path,
   207→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   208→    )
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = [
   212→    "ReviewPacketContext",
   213→    "build_external_submit_next_command",
   214→    "build_holistic_packet",
   215→    "build_review_packet_payload",
   216→    "build_run_batches_next_command",
   217→    "require_non_empty_packet",
   218→    "resolve_review_packet_context",
   219→    "write_review_packet_snapshot",
   220→]
   221→
```

```
1→"""Payload builders for show command query/output serialization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→
    10→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    11→class ShowPayloadMeta:
    12→    total_matches: int | None = None
    13→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int] | None = None
    14→    noise_budget: int | None = None
    15→    global_noise_budget: int | None = None
    16→
    17→
    18→def build_show_payload(
    19→    matches: list[dict[str, Any]],
    20→    pattern: str,
    21→    status_filter: str,
    22→    meta: ShowPayloadMeta | None = None,
    23→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    24→    """Build the structured JSON payload shared by query file and --output."""
    25→    resolved_meta = meta or ShowPayloadMeta()
    26→
    27→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    28→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    29→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
    30→    for issue in matches:
    31→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    32→        by_detector[issue["detector"]] += 1
    33→        by_tier[issue["tier"]] += 1
    34→
    35→    payload = {
    36→        "query": pattern,
    37→        "status_filter": status_filter,
    38→        "total": len(matches),
    39→        "summary": {
    40→            "by_tier": {f"T{tier}": count for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())},
    41→            "by_detector": dict(sorted(by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1])),
    42→            "files": len(by_file),
    43→        },
    44→        "by_file": {
    45→            fp: [
    46→                {
    47→                    "id": f["id"],
    48→                    "tier": f["tier"],
    49→                    "confidence": f["confidence"],
    50→                    "summary": f["summary"],
    51→                    "detail": f.get("detail", {}),
    52→                }
    53→                for f in fs
    54→            ]
    55→            for fp, fs in sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1]))
    56→        },
    57→    }
    58→    if resolved_meta.total_matches is not None:
    59→        payload["total_matching"] = resolved_meta.total_matches
    60→    if resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector:
    61→        payload["hidden"] = {
    62→            "by_detector": resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector,
    63→            "total": sum(resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector.values()),
    64→        }
    65→    if resolved_meta.noise_budget is not None:
    66→        payload["noise_budget"] = resolved_meta.noise_budget
    67→    if resolved_meta.global_noise_budget is not None:
    68→        payload["noise_global_budget"] = resolved_meta.global_noise_budget
    69→    return payload
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = ["ShowPayloadMeta", "build_show_payload"]
    73→
```

```
1→"""Formatting helpers for show command rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→DETAIL_DISPLAY = [
     6→    ("line", "line", None),
     7→    ("lines", "lines", lambda v: ", ".join(str(line_no) for line_no in v[:5])),
     8→    ("category", "category", None),
     9→    ("importers", "importers", None),
    10→    ("count", "count", None),
    11→    ("kind", "kind", None),
    12→    ("signals", "signals", lambda v: ", ".join(v[:3])),
    13→    ("concerns", "concerns", lambda v: ", ".join(v[:3])),
    14→    ("hook_total", "hooks", None),
    15→    ("prop_count", "props", None),
    16→    ("smell_id", "smell", None),
    17→    ("target", "target", None),
    18→    ("sole_tool", "sole tool", None),
    19→    ("direction", "direction", None),
    20→    ("family", "family", None),
    21→    ("patterns_used", "patterns", lambda v: ", ".join(v)),
    22→    (
    23→        "related_files",
    24→        "related files",
    25→        lambda v: ", ".join(v[:5]) + (f" +{len(v) - 5}" if len(v) > 5 else ""),
    26→    ),
    27→    ("review", "review", lambda v: v[:80]),
    28→    ("majority", "majority", None),
    29→    ("minority", "minority", None),
    30→    ("outliers", "outliers", lambda v: ", ".join(v[:5])),
    31→]
    32→
    33→
    34→def _append_fn_pair_detail(parts: list[str], detail: dict) -> None:
    35→    if not detail.get("fn_a"):
    36→        return
    37→    a, b = detail["fn_a"], detail["fn_b"]
    38→    parts.append(f"{a['name']}:{a.get('line', '')} ↔ {b['name']}:{b.get('line', '')}")
    39→
    40→
    41→def _append_pattern_evidence(parts: list[str], detail: dict) -> None:
    42→    pattern_evidence = detail.get("pattern_evidence")
    43→    if not isinstance(pattern_evidence, dict) or not pattern_evidence:
    44→        return
    45→    summary_parts = []
    46→    for pattern_name, hits in pattern_evidence.items():
    47→        if isinstance(hits, list):
    48→            summary_parts.append(f"{pattern_name}:{len(hits)} file(s)")
    49→    if summary_parts:
    50→        parts.append(f"evidence: {', '.join(summary_parts)}")
    51→
    52→
    53→def format_detail(detail: object) -> list[str]:
    54→    """Build display parts from a finding's detail payload."""
    55→    if isinstance(detail, str):
    56→        return [f"detail: {detail}"] if detail else []
    57→    if not isinstance(detail, dict):
    58→        return []
    59→
    60→    parts: list[str] = []
    61→    for key, label, formatter in DETAIL_DISPLAY:
    62→        value = detail.get(key)
    63→        if value is None or value == 0:
    64→            if key == "importers" and value is not None:
    65→                parts.append(f"{label}: {value}")
    66→            continue
    67→        parts.append(f"{label}: {formatter(value) if formatter else value}")
    68→
    69→    _append_fn_pair_detail(parts, detail)
    70→    _append_pattern_evidence(parts, detail)
    71→
    72→    return parts
    73→
    74→
    75→def suppressed_match_estimate(pattern: str, hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> int:
    76→    """Estimate hidden-match count for a show pattern using detector-level noise totals."""
    77→    if not isinstance(pattern, str) or not isinstance(hidden_by_detector, dict):
    78→        return 0
    79→    detector = pattern.split("::", 1)[0]
    80→    return int(hidden_by_detector.get(detector, 0))
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = ["DETAIL_DISPLAY", "format_detail", "suppressed_match_estimate"]
    84→
```
