   181→        heartbeat_seconds=15.0,
   182→    )
   183→
   184→
   185→def _content_failures(
   186→    *,
   187→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]],
   188→    clusters: list[str],
   189→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   190→) -> list[int]:
   191→    def _content_label(idx: int) -> str:
   192→        if idx < len(clusters):
   193→            return f"content:{clusters[idx]}"
   194→        return f"content:{idx}"
   195→
   196→    return _run_parallel_or_fail(
   197→        tasks=tasks,
   198→        stage_label="Sense-check",
   199→        batch_label_fn=_content_label,
   200→        log=log,
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def _parallel_failure_result(
   205→    failures: list[int],
   206→    *,
   207→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   208→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
   209→    print(colorize(f"  Sense-check: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}", "red"))
   210→    log(f"sense-check-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   211→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   212→        exit_code=1,
   213→        reason="parallel_execution_failed",
   214→    )
   215→
   216→
   217→def _reload_structure_plan(
   218→    *,
   219→    reload_plan: Callable[[], dict] | None,
   220→    log: Callable[[str], None],
   221→) -> dict | None:
   222→    if reload_plan is None:
   223→        return None
   224→    try:
   225→        reloaded = dict(reload_plan())
   226→        log("sense-check-plan-reloaded phase=structure")
   227→        return reloaded
   228→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive fallback
   229→        print(colorize("  Sense-check: failed to reload plan after content updates.", "red"))
   230→        log(f"sense-check-plan-reload-failed error={exc}")
   231→        return None
   232→
   233→
   234→def _merge_batch_outputs(batch_meta: list[tuple[str, Path]]) -> str:
   235→    parts: list[str] = []
   236→    for label, output_file in batch_meta:
   237→        content = ""
   238→        if output_file.exists():
   239→            try:
   240→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   241→            except OSError:
   242→                content = "(output missing)"
   243→        if not content:
   244→            content = "(no output)"
   245→        parts.append(f"## {label}\n\n{content}")
   246→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   247→
   248→
   249→def run_sense_check(
   250→    *,
   251→    plan: dict,
   252→    repo_root: Path,
   253→    prompts_dir: Path,
   254→    output_dir: Path,
   255→    logs_dir: Path,
   256→    timeout_seconds: int,
   257→    dry_run: bool = False,
   258→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   259→    apply_updates: bool = False,
   260→    reload_plan: Callable[[], dict] | None = None,
   261→    append_run_log=None,
   262→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
   263→    """Run sense-check via parallel codex subprocess batches."""
   264→    _log = append_run_log or _noop_log
   265→
   266→    clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   267→    total_content = len(clusters)
   268→    total = total_content + 1
   269→    _print_sense_header(total_content, apply_updates=apply_updates, log=_log)
   270→
   271→    policy_result = load_policy_result()
   272→    policy_text = render_policy_block(policy_result.policy)
   273→    if not policy_result.ok:
   274→        print(
   275→            colorize(
   276→                f"  Warning: ignoring malformed project policy ({policy_result.message or 'unknown error'}).",
   277→                "yellow",
   278→            )
   279→        )
   280→    content_mode, structure_mode = _sense_modes(apply_updates=apply_updates)
   281→    content_tasks, batch_meta = _content_tasks_and_meta(
   282→        clusters=clusters,
   283→        plan=plan,
   284→        repo_root=repo_root,
   285→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   286→        output_dir=output_dir,
   287→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   288→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   289→        dry_run=dry_run,
   290→        policy_text=policy_text,
   291→        content_mode=content_mode,
   292→        cli_command=cli_command,
   293→        log=_log,
   294→    )
   295→    structure_plan = dict(plan)
   296→    structure_config = _structure_batch_config(
   297→        plan=structure_plan,
   298→        repo_root=repo_root,
   299→        prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   300→        output_dir=output_dir,
   301→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   302→        mode=structure_mode,
   303→        cli_command=cli_command,
   304→    )
   305→    _write_batch_prompt(structure_config)
   306→    batch_meta.append((structure_config.label, structure_config.output_file))
   307→    print(colorize("    Structure batch: global dependency check", "dim"))
   308→    _log("sense-check-structure batch=global")
   309→
   310→    if dry_run:
   311→        if apply_updates:
   312→            print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute sequenced sense-check batches.", "dim"))
   313→        else:
   314→            print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel sense-check batches.", "dim"))
   315→        return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=0, reason="dry_run", dry_run=True)
   316→
   317→    if content_tasks:
   318→        content_failures = _content_failures(tasks=content_tasks, clusters=clusters, log=_log)
   319→        if content_failures:
   320→            return _parallel_failure_result(content_failures, log=_log)
   321→
   322→    if apply_updates and reload_plan is not None:
   323→        reloaded_plan = _reload_structure_plan(reload_plan=reload_plan, log=_log)
   324→        if reloaded_plan is None:
   325→            return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=1, reason="plan_reload_failed")
   326→        structure_plan = reloaded_plan
   327→        structure_config = _structure_batch_config(
   328→            plan=structure_plan,
   329→            repo_root=repo_root,
   330→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   331→            output_dir=output_dir,
   332→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   333→            mode=structure_mode,
   334→            cli_command=cli_command,
   335→        )
   336→        _write_batch_prompt(structure_config)
   337→
   338→    structure_tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]] = {
   339→        0: partial(
   340→            run_triage_stage,
   341→            prompt=structure_config.prompt,
   342→            repo_root=repo_root,
   343→            output_file=structure_config.output_file,
   344→            log_file=structure_config.log_file,
   345→            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   346→            validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   347→        )
   348→    }
   349→    structure_failures = _run_parallel_or_fail(
   350→        tasks=structure_tasks,
   351→        stage_label="Sense-check",
   352→        batch_label_fn=lambda _idx: "structure",
   353→        log=_log,
   354→    )
   355→    if structure_failures:
   356→        return _parallel_failure_result(structure_failures, log=_log)
   357→
   358→    merged = _merge_batch_outputs(batch_meta)
   359→    print(colorize(f"  Sense-check: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   360→    _log(f"sense-check-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   361→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   362→        exit_code=0,
   363→        merged_output=merged,
   364→    )
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = ["run_sense_check"]
   368→

1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result

    27→
    28→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    29→    *,
    30→    plan: dict,
    31→    stages: dict,
    32→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    33→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    34→    is_reuse: bool,
    35→    attestation: str | None,
    36→) -> bool:
    37→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    38→    if not open_review_ids:
    39→        return True
    40→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    41→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    42→        return True
    43→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    44→    cluster_ops = sum(
    45→        activity.get(key, 0)
    46→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    47→    )
    48→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    49→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    50→        return True
    51→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(
    54→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    55→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    56→                "yellow",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→        return True
    60→    print(
    61→        colorize(
    62→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    63→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    64→            "red",
    65→        )
    66→    )
    67→    print(
    68→        colorize(
    69→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    70→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    71→            "dim",
    72→        )
    73→    )
    74→    print(
    75→        colorize(
    76→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    77→            "dim",
    78→        )
    79→    )
    80→    return False
    81→
    82→
    83→def _validate_organize_submission(
    84→    *,
    85→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    86→    plan: dict,
    87→    state: dict,
    88→    stages: dict,
    89→    report: str | None,
    90→    attestation: str | None,
    91→    is_reuse: bool,
    92→    services: TriageServices,
    93→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
    94→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    95→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    96→    if not _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    97→        args=args,
    98→        plan=plan,
    99→        stages=stages,
   100→        attestation=attestation,
   101→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   102→            triage_input=triage_input,
   103→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   104→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   105→        ),
   106→    ):
   107→        return None
   108→
   109→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   110→    if manual_clusters is None:
   111→        return None
   112→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   113→        return None
   114→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   115→        return None
   116→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   117→        plan=plan,
   118→        stages=stages,
   119→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   120→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   121→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   122→        attestation=attestation,
   123→    ):
   124→        return None
   125→
   126→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   127→    if normalized_report is None:
   128→        return None
   129→
   130→    from .evidence_parsing import format_evidence_failures, validate_report_references_clusters
   131→
   132→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   133→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   134→        print(
   135→            colorize(
   136→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   137→                "red",
   138→            )
   139→        )
   140→        return None
   141→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   142→
   143→
   144→def _persist_organize_stage(
   145→    *,
   146→    plan: dict,
   147→    meta: dict,
   148→    report: str,
   149→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   150→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   151→    is_reuse: bool,
   152→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   153→    services: TriageServices,
   154→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   155→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   156→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   157→        stages,
   158→        report=report,
   159→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   160→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   161→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   162→    )
   163→    services.save_plan(plan)
   164→    services.append_log_entry(
   165→        plan,
   166→        "triage_organize",
   167→        actor="user",
   168→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   169→    )
   170→    services.save_plan(plan)
   171→    return cleared, stages
   172→
   173→
   174→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   175→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   176→    *,
   177→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   178→) -> None:
   179→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   180→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   181→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   182→
   183→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   184→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   185→
   186→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   187→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   188→        return
   189→
   190→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   191→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   192→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   193→
   194→    is_reuse = False
   195→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   196→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   197→        is_reuse = True
   198→
   199→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   200→        return
   201→
   202→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   203→    state = runtime.state
   204→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   205→
   206→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   207→        args=args,
   208→        plan=plan,
   209→        state=state,
   210→        stages=stages,
   211→        report=report,
   212→        attestation=attestation,
   213→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   214→        services=resolved_services,
   215→    )
   216→    if validated is None:
   217→        return
   218→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   219→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   220→        plan=plan,
   221→        meta=meta,
   222→        report=normalized_report,
   223→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   224→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   225→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   226→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   227→        services=resolved_services,
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    print_organize_result(
   231→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   232→        plan=plan,
   233→        report=normalized_report,
   234→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   235→        cleared=cleared,
   236→        stages=stages,
   237→    )
   238→
   239→
   240→def cmd_stage_organize(
   241→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   242→    *,
   243→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   244→) -> None:
   245→    """Public entrypoint for organize stage recording."""
   246→    _cmd_stage_organize(args, services=services)
   247→

1→"""Thin wrapper around shared codex process runner for triage execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import subprocess  # nosec B404
     6→import time
     7→from collections.abc import Callable
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.runner.codex_batch import (
    12→    CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    13→    codex_batch_command,
    14→    run_codex_batch,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    17→
    18→
    19→def _output_file_has_text(output_file: Path) -> bool:
    20→    """Return True when the output file exists and contains non-empty text."""
    21→    if not output_file.exists():
    22→        return False
    23→    try:
    24→        return len(output_file.read_text().strip()) > 0
    25→    except OSError:
    26→        return False
    27→
    28→
    29→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30→class TriageStageRunResult:
    31→    """Typed result contract for triage-stage execution."""
    32→
    33→    exit_code: int
    34→    reason: str | None = None
    35→    merged_output: str | None = None
    36→    dry_run: bool = False
    37→
    38→    @property
    39→    def ok(self) -> bool:
    40→        return self.status == "ok"
    41→
    42→    @property
    43→    def status(self) -> str:
    44→        if self.dry_run:
    45→            return "dry_run"
    46→        return "ok" if self.exit_code == 0 else "failed"
    47→
    48→
    49→def run_triage_stage(
    50→    *,
    51→    prompt: str,
    52→    repo_root: Path,
    53→    output_file: Path,
    54→    log_file: Path,
    55→    timeout_seconds: int = 1800,
    56→    validate_output_fn: Callable[[Path], bool] | None = None,
    57→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
    58→    """Execute a triage stage via codex subprocess and return a typed result."""
    59→    normalized_prompt = str(prompt).strip()
    60→    if not normalized_prompt:
    61→        safe_write_text(log_file, "Empty triage prompt — skipping execution.\n")
    62→        return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=2, reason="empty_prompt")
    63→    output_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    64→    log_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    65→    if validate_output_fn is None:
    66→        validate_output_fn = _output_file_has_text
    67→    timeout = timeout_seconds if timeout_seconds > 0 else 1800
    68→    preview = " ".join(
    69→        codex_batch_command(
    70→            prompt=normalized_prompt,
    71→            repo_root=repo_root,
    72→            output_file=output_file,
    73→        )
    74→    )
    75→    safe_write_text(log_file, f"RUNNER COMMAND PREVIEW:\n{preview}\n")
    76→    deps = CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
    77→        timeout_seconds=timeout,
    78→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
    79→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    80→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
    81→        use_popen_runner=True,
    82→        subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
    83→        live_log_interval_seconds=10.0,
    84→        stall_after_output_seconds=120,
    85→        max_retries=1,
    86→        retry_backoff_seconds=5.0,
    87→        sleep_fn=time.sleep,
    88→        validate_output_fn=validate_output_fn,
    89→    )
    90→    exit_code = run_codex_batch(
    91→        prompt=normalized_prompt,
    92→        repo_root=repo_root,
    93→        output_file=output_file,
    94→        log_file=log_file,
    95→        deps=deps,
    96→    )
    97→    reason = None if exit_code == 0 else f"runner_exit_{exit_code}"
    98→    return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=exit_code, reason=reason)
    99→
   100→
   101→__all__ = [
   102→    "TriageStageRunResult",
   103→    "run_triage_stage",
   104→]
   105→

1→"""Shared subprocess runner helpers for codex batch execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import shutil
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_process_impl.attempts import (
    11→    handle_early_attempt_return,
    12→    handle_failed_attempt,
    13→    handle_successful_attempt,
    14→    handle_timeout_or_stall,
    15→    resolve_retry_config,
    16→    run_batch_attempt,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_process_impl.io import extract_payload_from_log
    19→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_process_impl.types import (
    20→    CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    21→    FollowupScanDeps,
    22→)
    23→
    24→
    25→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    26→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
    27→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    28→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
    29→        effort = "low"
    30→    codex = shutil.which("codex") or "codex"
    31→    return [
    32→        codex,
    33→        "exec",
    34→        "--ephemeral",
    35→        "-C",
    36→        str(repo_root),
    37→        "-s",
    38→        "workspace-write",
    39→        "-c",
    40→        'approval_policy="never"',
    41→        "-c",
    42→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
    43→        "-o",
    44→        str(output_file),
    45→        prompt,
    46→    ]
    47→
    48→
    49→def run_codex_batch(
    50→    *,
    51→    prompt: str,
    52→    repo_root: Path,
    53→    output_file: Path,
    54→    log_file: Path,
    55→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    56→    codex_batch_command_fn=None,
    57→) -> int:
    58→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
    59→    if codex_batch_command_fn is None:
    60→        codex_batch_command_fn = codex_batch_command
    61→    cmd = codex_batch_command_fn(
    62→        prompt=prompt,
    63→        repo_root=repo_root,
    64→        output_file=output_file,
    65→    )
    66→    config = resolve_retry_config(deps)
    67→    log_sections: list[str] = []
    68→
    69→    for attempt in range(1, config.max_attempts + 1):
    70→        header, result = run_batch_attempt(
    71→            cmd=cmd,
    72→            deps=deps,
    73→            output_file=output_file,
    74→            log_file=log_file,
    75→            log_sections=log_sections,
    76→            attempt=attempt,
    77→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
    78→            use_popen=config.use_popen,
    79→            live_log_interval=config.live_log_interval,
    80→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    81→        )
    82→        early_return = handle_early_attempt_return(result)
    83→        if early_return is not None:
    84→            return early_return
    85→        timeout_or_stall = handle_timeout_or_stall(
    86→            header=header,
    87→            result=result,
    88→            deps=deps,
    89→            output_file=output_file,
    90→            log_file=log_file,
    91→            log_sections=log_sections,
    92→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    93→        )
    94→        if timeout_or_stall is not None:
    95→            if timeout_or_stall == 0:
    96→                return 0  # recovered from timeout/stall
    97→            # Non-recovered timeout/stall: retry if attempts remain
    98→            if attempt < config.max_attempts:
    99→                delay = config.retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))
   100→                log_sections.append(
   101→                    f"Timeout/stall on attempt {attempt}/{config.max_attempts}; "
   102→                    f"retrying in {delay:.1f}s."
   103→                )
   104→                if delay > 0:
   105→                    deps.sleep_fn(delay)
   106→                continue
   107→            return timeout_or_stall
   108→
   109→        log_sections.append(
   110→            f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
   111→        )
   112→
   113→        success_code = handle_successful_attempt(
   114→            result=result,
   115→            output_file=output_file,
   116→            log_file=log_file,
   117→            deps=deps,
   118→            log_sections=log_sections,
   119→        )
   120→        if success_code is not None:
   121→            return success_code
   122→        failure_code = handle_failed_attempt(
   123→            result=result,
   124→            deps=deps,
   125→            attempt=attempt,
   126→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
   127→            retry_backoff_seconds=config.retry_backoff_seconds,
   128→            log_file=log_file,
   129→            log_sections=log_sections,
   130→        )
   131→        if failure_code is not None:
   132→            return failure_code
   133→
   134→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   135→    return 1
   136→
   137→
   138→def run_followup_scan(
   139→    *,
   140→    lang_name: str,
   141→    scan_path: str,
   142→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   143→    force_queue_bypass: bool = False,
   144→) -> int:
   145→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   146→    scan_cmd = [
   147→        deps.python_executable,
   148→        "-m",
   149→        "desloppify",
   150→        "--lang",
   151→        lang_name,
   152→        "scan",
   153→        "--path",
   154→        scan_path,
   155→    ]
   156→    if force_queue_bypass:
   157→        followup_attest = (
   158→            "I understand this is not the intended workflow and "
   159→            "I am intentionally skipping queue completion"
   160→        )
   161→        scan_cmd.extend(["--force-rescan", "--attest", followup_attest])
   162→        print(
   163→            deps.colorize_fn(
   164→                "  Follow-up scan queue bypass enabled (--force-followup-scan).",
   165→                "yellow",
   166→            )
   167→        )
   168→    print(deps.colorize_fn("\n  Running follow-up scan...", "bold"))
   169→    try:
   170→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   171→            scan_cmd,
   172→            cwd=str(deps.project_root),
   173→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   174→        )
   175→    except deps.timeout_error:
   176→        print(
   177→            deps.colorize_fn(
   178→                f"  Follow-up scan timed out after {deps.timeout_seconds}s.",
   179→                "yellow",
   180→            ),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→        return 124
   184→    except OSError as exc:
   185→        print(
   186→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   187→            file=sys.stderr,
   188→        )
   189→        return 1
   190→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)
   191→
   192→
   193→__all__ = [
   194→    "CodexBatchRunnerDeps",
   195→    "FollowupScanDeps",
   196→    "extract_payload_from_log",
   197→    "codex_batch_command",
   198→    "run_codex_batch",
   199→    "run_followup_scan",
   200→]
   201→
```

1→"""Per-stage subagent prompt builders for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import Counter
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    11→    TriageInput,
    12→    build_triage_prompt,
    13→)
    14→
    15→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    16→from .stage_prompts_instruction_blocks import _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS
    17→from .stage_prompts_instruction_shared import (
    18→    _STAGES,
    19→    PromptMode,
    20→    render_cli_reference,
    21→    triage_prompt_preamble,
    22→)
    23→from .stage_prompts_observe import (
    24→    _observe_batch_instructions,
    25→    build_observe_batch_prompt,
    26→)
    27→from .stage_prompts_sense import (
    28→    build_sense_check_content_prompt,

    33→
    34→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Return sorted short hashes for open review issues."""
    36→    return sorted(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in triage_input.open_issues)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    40→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    41→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    42→    for issue in triage_input.open_issues.values():
    43→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    44→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    45→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    46→    parts = [
    47→        "## Issue Summary",
    48→        f"Open review issues: {len(triage_input.open_issues)}",
    49→    ]
    50→    if dims:
    51→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    52→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    53→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    54→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    55→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    56→    return "\n".join(parts)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _issue_context_for_stage(stage: str, triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    60→    """Return the amount of issue context appropriate for a stage."""
    61→    if stage in {"observe", "reflect"}:
    62→        parts = ["## Issue Data\n\n" + build_triage_prompt(triage_input)]
    63→        if stage == "reflect":
    64→            short_ids = _required_issue_hashes(triage_input)
    65→            parts.append(
    66→                "## Required Issue Hashes\n"
    67→                f"Total open review issues: {len(short_ids)}\n"
    68→                "Every one of these hashes must appear exactly once in your cluster/skip blueprint.\n"
    69→                "Do not repeat hashes outside that blueprint.\n"
    70→                + ", ".join(short_ids)
    71→            )
    72→            parts.append(
    73→                "## Coverage Ledger Template\n"
    74→                "Your final report MUST contain a `## Coverage Ledger` section with one line per issue.\n"
    75→                "Allowed forms:\n"
    76→                '- `- abcd1234 -> cluster "cluster-name"`\n'
    77→                '- `- abcd1234 -> skip "specific-reason-tag"`\n'
    78→                "Do not mention hashes outside the `## Coverage Ledger` section.\n"
    79→                + "\n".join(f"- {short_id} -> TODO" for short_id in short_ids)
    80→            )
    81→        return "\n\n".join(parts)
    82→    return _compact_issue_summary(triage_input)
    83→
    84→
    85→def _format_assessments_table(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
    86→    """Format structured observe assessments as a readable table for downstream stages."""
    87→    if not assessments:
    88→        return ""
    89→    lines = ["### Structured Observe Assessments\n"]
    90→    lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
    91→    lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
    92→    for a in assessments:
    93→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
    94→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
    95→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
    96→        # Truncate recommendation for table readability
    97→        if len(r) > 80:
    98→            r = r[:77] + "..."
    99→        lines.append(f"| {h} | {v} | {r} |")
   100→    return "\n".join(lines)
   101→
   102→
   103→def _relevant_prior_reports(
   104→    stage: str,
   105→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   106→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   107→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   108→    """Return the stage reports that matter for the current stage."""
   109→    wanted = {
   110→        "reflect": ("observe",),
   111→        "organize": ("reflect",),
   112→        "enrich": ("organize",),
   113→        "sense-check": ("organize", "enrich"),
   114→    }.get(stage, tuple(prior_reports))
   115→    result = [(name, prior_reports[name]) for name in wanted if name in prior_reports]
   116→
   117→    # Append structured observe assessments for stages that need verdict data
   118→    if stage in {"reflect", "organize"} and stages_data:
   119→        observe_data = stages_data.get("observe", {})
   120→        assessments = observe_data.get("assessments", [])
   121→        if assessments:
   122→            table = _format_assessments_table(assessments)
   123→            if table:
   124→                result.append(("observe-assessments", table))
   125→
   126→    return result
   127→
   128→
   129→def build_stage_prompt(
   130→    stage: str,
   131→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   132→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   133→    *,
   134→    repo_root: Path,
   135→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
   136→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   137→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   138→) -> str:
   139→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   140→    parts: list[str] = []
   141→
   142→    # Preamble
   143→    parts.append(
   144→        triage_prompt_preamble(mode).format(
   145→            stage=stage.upper(),
   146→            repo_root=repo_root,
   147→            cli_command=cli_command,
   148→        )
   149→    )
   150→
   151→    # Prior stage reports
   152→    relevant_prior_reports = _relevant_prior_reports(stage, prior_reports, stages_data)
   153→    if relevant_prior_reports:
   154→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   155→        for prior_stage, report in relevant_prior_reports:
   156→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   157→
   158→    # Issue data / summary
   159→    parts.append(_issue_context_for_stage(stage, triage_input))
   160→
   161→    # Stage-specific instructions
   162→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   163→    if instruction_fn:
   164→        parts.append(instruction_fn(mode))
   165→
   166→    # CLI reference
   167→    if mode == "self_record":
   168→        parts.append(render_cli_reference(cli_command))
   169→
   170→    # Validation requirements
   171→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   172→
   173→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   174→
   175→
   176→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   177→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   178→    *,
   179→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   180→) -> None:
   181→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   182→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   183→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   184→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   185→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   186→    state = runtime.state
   187→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   188→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   189→
   190→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   191→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   192→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   193→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   194→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   195→        if prior_stage == stage:
   196→            break
   197→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   198→        if report:
   199→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   200→
   201→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root, stages_data=stages)
   202→    print(prompt)
   203→
   204→
   205→__all__ = [
   206→    "build_observe_batch_prompt",
   207→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   208→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   209→    "build_stage_prompt",
   210→    "cmd_stage_prompt",
   211→    "_observe_batch_instructions",
   212→    "_validation_requirements",
   213→]
   214→

1→"""Sense-check prompt builders for triage runner."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from ..helpers import cluster_issue_ids
     8→
     9→
    10→def build_sense_check_content_prompt(
    11→    *,
    12→    cluster_name: str,
    13→    plan: dict,
    14→    repo_root: Path,
    15→    policy_block: str = "",
    16→    mode: str = "output_only",
    17→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
    18→) -> str:
    19→    """Build a content-verification prompt for a single cluster."""
    20→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
    21→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    22→    issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    23→
    24→    parts: list[str] = []
    25→    parts.append(
    26→        f"You are sense-checking cluster `{cluster_name}` "
    27→        f"({len(steps)} steps, {len(issue_ids)} issues).\n"
    28→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
    29→    )
    30→
    31→    if mode == "self_record":
    32→        parts.append(
    33→            "## Your job\n"
    34→            "For EVERY step in this cluster, read the actual source file, verify\n"
    35→            "every factual claim, and apply the needed cluster-step updates directly.\n"
    36→        )
    37→    else:
    38→        parts.append(
    39→            "## Your job\n"
    40→            "For EVERY step in this cluster, read the actual source file and verify\n"
    41→            "every factual claim. Then fix anything wrong or vague.\n"
    42→        )
    43→
    44→    parts.append(
    45→        "## What to check and fix\n"
    46→        "1. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match what the step describes?\n"
    47→        "   Fix: update the line range to match current file state.\n"
    48→        "2. NAMES: Do the function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?\n"
    49→        "   Fix: correct the names.\n"
    50→        "3. COUNTS: \"Update the 3 imports\" — are there actually 3? Or 5?\n"
    51→        "   Fix: correct the count.\n"
    52→        "4. STALENESS: Is the problem the issue describes still present in the code?\n"
    53→        "   If already fixed, note in your report.\n"
    54→        "5. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step without\n"
    55→        "   asking a single question? If not:\n"
    56→        "   - Replace \"refactor X\" with the specific transformation\n"
    57→        "   - Replace \"update imports\" with the specific file list\n"
    58→        "   - Replace \"extract into new hook\" with the filename, function signature, return type\n"
    59→        "6. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match the actual scope? A one-line rename is \"trivial\",\n"
    60→        "   not \"small\". Decomposing a 400-line file is \"large\", not \"medium\".\n"
    61→        "7. DUPLICATES: If you notice this step does the same thing as a step in another\n"
    62→        "   cluster, note it in your report.\n"
    63→        "8. OVER-ENGINEERING: Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:\n"
    64→        "   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation\n"
    65→        "   - Introduce unnecessary config, feature flags, or generalization\n"
    66→        "   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit\n"
    67→        "   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires\n"
    68→        "   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)\n"
    69→        "   If a step is net-negative, recommend removing it or simplifying the approach.\n"
    70→        "   If the entire cluster is net-negative, say so clearly in your report.\n"
    71→    )
    72→
    73→    if policy_block:
    74→        parts.append(policy_block)
    75→
    76→    if mode == "self_record":
    77→        parts.append(
    78→            "## How to apply fixes\n"
    79→            f"Use the exact CLI prefix: `{cli_command}`\n"
    80→            "1. Inspect current state first:\n"
    81→            f"   `{cli_command} plan cluster show {cluster_name}`\n"
    82→            "2. Apply step corrections directly in this cluster:\n"
    83→            f"   `{cli_command} plan cluster update {cluster_name} --update-step N --detail \"...\" --effort <trivial|small|medium|large> --issue-refs <id...>`\n"
    84→            f"   `{cli_command} plan cluster update {cluster_name} --remove-step N`\n"
    85→            "3. Re-check the cluster after edits:\n"
    86→            f"   `{cli_command} plan cluster show {cluster_name}`\n"
    87→        )
    88→    else:
    89→        parts.append(
    90→            "## How to report fixes\n"
    91→            "Describe the exact step corrections needed, including the corrected detail text,\n"
    92→            "the effort tag, and any stale/duplicate/over-engineered steps that should be removed.\n"
    93→            "The orchestrator will apply the updates.\n"
    94→        )
    95→
    96→    if mode == "self_record":
    97→        parts.append(
    98→            "## What NOT to do\n"
    99→            "- Do NOT reorder steps (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   100→            "- Do NOT add --depends-on (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   101→            "- Do NOT add new steps for missing cascade updates (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   102→            "- Do NOT modify any cluster other than the one assigned in this prompt\n"
   103→            "- Do NOT run triage stage commands (`plan triage --stage ...`)\n"
   104→            "- Do NOT debug or repair the CLI / environment\n"
   105→        )
   106→    else:
   107→        parts.append(
   108→            "## What NOT to do\n"
   109→            "- Do NOT reorder steps (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   110→            "- Do NOT add --depends-on (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   111→            "- Do NOT add new steps for missing cascade updates (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   112→            "- Do NOT run any `desloppify` commands\n"
   113→            "- Do NOT debug or repair the CLI / environment\n"
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    # Include cluster steps
   117→    parts.append("## Current Steps\n")
   118→    for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   119→        title = step.get("title", str(step)) if isinstance(step, dict) else str(step)
   120→        detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   121→        effort = step.get("effort", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   122→        refs = step.get("issue_refs", []) if isinstance(step, dict) else []
   123→        line = f"{i}. **{title}**"
   124→        if effort:
   125→            line += f" [{effort}]"
   126→        if refs:
   127→            line += f" (refs: {', '.join(refs[:3])})"
   128→        if detail:
   129→            line += f"\n   {detail[:300]}"
   130→        parts.append(line)
   131→
   132→    if mode == "self_record":
   133→        parts.append(
   134→            "\n## Output\n"
   135→            "Write a plain-text summary of what you verified and what you changed in this cluster."
   136→        )
   137→    else:
   138→        parts.append(
   139→            "\n## Output\n"
   140→            "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
   141→        )
   142→
   143→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   144→
   145→
   146→def build_sense_check_structure_prompt(
   147→    *,
   148→    plan: dict,
   149→    repo_root: Path,
   150→    mode: str = "output_only",
   151→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   152→) -> str:
   153→    """Build a structure-verification prompt for cross-cluster dependency checking."""
   154→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   155→
   156→    parts: list[str] = []
   157→    parts.append(
   158→        "You are checking cross-cluster dependencies for the entire triage plan.\n"
   159→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   160→    )
   161→
   162→    parts.append(
   163→        "## Your job\n"
   164→        "Build a file-touch graph: for each cluster, which files do its steps reference?\n"
   165→        "Then check for unsafe relationships between clusters.\n"
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    parts.append(
   169→        "## What to check and fix\n"
   170→        "1. SHARED FILES: If cluster A and cluster B both have steps touching the same file,\n"
   171→        "   and neither depends on the other → report which dependency edge should be added.\n"
   172→        "2. MISSING CASCADE: If a step renames/removes a function or export, check whether\n"
   173→        "   any other file imports it. If those importers aren't covered by any step in any\n"
   174→        "   cluster → report the cascade step that should be added.\n"
   175→        "   Include the cluster name, affected importers, and issue hash in your report.\n"
   176→        "3. CIRCULAR DEPS: If adding a dependency would create a cycle, flag it in your report\n"
   177→        "   instead of adding it.\n"
   178→    )
   179→
   180→    if mode == "self_record":
   181→        parts.append(
   182→            "## How to apply structure fixes\n"
   183→            f"Use the exact CLI prefix: `{cli_command}`\n"
   184→            "Apply only structure-level mutations:\n"
   185→            f"- Add dependency edges: `{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other-cluster>`\n"
   186→            f"- Add missing cascade steps: `{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --add-step \"...\" --detail \"...\" --effort <trivial|small|medium|large> --issue-refs <id...>`\n"
   187→        )
   188→        parts.append(
   189→            "## What NOT to do\n"
   190→            "- Do NOT modify existing step detail text (content subagents handled that)\n"
   191→            "- Do NOT change effort tags on existing steps\n"
   192→            "- Do NOT remove existing steps\n"
   193→            "- Do NOT run triage stage commands (`plan triage --stage ...`)\n"
   194→            "- Do NOT debug or repair the CLI / environment\n"
   195→        )
   196→    else:
   197→        parts.append(
   198→            "## What NOT to do\n"
   199→            "- Do NOT modify step detail text (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   200→            "- Do NOT change effort tags (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   201→            "- Do NOT remove steps or deduplicate (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   202→            "- Do NOT run any `desloppify` commands\n"
   203→            "- Do NOT debug or repair the CLI / environment\n"
   204→        )
   205→
   206→    # Include all clusters with their steps and dependencies
   207→    parts.append("## Clusters\n")
   208→    for name, c in sorted(clusters.items()):
   209→        if c.get("auto"):
   210→            continue
   211→        steps = c.get("action_steps", [])
   212→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   213→        issues = cluster_issue_ids(c)
   214→        header = f"### {name} ({len(steps)} steps, {len(issues)} issues)"
   215→        if deps:
   216→            header += f"\n  depends_on: {', '.join(deps)}"
   217→        parts.append(header)
   218→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   219→            title = step.get("title", str(step)) if isinstance(step, dict) else str(step)
   220→            detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   221→            line = f"  {i}. {title}"
   222→            if detail:
   223→                line += f"\n     {detail[:200]}"
   224→            parts.append(line)
   225→
   226→    if mode == "self_record":
   227→        parts.append(
   228→            "\n## Output\n"
   229→            "Write a plain-text summary of dependency/cascade fixes you applied."
   230→        )
   231→    else:
   232→        parts.append(
   233→            "\n## Output\n"
   234→            "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
   235→        )
   236→
   237→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   238→
   239→
   240→__all__ = [
   241→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   242→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   243→]
   244→
```

     8→def _observe_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
     9→    tail = """\
    10→When done, run:

    12→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<your analysis with structured assessments>"

    21→## OBSERVE Stage Instructions
    22→
    23→Your task: verify every open review issue against the actual source code.
    24→
    25→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    26→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
    27→- Describe code that was already refactored
    28→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
    29→- Count props/returns/args wrong
    30→
    31→Your job is to catch these. An observe report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
    32→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
    33→
    34→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. That's the next stage (reflect).
    35→Just verify: is each issue real?
    36→
    37→**CRITICAL: You must cite specific issue IDs (hash prefixes like [abcd1234]) in your report.**
    38→The confirmation gate requires citing at least 10% of issues (or 5, whichever is smaller).
    39→
    40→**USE SUBAGENTS to parallelize this work.** Launch parallel subagents — one per dimension
    41→group — to investigate concurrently. Each subagent MUST:
    42→- Open and read the actual source file for EVERY assigned issue
    43→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
    44→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
    45→- Report a clear verdict per issue: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
    46→
    47→Example subagent split for 90 issues across 17 dimensions:
    48→- Subagent 1: architecture + organization (cross_module_architecture, package_organization, high_level_elegance)
    49→- Subagent 2: abstraction + design (abstraction_fitness, design_coherence, mid_level_elegance)
    50→- Subagent 3: duplicates + contracts (contract_coherence, api_surface_coherence, low_level_elegance)
    51→- Subagent 4: migrations + debt + conventions (incomplete_migration, ai_generated_debt, convention_outlier, naming_quality)
    52→- Subagent 5: type safety + errors + tests (type_safety, error_consistency, test_strategy, initialization_coupling, dependency_health)
    53→
    54→### Structured Assessment Template
    55→
    56→**Your report MUST include a structured assessment for EVERY issue.** Copy and fill out this
    57→template for each issue:
    58→
    59→```
    60→- hash: <issue hash>
    61→  verdict: genuine | false-positive | exaggerated | over-engineering
    62→  verdict_reasoning: <why this verdict — what you found when you read the code>
    63→  files_read: [<file paths the agent looked at>]
    64→  recommendation: <what to do about this issue>
    65→```
    66→
    67→**Example:**
    68→```
    69→- hash: 34580232
    70→  verdict: false-positive
    71→  verdict_reasoning: Uses branded string union KnownTaskType with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist.
    72→  files_read: [src/types/database.ts]
    73→  recommendation: No action needed — issue is inaccurate
    74→
    75→- hash: b634fc71
    76→  verdict: genuine
    77→  verdict_reasoning: Confirmed 65 properties at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation.
    78→  files_read: [src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts]
    79→  recommendation: Decompose into focused sub-hooks
    80→```
    81→
    82→**Validation checks (all blocking):**
    83→- Every entry must have a recognized `verdict` keyword
    84→- Every entry must have non-empty `verdict_reasoning`
    85→- Every entry must have non-empty `files_read` list
    86→- Every entry must have non-empty `recommendation`
    87→
    88→**What a LAZY observe report looks like (will be rejected):**
    89→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
    90→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
    91→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
    92→- Template fields left empty or with placeholder text
    93→
    94→{tail}
    95→"""

    98→def _reflect_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
    99→    tail = """\
   100→When done, run:
   101→```
   102→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<your strategy with cluster blueprint>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning dimensions or recurring patterns>"
   103→```
   104→"""
   105→    if mode == "output_only":
   106→        tail = """\
   107→When done, write a plain-text reflect report with a concrete cluster blueprint.
   108→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
   109→"""
   110→    return f"""\
   111→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   112→
   113→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   114→
   115→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   116→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   117→
   118→**The Structured Observe Assessments table (provided below) is your primary input.** It contains
   119→a per-issue verdict (genuine/false-positive/exaggerated/over-engineering) with reasoning. Use
   120→these verdicts as authoritative — do not second-guess observe unless you have specific evidence.
   121→Issues with verdict `false-positive` or `over-engineering` should go into skip lines, not clusters.
   122→
   123→### What you must do:
   124→
   125→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from the observe assessments table)?
   126→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   127→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   128→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? (with per-issue justification — "low priority" is
   129→   not a justification; "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication" is)
   130→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   131→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   132→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   133→   another round of fixes.
   134→7. **Account for every issue exactly once** — every open issue hash must appear in exactly one
   135→   cluster line or one skip line. Do not drop hashes, and do not repeat a hash in multiple
   136→   clusters or in both a cluster and a skip.
   137→
   138→### Your report MUST include both a coverage ledger and a concrete cluster blueprint
   139→
   140→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   141→```
   142→## Coverage Ledger
   143→- a5996373 -> cluster "travel-structure-contract-unification"
   144→- fb113678 -> skip "false-positive-current-code"
   145→
   146→## Cluster Blueprint
   147→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks" (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   148→Cluster "task-typing" (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   149→
   150→## Skip Decisions
   151→Skip "false-positive-current-code" (false positive per observe)
   152→```
   153→
   154→### Hard accounting rule
   155→
   156→- Start your report with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.
   157→- In that section, mention each issue hash **once and only once** on its own ledger line.
   158→- Do **not** mention issue hashes again in cluster rationale paragraphs, recurring-pattern notes,
   159→  or ordering explanations. After the ledger, refer to clusters by name.
   160→- Before finishing, do a self-check: the ledger must cover all open issue hashes exactly once.
   161→
   162→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   163→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   164→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   165→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   166→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   167→- No `## Coverage Ledger`
   168→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   169→- Missing or duplicated issue hashes
   170→
   171→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   172→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   173→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   174→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   175→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   176→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering).
   177→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   178→
   179→{tail}
   180→"""
   181→
   182→
   183→def _organize_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   184→    intro = (
   185→        "The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go\n"
   186→        "where, what to skip. The reflect report included above is authoritative. Do not go\n"
   187→        "search old triage runs for a different blueprint unless you find a concrete mismatch.\n"

   192→2. **Skip issues that observe flagged as false-positive or over-engineering.** This is mandatory,

   199→   Do NOT cluster an issue that observe determined is not a real defect.

1→"""Shared static text blocks for triage stage prompts."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Literal
     6→
     7→_STAGES = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
     8→PromptMode = Literal["self_record", "output_only"]
     9→
    10→_PREAMBLE_SELF_RECORD = """\
    11→You are a triage subagent with full codebase access and the desloppify CLI.
    12→Your job is to complete the **{stage}** stage of triage planning.
    13→
    14→Repo root: {repo_root}
    15→
    16→## Standards
    17→
    18→You are expected to produce **exceptional** work. The output of this triage becomes the
    19→actual plan that an executor follows — if you are lazy, vague, or sloppy, real work gets
    20→wasted. Concretely:
    21→
    22→- **Read the actual source code.** Every opinion you form must come from reading the file,
    23→  not from reading the issue title. Issues frequently exaggerate, miscount, or describe
    24→  code that has already been fixed. Trust nothing until you verify it.
    25→- **Have specific opinions.** "This seems like it could be an issue" is worthless. "This is
    26→  a false positive because line 47 already uses the pattern the issue suggests" is useful.
    27→- **Do the hard thinking.** If two issues seem related, figure out WHY. If something should
    28→  be skipped, explain the specific reason for THIS issue, not a generic category.
    29→- **Don't take shortcuts.** Reading 5 files and extrapolating to 30 is lazy. Read all 30.
    30→  If you have too many, use subagents to parallelize — don't skip.
    31→- The prompt below already contains the authoritative stage contract and prior reports.
    32→  Do NOT search old triage run artifacts for alternate instructions unless you hit a
    33→  concrete mismatch you need to explain.
    34→
    35→Use the desloppify CLI to record your work. Every command you run mutates plan.json directly.
    36→The orchestrator will review your work and confirm the stage after you record it.
    37→Use the exact CLI command prefix shown in the CLI Command Reference: `{cli_command}`.
    38→Do NOT debug, repair, reinstall, or inspect the CLI/environment. If the command fails, stop
    39→and explain the failure in your stdout summary.
    40→
    41→After you finish recording the stage, write a short plain-text summary to stdout describing
    42→what you changed, which commands you ran, and any blockers you hit. The runner requires
    43→non-empty stdout output.
    44→
    45→**CRITICAL: Only run commands for YOUR stage ({stage}).** Do NOT re-run earlier stages
    46→(e.g., do not run `--stage observe` if you are the organize subagent). Earlier stages
    47→are already confirmed. Re-running them will corrupt the plan state.
    48→"""
    49→
    50→_PREAMBLE_OUTPUT_ONLY = """\
    51→You are a triage analysis subagent with full codebase access.
    52→Your job is to complete the **{stage}** stage of triage planning.
    53→
    54→Repo root: {repo_root}
    55→
    56→## Standards
    57→
    58→You are expected to produce **exceptional** work. The output of this triage becomes the
    59→actual plan that an executor follows — if you are lazy, vague, or sloppy, real work gets
    60→wasted. Concretely:
    61→
    62→- **Read the actual source code.** Every opinion you form must come from reading the file,
    63→  not from reading the issue title. Issues frequently exaggerate, miscount, or describe
    64→  code that has already been fixed. Trust nothing until you verify it.
    65→- **Have specific opinions.** "This seems like it could be an issue" is worthless. "This is
    66→  a false positive because line 47 already uses the pattern the issue suggests" is useful.
    67→- **Do the hard thinking.** If two issues seem related, figure out WHY. If something should
    68→  be skipped, explain the specific reason for THIS issue, not a generic category.
    69→- **Don't take shortcuts.** Reading 5 files and extrapolating to 30 is lazy. Read all 30.
    70→  If you have too many, use subagents to parallelize — don't skip.
    71→- The prompt below already contains the authoritative stage contract and prior reports.
    72→  Do NOT search old triage run artifacts for alternate instructions unless you hit a
    73→  concrete mismatch you need to explain.
    74→
    75→## Output Contract
    76→
    77→- **Do NOT run any `desloppify` commands.**
    78→- **Do NOT debug, repair, reinstall, or inspect the `desloppify` CLI/environment.**
    79→- **Do NOT mutate `plan.json` directly or indirectly.**
    80→- Use shell/read-only repo inspection as needed, but your only deliverable is a plain-text
    81→  stage report for the orchestrator to record and confirm.
    82→- If the prompt mentions CLI commands, treat them as background context for the orchestrator,
    83→  not instructions for you to execute.
    84→"""
    85→
    86→_CLI_REFERENCE_TEMPLATE = """\
    87→## CLI Command Reference
    88→
    89→### Stage recording
    90→```
    91→{cli_command} plan triage --stage observe --report "<analysis>"
    92→{cli_command} plan triage --stage reflect --report "<strategy>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    93→{cli_command} plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    94→{cli_command} plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    95→{cli_command} plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<verification summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    96→```
    97→
    98→### Cluster management
    99→```
   100→{cli_command} plan cluster create <name> --description "<what this cluster addresses>"
   101→{cli_command} plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns...>
   102→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --description "<desc>" --steps "step 1" "step 2"
   103→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --add-step "<title>" --detail "<sub-points>" --effort small --issue-refs <id1> <id2>
   104→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "<sub-points>" --effort medium --issue-refs <id1>
   105→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other-cluster-name>
   106→{cli_command} plan cluster show <name>
   107→{cli_command} plan cluster list --verbose
   108→```
   109→
   110→### Skip/dismiss
   111→```
   112→{cli_command} plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."
   113→```
   114→**Important:** The --attest value MUST contain the words "reviewed" and "not gaming" (exact phrases required by validation).
   115→
   116→### Effort tags
   117→Valid values: trivial, small, medium, large. Set on steps via --effort flag.
   118→"""
   119→
   120→def triage_prompt_preamble(mode: PromptMode) -> str:
   121→    """Return the shared prompt preamble for the requested runner mode."""
   122→    if mode == "output_only":
   123→        return _PREAMBLE_OUTPUT_ONLY
   124→    return _PREAMBLE_SELF_RECORD
   125→
   126→
   127→def render_cli_reference(cli_command: str = "desloppify") -> str:
   128→    """Render the CLI reference with the exact command prefix to execute."""
   129→    return _CLI_REFERENCE_TEMPLATE.format(cli_command=cli_command)
   130→
   131→

   135→    "_STAGES",
   136→    "render_cli_reference",
   137→    "triage_prompt_preamble",

   268→
   269→
   270→def _enrich_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   271→    subagent_block = """\
   272→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   273→
   274→1. Run `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to get current steps and issue list
   275→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   276→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   277→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   278→   function name), and the exact change to make
   279→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   280→"""
   281→    tail = """\
   282→When done, run:
   283→```
   284→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   285→```
   286→"""
   287→    if mode == "output_only":
   288→        subagent_block = """\
   289→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   290→
   291→1. Inspect the cluster definition provided in the prompt context
   292→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   293→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   294→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   295→   function name), and the exact change to make
   296→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   297→"""
   298→        tail = """\
   299→When done, write a plain-text enrichment report describing the corrected step details,
   300→effort tags, and issue refs for each cluster. The orchestrator records the stage.
   301→"""
   302→    return f"""\
   303→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   304→
   305→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   306→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   307→
   308→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   309→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   310→Use the organize report included above as the authoritative cluster plan; do not re-derive
   311→strategy from old triage runs unless you find a concrete mismatch you need to explain.
   312→
   313→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   314→
   315→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   316→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   317→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   318→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)
   319→5. No step may reference a skipped/wontfixed issue in its issue_refs
   320→
   321→### How to enrich
   322→
   323→{subagent_block}
   324→
   325→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   326→
   327→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   328→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   329→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   330→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   331→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   332→
   333→**Vague action verbs.** "Refactor", "clean up", "improve", "fix" are not actions.
   334→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   335→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   336→
   337→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   338→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   339→
   340→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   341→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   342→
   343→### Examples
   344→
   345→**GOOD step detail:**
   346→```
   347→--detail "In src/shared/hooks/billing/useAutoTopup.ts lines 118-129, add onMutate handler
   348→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore
   349→previous state and change showToast from false to true."

    82→        '    "likely_symptoms": ["optional: identifiers that look symptom-level"],\n'
    83→        '    "possible_false_positives": ["optional: prior concept keys likely mis-scoped"]\n'
    84→        "  }\n"
    85→        "}\n"
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _extract_dimension_prompts(batch: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    90→    """Extract dimension prompts embedded by explode_to_single_dimension."""
    91→    prompt = batch.get("_dimension_prompt")
    92→    if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
    93→        return {}
    94→    dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
    95→    if isinstance(dims, list) and len(dims) == 1:
    96→        return {str(dims[0]): prompt}
    97→    return {}
    98→
    99→
   100→def render_batch_prompt(
   101→    *,
   102→    repo_root: Path,
   103→    packet_path: Path,
   104→    batch_index: int,
   105→    batch: dict[str, object],
   106→    policy_block: str = "",
   107→) -> str:
   108→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   109→    context = build_batch_context(batch, batch_index)
   110→    dim_prompts = _extract_dimension_prompts(batch)
   111→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   112→        _render_metadata_block(
   113→            repo_root=repo_root,
   114→            packet_path=packet_path,
   115→            batch_index=batch_index,
   116→            context=context,
   117→        ),
   118→        render_dimension_prompts_block(context.dimensions, dim_prompts),
   119→        policy_block,
   120→        render_scoring_frame(),
   121→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   122→        render_seed_files_block(context),
   123→        render_historical_focus(batch),
   124→        render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   125→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=context.issues_cap, dim_set=context.dimension_set),
   126→        render_scope_enums(),
   127→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   128→    )
   129→
   130→
   131→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]
   132→

   148→    )

   152→    if question:

   165→def render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:

   167→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")

   171→    lines: list[str] = []

   233→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "

   237→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "

   246→        render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   247→        + render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   248→        + render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   249→        + render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   250→    )

   252→
   253→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   254→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   255→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   256→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   257→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   258→
   259→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   260→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension.  When *dimension_prompts*
   261→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a ``_dimension_prompt`` key with the
   262→    prompt for its single dimension so that downstream renderers can use it
   263→    without extra parameter threading.
   264→    """
   265→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   266→    result: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   267→    for batch in batches:
   268→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   269→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   270→            result.append(batch)
   271→            continue
   272→        for dim in dims:
   273→            exploded: dict[str, object] = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   274→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   275→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   276→                exploded["_dimension_prompt"] = dim_prompt
   277→            result.append(exploded)
   278→    return result
   279→
   280→
   281→def render_dimension_prompts_block(
   282→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],
   283→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   284→) -> str:
   285→    """Render inline dimension guidance so the reviewer sees the full rubric."""
   286→    if not dimensions or not dimension_prompts:
   287→        return ""
   288→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   289→    for dim in dimensions:
   290→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   291→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   292→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   293→            continue
   294→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   295→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   296→        if description:
   297→            lines.append(description)
   298→
   299→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   300→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   301→            lines.append("Look for:")
   302→            for item in look_for:
   303→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   304→
   305→        skip = prompt.get("skip")
   306→        if isinstance(skip, list) and skip:
   307→            lines.append("Skip:")
   308→            for item in skip:
   309→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   310→        lines.append("")
   311→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   312→
   313→
   314→def render_scoring_frame() -> str:
   315→    return (
   316→        "YOUR TASK: Read the code for this batch's dimension. Judge "
   317→        "how well the codebase serves a developer from that perspective. The dimension "
   318→        "rubric above defines what good looks like. "
   319→        "Cite specific observations that explain your judgment.\n\n"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def render_scan_evidence_note() -> str:
   324→    return (
   325→        "Mechanical scan evidence — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\n"
   326→        "The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` with aggregated signals "
   327→        "from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, error hotspots, signal "
   328→        "density index, boundary violations, and systemic patterns. Use these as starting "
   329→        "points for where to look beyond the seed files.\n\n"
   330→    )
   331→
   332→
   333→def render_seed_files_block(context: PromptBatchContext) -> str:
   334→    return f"Seed files (start here):\n{context.seed_files_text}\n\n"

    72→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    73→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    74→        return None
    75→    try:
    76→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    77→    except ValueError:
    78→        return None
    79→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    80→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)

    95→
    96→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    97→    if not session_id.strip():
    98→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-id is required.", exit_code=2)
    99→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
   100→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
   101→        raise CommandError("Error: invalid --session-id value.", exit_code=2)

   104→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   105→    if not path.exists():
   106→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
   107→    try:
   108→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   109→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   110→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
   111→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   112→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
   113→    return payload
   114→
   115→
   116→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   117→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   118→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   119→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   120→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   121→    if payload_id != session_id:
   122→        raise CommandError(
   123→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   124→        )
   125→    return path, payload
   126→
   127→
   128→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   129→    args,
   130→    state: dict,
   131→    lang,
   132→    *,
   133→    config: dict[str, Any],
   134→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path]:
   135→    """Prepare holistic review packet and persist immutable+blind snapshots."""
   136→    context = resolve_review_packet_context(args)
   137→    next_command = build_external_submit_next_command(context)
   138→    try:
   139→        packet = build_review_packet_payload(
   140→            state=state,
   141→            lang=lang,
   142→            config=config,
   143→            context=context,
   144→            next_command=next_command,
   145→            setup_lang_fn=setup_lang_concrete,
   146→        )
   147→    except ValueError as exc:
   148→        msg = str(exc).strip()
   149→        if not msg:
   150→            msg = f"no files found at path '{context.path}'. Nothing to review."

   192→    """Build a copy/paste-ready prompt for a Claude blind reviewer subagent."""
   193→    header = (
   194→        "# Claude Blind Reviewer Launch Prompt\n\n"
   195→        "You are an isolated blind reviewer. Do not use prior chat context, "
   196→        "prior score history, or target-score anchoring.\n\n"
