   202→    )
   203→
   204→    raw_batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   205→    if not isinstance(raw_batches, list):
   206→        raw_batches = []
   207→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   208→    dim_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = (
   209→        raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else {}
   210→    )
   211→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   212→        [b for b in raw_batches if isinstance(b, dict)],
   213→        dimension_prompts=dim_prompts or None,
   214→    )
   215→
   216→    all_dims: set[str] = set()
   217→    combined_cap = 0
   218→    batch_sections: list[str] = []
   219→    for i, batch in enumerate(batches):
   220→        ctx = build_batch_context(batch, i)
   221→        all_dims.update(ctx.dimension_set)
   222→        combined_cap += ctx.issues_cap
   223→
   224→        section = (
   225→            f"--- Batch {i + 1}: {ctx.name} ---\n"
   226→            f"Rationale: {ctx.rationale}\n"
   227→        )
   228→        section += render_dimension_prompts_block(ctx.dimensions, dim_prompts)
   229→        section += render_seed_files_block(ctx)
   230→        section += render_historical_focus(batch)
   231→        section += render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch)
   232→        batch_sections.append(section)
   233→
   234→    if not combined_cap:
   235→        combined_cap = 10
   236→
   237→    output_schema = (
   238→        "Output schema:\n"
   239→        "{\n"
   240→        '  "session": {"id": "<preserve from template>", "token": "<preserve from template>"},\n'
   241→        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100 with one decimal place>},\n'

   258→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
   259→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
   260→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name"\n'
   261→        "  }]\n"
   262→        "}\n\n"
   263→    )
   264→
   265→    session_requirements = (
   266→        "Session requirements:\n"
   267→        f"1. Keep `session.id` exactly `{session_id}`.\n"
   268→        f"2. Keep `session.token` exactly `{token}`.\n"
   269→        "3. Do not include provenance metadata (CLI injects canonical provenance).\n"
   270→    )
   271→
   272→    from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_policy_result, render_policy_block
   273→
   274→    policy_result = load_policy_result()
   275→    policy_text = render_policy_block(policy_result.policy)
   276→    if not policy_result.ok:
   277→        print(

   280→                "yellow",
   281→            )
   282→        )
   283→
   284→    return join_non_empty_sections(

   286→        *batch_sections,

   288→        render_scoring_frame(),
   289→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   290→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=combined_cap, dim_set=all_dims),
   291→        render_scope_enums(),
   292→        output_schema,
   293→        session_requirements,

## Summary: Tool Pattern Analysis for Triage vs Review

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's how desloppify provides tools to LLM agents and why the current review approach is fundamentally different from triage:

### **Triage Flow: Static Prompts + Subprocess Execution**

The triage pipeline uses a **"prompt + subprocess + record" pattern**:

1. **Prompt Structure** (`stage_prompts_instruction_*.py`):
   - `_PREAMBLE_SELF_RECORD`: Tells the LLM it has "full codebase access and the desloppify CLI"
   - `_CLI_REFERENCE_TEMPLATE`: Includes exact CLI commands the agent can execute
   - Stage-specific instructions (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check)

2. **How Tools Are "Provided"** (from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts_instruction_shared.py`):
   ```
   Use the desloppify CLI to record your work. Every command you run mutates plan.json directly.
   The orchestrator will review your work and confirm the stage after you record it.
   Use the exact CLI command prefix shown in the CLI Command Reference: `{cli_command}`.
   ```
   
   Available commands embedded in prompt text:
   - `desloppify plan cluster create <name>`
   - `desloppify plan cluster add <name>`
   - `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step/--update-step`
   - `desloppify plan skip --permanent`
   - `desloppify plan triage --stage <name> --report "..."`

3. **Execution Model** (`codex_runner.py`, `orchestrator_codex_sense.py`):
   - Calls `codex exec` subprocess with the prompt
   - Passes `--ephemeral -C {repo_root} -s workspace-write`
   - Agent runs commands directly; stdout becomes the output
   - Orchestrator parses stdout for validation

4. **Two Prompt Modes**:
   - **`self_record`**: Agent has CLI access; it runs commands and mutates plan.json
   - **`output_only`**: Agent is read-only; returns plain-text report that orchestrator records

### **Review Flow: Static Context + JSON Schema**

The review pipeline is fundamentally **"blind packet + static context + JSON output"**:

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/prompt_template.py`:

```python
def _render_output_schema(context):
    return (
        "Output schema:\n"
        "{\n"
        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100>},\n'
        '  "dimension_notes": {...},\n'
        '  "issues": [{...}],\n'
        '  "retrospective": {...}\n'
        "}\n"
    )
```

1. **What the Reviewer Gets** (from `prompt_sections.py`):
   - Seed files list (starting points)
   - Mechanical concern signals (navigation aid)
   - Scan evidence (hotspots, complexity signals)
   - Historical issues for context
   - Dimension rubrics (inline scoring guidance)
   - **No tools, no CLI, no ability to mutate state**

2. **Review Instruction Pattern** (from `render_task_requirements`):
   ```python
   "2. Start from the seed files, then freely explore the repository to build your understanding.",
   "...complete `dimension_judgment` for your dimension...",
   "...Do not edit repository files.",
   "...Return ONLY valid JSON..."
   ```
   
   Notably: **"Do not edit repository files"** — the review subagent is explicitly read-only.

3. **External Review Mode** (Claude Launch):
   - Manual copy/paste of prompt into isolated Claude session
   - Reviewer has codebase access (can read files)
   - No CLI, no tools defined
   - Outputs JSON to a file path that orchestrator imports

### **Why Triage Has Tools, Review Doesn't**

**Triage's task requires mutation:**
- Create clusters, add issues, update steps, record decisions
- These are plan-building actions
- The CLI is the tool that persists state
- Multiple sequential stages need to coordinate (organize → enrich → sense-check)

**Review's task is analysis-only:**
- Assess codebase health against dimensions
- Identify defects and patterns
- Return structured findings
- No need to mutate anything
- Each batch is independent

### **The Pattern You Can Replicate for Interactive Review**

If you want reviewers to investigate interactively (like triage does), here's the pattern:

**Option A: Batch-scoped CLI tools** (like triage sense-check)
- Define reviewer-specific CLI commands: `desloppify review batch explore --issue-hash <h> --question "..."`
- Commands return analysis results that reviewer can cite in JSON output
- Similar to how sense-check calls `desloppify plan cluster show`

**Option B: Subprocess execution** (like triage self_record)
- Switch review to use `codex exec` subprocess runner (not API)
- Pass `workspace-read` capability (not write)
- Include readonly CLI tools in prompt reference
- Let reviewer run: `desloppify codebase explore --files-matching "pattern"` or similar
- Reviewer cites findings in structured JSON

**Option C: MCP/Tool-use protocol**
- Define review tools via function_calling (JSON schema for tool definitions)
- Tools: `read_file(path)`, `list_files(pattern)`, `search_codebase(query)`, `get_file_context(hash)`
- Review agent calls tools to gather evidence, then returns JSON
- Currently review doesn't use this—it assumes manual file reading

### **Key Files to Reference**

1. **Triage tool pattern**:
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts_instruction_shared.py` (lines 10-48: preamble)
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts_instruction_shared.py` (lines 86-118: CLI reference)

2. **Triage execution**:
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/runner/codex_batch.py` (codex subprocess builder)
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/codex_runner.py` (execution wrapper)

3. **Review prompting** (no tools currently):
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/prompt_template.py` (batch prompt builder)
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py` (seed files, concerns, task requirements)
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/external.py` (Claude Launch prompt builder, no tools)

### **Triage vs Review Comparison Table**

| Aspect | Triage | Review |
|--------|--------|--------|
| **Execution** | `codex exec` subprocess | Batch API or manual copy/paste |
| **Tools Available** | CLI commands (plan cluster, skip, etc.) | None (read codebase manually) |
| **State Mutation** | Yes (plan.json) | No (findings-only) |
| **Prompt Mode** | `self_record` (CLI aware) or `output_only` (report only) | Static context blob + JSON schema |
| **Task Model** | Sequential stages with coordination | Independent parallel batches |
| **Evidence Gathering** | Agent-driven (runs CLI commands, reads files) | Pre-gathered (seed files, scan evidence, signals) |
| **Output** | Plain-text report + mutated plan.json | Structured JSON only |

The key insight: **triage gives tools because it needs state mutation + multi-stage coordination. Review doesn't because it's stateless analysis.**

> DEVELOPER

Design a plan for giving the holistic review subagent the ability to explore and adjudicate judgment-required detector findings during the review process.

## Current State

### The problem
Judgment-required findings (from 21 detectors like `smells`, `structural`, `coupling`, `naming`, etc.) are currently presented to the reviewer as aggregated evidence blobs — e.g., "4 files with complexity hotspots." The reviewer sees this as navigation context but has no structured way to:
1. See the individual findings
2. Investigate each one
3. Record a verdict (promote to concern, dismiss, skip)

### How review currently works
- Review runs via `codex exec` with `workspace-write` permissions (`desloppify/app/commands/runner/codex_batch.py`)
- The reviewer gets a text prompt with: dimension rubrics, seed files, up to 8 mechanical concern signals, scan evidence (aggregated), historical issues
- Output is JSON: assessments, dimension_notes, dimension_judgment, issues[], retrospective
- The `issues[]` array is where the reviewer reports defects — each with dimension, summary, related_files, evidence, suggestion, confidence
- When an issue has `concern_verdict: "confirmed"`, it becomes a `concerns::` issue (detector="concerns", needs_judgment=False) which auto-clusters and flows to triage
- When `concern_verdict: "dismissed"`, a fingerprinted dismissal is stored in `state["concern_dismissals"]`

### How triage gives tools (the pattern to follow)
Triage runs via `codex exec` with `workspace-write`. The prompt includes:
- A preamble: "You are a triage subagent with full codebase access and the desloppify CLI"  
- A CLI Command Reference section listing exact commands to run
- Commands like `desloppify plan cluster create/add/show`, `desloppify plan skip --permanent`
- The agent runs these commands, which mutate `plan.json` directly
- File: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts_instruction_shared.py`

### What data exists for judgment findings
Each finding in `state["issues"]` has:
- Stable ID (e.g., `structural::src/big_file.py`)
- `detector`, `file`, `tier`, `confidence`, `summary`
- `detail` dict with detector-specific payload (loc, complexity_score, complexity_signals, smell_id, etc.)
- `status`, `zone`, `note`, `reopen_count`

The concern generator system (`desloppify/engine/_concerns/generators.py`) already:
- Groups judgment findings by file
- Builds `Concern` objects with type, file, summary, evidence, question, fingerprint, source_issues
- Checks dismissals before creating concerns
- Has dismissal fingerprinting and cleanup of stale dismissals

### Existing suppression/dismissal mechanisms
1. `suppressed` flag on issues (via `desloppify suppress <pattern>`) — hides from scoring entirely
2. `status: "wontfix"` (via `desloppify resolve <pattern> --status wontfix`) — keeps in scoring, out of queue
3. `concern_dismissals` in state — fingerprinted, prevents re-surfacing of dismissed concerns
4. Triage `plan skip --permanent` — plan-scoped only

## Requirements

1. **Individual findings should be explorable**: The reviewer should be able to list all judgment-required findings, filter by detector, and see individual details
2. **Verdicts per finding**: The reviewer should be able to mark individual findings as:
   - **Confirm** → becomes a `concerns::` issue (existing flow)
   - **Dismiss** → permanently suppressed (survives re-scan as long as the issue exists)
   - **Skip** → no action this review pass (can be re-evaluated next time)
3. **Use existing tools**: The reviewer already has codebase access via codex exec. We should give them CLI commands to query findings, similar to how triage gets cluster commands.
4. **Dismissals should persist**: When a finding is dismissed during review, it should not keep resurfacing in future reviews. The existing `concern_dismissals` mechanism handles this for generated concerns — we need something similar for raw findings.
5. **Existing flows unchanged**: The current concern generation, scoring, and review import pipelines should continue working.

## Design Constraints

- The reviewer agent already runs via `codex exec` with full codebase + CLI access
- The prompt is built in `desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/prompt_template.py` and `prompt_sections.py`
- Output JSON schema is in `prompt_template.py::_render_output_schema`
- Review results are imported via `desloppify/intelligence/review/importing/holistic_issue_flow.py`
- The concern generator (`_concerns/generators.py`) already has fingerprinting and dismissal checking

## Key questions to address in the plan

1. Should we add new CLI commands for the reviewer to query findings? (e.g., `desloppify show structural --json`) — or does `desloppify show <detector>` already work?
2. How should dismissals work? Options:
   a. Use the existing `desloppify resolve <id> --status wontfix` 
   b. Use `desloppify suppress` 
   c. Add a new `review-dismiss` mechanism that ties into the concern_dismissals system
   d. Extend the review output JSON to include per-finding verdicts
3. How should the prompt be extended to tell the reviewer about these tools?
4. Should findings be presented as a checklist in the prompt, or should the reviewer query them on-demand via CLI?

Please design the simplest approach that achieves the goals. Prefer reusing existing mechanisms over building new ones.

    58→        '      "sub_axes": {"abstraction_leverage": 0-100, "indirection_cost": 0-100, "interface_honesty": 0-100, "delegation_density": 0-100, "definition_directness": 0-100, "type_discipline": 0-100}  // required for abstraction_fitness when evidence supports it; all one decimal place\n'
    59→        "    }\n"
    60→        "  },\n"
    61→        '  "dimension_judgment": {\n'
    62→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
    63→        '      "strengths": ["0-5 specific things the codebase does well from this dimension\'s perspective"],\n'
    64→        '      "issue_character": "one sentence characterizing the nature/pattern of issues from this dimension\'s perspective",\n'
    65→        '      "score_rationale": "2-3 sentences explaining the score from this dimension\'s perspective, referencing global anchors"\n'
    66→        "    }  // required for every assessed dimension; do not omit\n"
    67→        "  },\n"
    68→        '  "issues": [{\n'
    69→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
    70→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
    71→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
    72→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
    73→        '    "evidence": ["specific code observation"],\n'
    74→        '    "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",\n'
    75→        '    "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
    76→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
    77→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    78→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name_when_supported_by_history"\n'
    79→        "  }],\n"
    80→        '  "retrospective": {\n'
    81→        '    "root_causes": ["optional: concise root-cause hypotheses"],\n'
    82→        '    "likely_symptoms": ["optional: identifiers that look symptom-level"],\n'
    83→        '    "possible_false_positives": ["optional: prior concept keys likely mis-scoped"]\n'
    84→        "  }\n"
    85→        "}\n"
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _extract_dimension_prompts(batch: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    90→    """Extract dimension prompts embedded by explode_to_single_dimension."""
    91→    prompt = batch.get("_dimension_prompt")
    92→    if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
    93→        return {}
    94→    dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
    95→    if isinstance(dims, list) and len(dims) == 1:
    96→        return {str(dims[0]): prompt}
    97→    return {}
    98→
    99→
   100→def render_batch_prompt(
   101→    *,
   102→    repo_root: Path,
   103→    packet_path: Path,
   104→    batch_index: int,
   105→    batch: dict[str, object],
   106→    policy_block: str = "",
   107→) -> str:
   108→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   109→    context = build_batch_context(batch, batch_index)
   110→    dim_prompts = _extract_dimension_prompts(batch)
   111→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   112→        _render_metadata_block(
   113→            repo_root=repo_root,
   114→            packet_path=packet_path,
   115→            batch_index=batch_index,
   116→            context=context,
   117→        ),
   118→        render_dimension_prompts_block(context.dimensions, dim_prompts),
   119→        policy_block,
   120→        render_scoring_frame(),
   121→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   122→        render_seed_files_block(context),
   123→        render_historical_focus(batch),
   124→        render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   125→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=context.issues_cap, dim_set=context.dimension_set),
   126→        render_scope_enums(),
   127→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   128→    )
   129→
   130→
   131→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]
   132→

1→"""Concern generators bridging mechanical findings to holistic review cues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.registry import JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    10→
    11→from .constants import MIN_FILES_FOR_SMELL_PATTERN, MIN_FILES_FOR_SYSTEMIC
    12→from .signals import _extract_signals, _has_elevated_signals
    13→from .state import _group_by_file, _open_issues
    14→from .text import _build_evidence, _build_question, _build_summary, _classify
    15→from .types import Concern
    16→from .utils import _fingerprint, _is_dismissed
    17→
    18→
    19→def _try_make_concern(
    20→    *,
    21→    concern_type: str,
    22→    file: str,
    23→    fp_keys: tuple[str, ...],
    24→    all_ids: tuple[str, ...],
    25→    dismissals: dict[str, Any],
    26→    summary: str,
    27→    evidence: tuple[str, ...],
    28→    question: str,
    29→    fp_file: str | None = None,
    30→) -> Concern | None:
    31→    """Create a concern unless a matching dismissal exists."""
    32→    fp = _fingerprint(concern_type, fp_file if fp_file is not None else file, fp_keys)
    33→    if _is_dismissed(dismissals, fp, all_ids):
    34→        return None
    35→    return Concern(
    36→        type=concern_type,
    37→        file=file,
    38→        summary=summary,
    39→        evidence=evidence,
    40→        question=question,
    41→        fingerprint=fp,
    42→        source_issues=all_ids,
    43→    )
    44→
    45→
    46→def _file_concerns(state: StateModel, dismissals: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Concern]:
    47→    """Build per-file concerns from aggregated judgment detector signals."""
    48→    by_file = _group_by_file(state)
    49→    concerns: list[Concern] = []
    50→
    51→    for file, all_issues in by_file.items():
    52→        judgment = [
    53→            finding
    54→            for finding in all_issues
    55→            if finding.get("detector", "") in JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
    56→        ]
    57→        if not judgment:
    58→            continue
    59→
    60→        judgment_dets = {finding.get("detector", "") for finding in judgment}
    61→        elevated = _has_elevated_signals(judgment)
    62→
    63→        mechanical_count = len(all_issues)
    64→        if len(judgment_dets) < 2 and not elevated:
    65→            if not (len(judgment_dets) >= 1 and mechanical_count >= 3):
    66→                continue
    67→
    68→        signals = _extract_signals(judgment)
    69→        concern_type = _classify(judgment_dets, signals)
    70→        all_ids = tuple(sorted(finding.get("id", "") for finding in judgment))
    71→        fp_keys = tuple(sorted(judgment_dets))
    72→
    73→        concern = _try_make_concern(
    74→            concern_type=concern_type,
    75→            file=file,
    76→            fp_keys=fp_keys,
    77→            all_ids=all_ids,
    78→            dismissals=dismissals,
    79→            summary=_build_summary(concern_type, judgment_dets, signals),
    80→            evidence=_build_evidence(judgment, signals),
    81→            question=_build_question(judgment_dets, signals),
    82→        )
    83→        if concern is not None:
    84→            concerns.append(concern)
    85→
    86→    return concerns
    87→
    88→
    89→def _cross_file_patterns(state: StateModel, dismissals: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Concern]:
    90→    """Build systemic concerns for detector-combo patterns across files."""
    91→    by_file = _group_by_file(state)
    92→
    93→    profile_to_files: dict[frozenset[str], list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    94→    for file, issues in by_file.items():
    95→        detectors = frozenset(
    96→            finding.get("detector", "")
    97→            for finding in issues
    98→            if finding.get("detector", "") in JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
    99→        )
   100→        if len(detectors) >= 2:
   101→            profile_to_files[detectors].append(file)
   102→
   103→    concerns: list[Concern] = []
   104→    for det_combo, files in profile_to_files.items():
   105→        if len(files) < MIN_FILES_FOR_SYSTEMIC:
   106→            continue
   107→
   108→        sorted_files = sorted(files)
   109→        combo_names = tuple(sorted(det_combo))
   110→        all_ids = tuple(
   111→            sorted(
   112→                finding.get("id", "")
   113→                for file in sorted_files
   114→                for finding in by_file[file]
   115→                if finding.get("detector", "") in det_combo
   116→            )
   117→        )
   118→        concern = _try_make_concern(
   119→            concern_type="systemic_pattern",
   120→            file=sorted_files[0],
   121→            fp_file=",".join(sorted_files[:5]),
   122→            fp_keys=combo_names,
   123→            all_ids=all_ids,
   124→            dismissals=dismissals,
   125→            summary=(
   126→                f"{len(files)} files share the same problem pattern "
   127→                f"({', '.join(combo_names)})"
   128→            ),
   129→            evidence=(
   130→                f"Affected files: {', '.join(sorted_files[:10])}",
   131→                f"Shared detectors: {', '.join(combo_names)}",
   132→                f"Total files: {len(files)}",
   133→            ),
   134→            question=(
   135→                f"These {len(files)} files all have the same combination "
   136→                f"of issues ({', '.join(combo_names)}). Is this a systemic "
   137→                "pattern that should be addressed at the architecture level "
   138→                "(shared base class, framework change, lint rule), or are "
   139→                "these independent issues that happen to look similar?"
   140→            ),
   141→        )
   142→        if concern is not None:
   143→            concerns.append(concern)
   144→
   145→    return concerns
   146→
   147→
   148→def _systemic_smell_patterns(
   149→    state: StateModel,
   150→    dismissals: dict[str, Any],
   151→) -> list[Concern]:
   152→    """Build systemic concerns when a single smell appears across many files."""
   153→    smell_files: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   154→    smell_ids_map: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   155→
   156→    for finding in _open_issues(state):
   157→        if finding.get("detector") != "smells":
   158→            continue
   159→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   160→        smell_id = detail.get("smell_id", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
   161→        filepath = finding.get("file", "")
   162→        if smell_id and filepath and filepath != ".":
   163→            smell_files[smell_id].append(filepath)
   164→            smell_ids_map[smell_id].append(finding.get("id", ""))
   165→
   166→    concerns: list[Concern] = []
   167→    for smell_id, files in smell_files.items():
   168→        unique_files = sorted(set(files))
   169→        if len(unique_files) < MIN_FILES_FOR_SMELL_PATTERN:
   170→            continue
   171→
   172→        all_ids = tuple(sorted(smell_ids_map[smell_id]))
   173→        concern = _try_make_concern(
   174→            concern_type="systemic_smell",
   175→            file=unique_files[0],
   176→            fp_file=smell_id,
   177→            fp_keys=(smell_id,),
   178→            all_ids=all_ids,
   179→            dismissals=dismissals,
   180→            summary=(
   181→                f"'{smell_id}' appears in {len(unique_files)} files — "
   182→                "likely a systemic pattern"
   183→            ),
   184→            evidence=(
   185→                f"Smell: {smell_id}",
   186→                f"Affected files ({len(unique_files)}): {', '.join(unique_files[:10])}",
   187→            ),
   188→            question=(
   189→                f"The smell '{smell_id}' appears across {len(unique_files)} files. "
   190→                "Is this a codebase-wide convention that should be addressed "
   191→                "systemically (lint rule, shared utility, architecture change), "
   192→                "or are these independent occurrences?"
   193→            ),
   194→        )
   195→        if concern is not None:
   196→            concerns.append(concern)
   197→
   198→    return concerns
   199→
   200→
   201→_GENERATORS = [_file_concerns, _cross_file_patterns, _systemic_smell_patterns]
   202→
   203→
   204→def generate_concerns(state: StateModel) -> list[Concern]:
   205→    """Run all concern generators against current state."""
   206→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
   207→    concerns: list[Concern] = []
   208→    seen_fingerprints: set[str] = set()
   209→
   210→    for generator in _GENERATORS:
   211→        for concern in generator(state, dismissals):
   212→            if concern.fingerprint not in seen_fingerprints:
   213→                seen_fingerprints.add(concern.fingerprint)
   214→                concerns.append(concern)
   215→
   216→    concerns.sort(key=lambda concern: (concern.type, concern.file))
   217→    return concerns
   218→
   219→
   220→def cleanup_stale_dismissals(state: StateModel) -> int:
   221→    """Remove concern dismissals whose source issues all disappeared."""
   222→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
   223→    if not dismissals:
   224→        return 0
   225→    open_ids = {finding.get("id", "") for finding in _open_issues(state)}
   226→    stale_fingerprints = [
   227→        fingerprint
   228→        for fingerprint, entry in dismissals.items()
   229→        if entry.get("source_issue_ids")
   230→        and not any(source_id in open_ids for source_id in entry["source_issue_ids"])
   231→    ]
   232→    for fingerprint in stale_fingerprints:
   233→        del dismissals[fingerprint]
   234→    return len(stale_fingerprints)
   235→
   236→
   237→__all__ = [
   238→    "_cross_file_patterns",
   239→    "_file_concerns",
   240→    "cleanup_stale_dismissals",
   241→    "generate_concerns",
   242→]
   243→

1→"""Shared prompt rendering sections used by both batch and external review paths."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
     8→    max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    13→class PromptBatchContext:
    14→    name: str
    15→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...]
    16→    rationale: str
    17→    seed_files: tuple[str, ...]
    18→    issues_cap: int
    19→
    20→    @property
    21→    def dimension_set(self) -> set[str]:
    22→        return set(self.dimensions)
    23→
    24→    @property
    25→    def dimensions_text(self) -> str:
    26→        return ", ".join(self.dimensions) if self.dimensions else "(none)"
    27→
    28→    @property
    29→    def seed_files_text(self) -> str:
    30→        return "\n".join(f"- {path}" for path in self.seed_files) if self.seed_files else "- (none)"
    31→
    32→
    33→def coerce_string_list(raw: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    34→    if not isinstance(raw, list | tuple):
    35→        return ()
    36→    return tuple(str(item) for item in raw if isinstance(item, str) and item)
    37→
    38→
    39→def build_batch_context(batch: dict[str, object], batch_index: int) -> PromptBatchContext:
    40→    dimensions = coerce_string_list(batch.get("dimensions", []))
    41→    return PromptBatchContext(
    42→        name=str(batch.get("name", f"Batch {batch_index + 1}")),
    43→        dimensions=dimensions,
    44→        rationale=str(batch.get("why", "")).strip(),
    45→        seed_files=coerce_string_list(batch.get("files_to_read", [])),
    46→        issues_cap=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dimensions)),
    47→    )
    48→
    49→
    50→SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION = {
    51→    "initialization_coupling": (
    52→        "9e. For initialization_coupling, use evidence from "
    53→        "`holistic_context.scan_evidence.mutable_globals` and "
    54→        "`holistic_context.errors.mutable_globals`. Investigate initialization ordering "
    55→        "dependencies, coupling through shared mutable state, and whether state should "
    56→        "be encapsulated behind a proper registry/context manager.\n"
    57→    ),
    58→    "design_coherence": (
    59→        "9f. For design_coherence, use evidence from "
    60→        "`holistic_context.scan_evidence.signal_density` — files where "
    61→        "multiple mechanical detectors fired. Investigate what design change would address "
    62→        "multiple signals simultaneously. Check `scan_evidence.complexity_hotspots` for "
    63→        "files with high responsibility cluster counts.\n"
    64→    ),
    65→    "error_consistency": (
    66→        "9g. For error_consistency, use evidence from "
    67→        "`holistic_context.errors.exception_hotspots` — files with "
    68→        "concentrated exception handling issues. Investigate whether error handling is "
    69→        "designed or accidental. Check for broad catches masking specific failure modes.\n"
    70→    ),
    71→    "cross_module_architecture": (
    72→        "9h. For cross_module_architecture, also consult "
    73→        "`holistic_context.coupling.boundary_violations` for import paths that "
    74→        "cross architectural boundaries, and `holistic_context.dependencies.deferred_import_density` "
    75→        "for files with many function-level imports (proxy for cycle pressure).\n"
    76→    ),
    77→    "convention_outlier": (
    78→        "9i. For convention_outlier, also consult "
    79→        "`holistic_context.conventions.duplicate_clusters` for cross-file "
    80→        "function duplication and `conventions.naming_drift` for directory-level naming "
    81→        "inconsistency.\n"
    82→    ),
    83→}
    84→
    85→
    86→def render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
    87→    """Render dimension-specific scan_evidence guidance."""
    88→    return "".join(
    89→        text
    90→        for dim, text in SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION.items()
    91→        if dim in dim_set
    92→    )
    93→
    94→
    95→def render_historical_focus(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
    96→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
    97→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
    98→        return ""
    99→
   100→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
   101→    try:
   102→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
   103→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   104→        selected_count = 0
   105→
   106→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])
   107→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   108→        issues = []
   109→
   110→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
   111→        return ""
   112→
   113→    lines: list[str] = []
   114→    lines.append(
   115→        "Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:"
   116→    )
   117→    lines.append(
   118→        "Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report"
   119→        " issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or"
   120→        " what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out."
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    for entry in issues:
   124→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   125→            continue
   126→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   127→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   128→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
   129→
   130→        line = f"  - [{status}] {summary}"
   131→        if note:
   132→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   133→        lines.append(line)
   134→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   135→
   136→
   137→def _concern_signal_lines(entry: dict[str, object]) -> list[str]:
   138→    """Render one concern signal entry into prompt lines."""
   139→    file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown file)"
   140→    concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   141→    summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   142→    question = str(entry.get("question", "")).strip()
   143→    evidence_raw = entry.get("evidence", [])
   144→    evidence = (
   145→        [str(item).strip() for item in evidence_raw if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
   146→        if isinstance(evidence_raw, list)
   147→        else []
   148→    )
   149→    lines = [f"  - [{concern_type}] {file}"]
   150→    if summary:
   151→        lines.append(f"    summary: {summary}")
   152→    if question:
   153→        lines.append(f"    question: {question}")
   154→    lines.extend(f"    evidence: {snippet}" for snippet in evidence[:2])
   155→    return lines
   156→
   157→
   158→def _iter_valid_concern_signals(
   159→    signals: list[object],
   160→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   161→    """Filter signal entries to mapping payloads only."""
   162→    return [entry for entry in signals if isinstance(entry, dict)]
   163→
   164→
   165→def render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
   166→    """Render mechanically-generated concern hypotheses for this batch."""
   167→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")
   168→    if not isinstance(signals, list) or not signals:
   169→        return ""
   170→
   171→    lines: list[str] = []
   172→    lines.append("Mechanical concern signals — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:")
   173→    lines.append(
   174→        "Confirm or refute each with your own code reading. Report only confirmed defects."
   175→    )
   176→
   177→    valid_signals = _iter_valid_concern_signals(signals)
   178→    capped_signals = valid_signals[:8]
   179→    for entry in capped_signals:
   180→        lines.extend(_concern_signal_lines(entry))
   181→
   182→    extra = max(0, len(valid_signals) - len(capped_signals))
   183→    if extra:
   184→        lines.append(f"  - (+{extra} more concern signals)")
   185→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   186→
   187→
   188→def render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   189→    """Render workflow integrity checks for architecture/integration dimensions."""
   190→    if not dim_set.intersection(
   191→        {
   192→            "cross_module_architecture",
   193→            "high_level_elegance",
   194→            "mid_level_elegance",
   195→            "design_coherence",
   196→            "initialization_coupling",
   197→        }
   198→    ):
   199→        return ""
   200→    return (
   201→        "9j. Workflow integrity checks: when reviewing orchestration/queue/review flows,\n"
   202→        "    explicitly look for loop-prone patterns and blind spots:\n"
   203→        "    - repeated stale/reopen churn without clear exit criteria or gating,\n"
   204→        "    - packet/batch data being generated but dropped before prompt execution,\n"
   205→        "    - ranking/triage logic that can starve target-improving work,\n"
   206→        "    - reruns happening before existing open review work is drained.\n"
   207→        "    If found, propose concrete guardrails and where to implement them.\n"
   208→    )
   209→
   210→
   211→def render_package_org_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   212→    if "package_organization" not in dim_set:
   213→        return ""
   214→    return (
   215→        "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   216→        "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   217→        "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   218→        "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   219→        "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   220→        "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   221→        "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_issues` for directories "
   222→        "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   223→    )
   224→
   225→
   226→def render_abstraction_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   227→    if "abstraction_fitness" not in dim_set:
   228→        return ""
   229→    return (
   230→        "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   231→        "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   232→        "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   233→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   234→        "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   235→        "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   236→        "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   237→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   238→        "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   239→        "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   240→        "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   241→    )
   242→
   243→
   244→def render_dimension_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   245→    return (
   246→        render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   247→        + render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   248→        + render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   249→        + render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   250→    )
   251→
   252→
   253→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   254→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   255→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   256→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   257→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   258→
   259→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   260→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension.  When *dimension_prompts*
   261→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a ``_dimension_prompt`` key with the
   262→    prompt for its single dimension so that downstream renderers can use it
   263→    without extra parameter threading.
   264→    """
   265→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   266→    result: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   267→    for batch in batches:
   268→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   269→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   270→            result.append(batch)
   271→            continue
   272→        for dim in dims:
   273→            exploded: dict[str, object] = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   274→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   275→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   276→                exploded["_dimension_prompt"] = dim_prompt
   277→            result.append(exploded)
   278→    return result
   279→
   280→
   281→def render_dimension_prompts_block(
   282→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],
   283→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   284→) -> str:
   285→    """Render inline dimension guidance so the reviewer sees the full rubric."""
   286→    if not dimensions or not dimension_prompts:
   287→        return ""
   288→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   289→    for dim in dimensions:
   290→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   291→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   292→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   293→            continue
   294→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   295→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   296→        if description:
   297→            lines.append(description)
   298→
   299→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   300→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   301→            lines.append("Look for:")
   302→            for item in look_for:
   303→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   304→
   305→        skip = prompt.get("skip")
   306→        if isinstance(skip, list) and skip:
   307→            lines.append("Skip:")
   308→            for item in skip:
   309→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   310→        lines.append("")
   311→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   312→
   313→
   314→def render_scoring_frame() -> str:
   315→    return (
   316→        "YOUR TASK: Read the code for this batch's dimension. Judge "
   317→        "how well the codebase serves a developer from that perspective. The dimension "
   318→        "rubric above defines what good looks like. "
   319→        "Cite specific observations that explain your judgment.\n\n"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def render_scan_evidence_note() -> str:
   324→    return (
   325→        "Mechanical scan evidence — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\n"
   326→        "The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` with aggregated signals "
   327→        "from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, error hotspots, signal "
   328→        "density index, boundary violations, and systemic patterns. Use these as starting "
   329→        "points for where to look beyond the seed files.\n\n"
   330→    )
   331→
   332→
   333→def render_seed_files_block(context: PromptBatchContext) -> str:
   334→    return f"Seed files (start here):\n{context.seed_files_text}\n\n"
   335→
   336→
   337→def render_task_requirements(*, issues_cap: int, dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   338→    dim_focus = render_dimension_focus(dim_set)
   339→    # Build numbered items; dimension focus items get renumbered dynamically.
   340→    lines = [
   341→        "Task requirements:",
   342→        "1. Read the blind packet's `system_prompt` — it contains scoring rules and calibration.",
   343→        "2. Start from the seed files, then freely explore the repository to build your understanding.",
   344→        "3. Keep issues and scoring scoped to this batch's dimension.",
   345→        "4. Respect scope controls: do not include files/directories marked by `exclude`, `suppress`, or non-production zone overrides.",
   346→        f"5. Return 0-{issues_cap} issues for this batch (empty array allowed).",
   347→    ]
   348→    next_num = 6
   349→    if dim_focus:
   350→        for focus_line in dim_focus.rstrip("\n").split("\n"):
   351→            lines.append(f"{next_num}. {focus_line.lstrip('0123456789abcdefghij. ')}")
   352→            next_num += 1
   353→    lines.append(
   354→        f"{next_num}. Complete `dimension_judgment` for your dimension — all three fields "
   355→        "(strengths, issue_character, score_rationale) are required. Write the judgment BEFORE setting the score."
   356→    )
   357→    next_num += 1
   358→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Do not edit repository files.")
   359→    next_num += 1
   360→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.")
   361→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   362→
   363→
   364→def render_scope_enums() -> str:
   365→    return (
   366→        "Scope enums:\n"
   367→        '- impact_scope: "local" | "module" | "subsystem" | "codebase"\n'
   368→        '- fix_scope: "single_edit" | "multi_file_refactor" | "architectural_change"\n\n'
   369→    )
   370→
   371→
   372→def join_non_empty_sections(*sections: str) -> str:
   373→    return "".join(section for section in sections if section)
   374→
   375→
   376→__all__ = [
   377→    "PromptBatchContext",
   378→    "coerce_string_list",
   379→    "build_batch_context",
   380→    "explode_to_single_dimension",
   381→    "render_dimension_prompts_block",
   382→    "SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION",
   383→    "render_scan_evidence_focus",
   384→    "render_historical_focus",
   385→    "render_mechanical_concern_signals",
   386→    "render_workflow_integrity_focus",

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from typing import Literal

     9→
    10→_PREAMBLE_SELF_RECORD = """\
    11→You are a triage subagent with full codebase access and the desloppify CLI.
    12→Your job is to complete the **{stage}** stage of triage planning.
    13→
    14→Repo root: {repo_root}
    15→
    16→## Standards
    17→
    18→You are expected to produce **exceptional** work. The output of this triage becomes the
    19→actual plan that an executor follows — if you are lazy, vague, or sloppy, real work gets
    20→wasted. Concretely:
    21→
    22→- **Read the actual source code.** Every opinion you form must come from reading the file,
    23→  not from reading the issue title. Issues frequently exaggerate, miscount, or describe
    24→  code that has already been fixed. Trust nothing until you verify it.
    25→- **Have specific opinions.** "This seems like it could be an issue" is worthless. "This is
    26→  a false positive because line 47 already uses the pattern the issue suggests" is useful.
    27→- **Do the hard thinking.** If two issues seem related, figure out WHY. If something should
    28→  be skipped, explain the specific reason for THIS issue, not a generic category.
    29→- **Don't take shortcuts.** Reading 5 files and extrapolating to 30 is lazy. Read all 30.
    30→  If you have too many, use subagents to parallelize — don't skip.
    31→- The prompt below already contains the authoritative stage contract and prior reports.
    32→  Do NOT search old triage run artifacts for alternate instructions unless you hit a
    33→  concrete mismatch you need to explain.
    34→
    35→Use the desloppify CLI to record your work. Every command you run mutates plan.json directly.
    36→The orchestrator will review your work and confirm the stage after you record it.
    37→Use the exact CLI command prefix shown in the CLI Command Reference: `{cli_command}`.
    38→Do NOT debug, repair, reinstall, or inspect the CLI/environment. If the command fails, stop
    39→and explain the failure in your stdout summary.
    40→
    41→After you finish recording the stage, write a short plain-text summary to stdout describing
    42→what you changed, which commands you ran, and any blockers you hit. The runner requires
    43→non-empty stdout output.
    44→
    45→**CRITICAL: Only run commands for YOUR stage ({stage}).** Do NOT re-run earlier stages
    46→(e.g., do not run `--stage observe` if you are the organize subagent). Earlier stages
    47→are already confirmed. Re-running them will corrupt the plan state.
    48→"""
    49→
    50→_PREAMBLE_OUTPUT_ONLY = """\

    56→## Standards

    71→- The prompt below already contains the authoritative stage contract and prior reports.

    74→

    76→
    77→- **Do NOT run any `desloppify` commands.**
    78→- **Do NOT debug, repair, reinstall, or inspect the `desloppify` CLI/environment.**

    82→- If the prompt mentions CLI commands, treat them as background context for the orchestrator,
    83→  not instructions for you to execute.
    84→"""
    85→
    86→_CLI_REFERENCE_TEMPLATE = """\
    87→## CLI Command Reference
    88→
    89→### Stage recording
    90→```
    91→{cli_command} plan triage --stage observe --report "<analysis>"
    92→{cli_command} plan triage --stage reflect --report "<strategy>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    93→{cli_command} plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    94→{cli_command} plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    95→{cli_command} plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<verification summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    96→```
    97→
    98→### Cluster management
    99→```
   100→{cli_command} plan cluster create <name> --description "<what this cluster addresses>"
   101→{cli_command} plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns...>
   102→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --description "<desc>" --steps "step 1" "step 2"
   103→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --add-step "<title>" --detail "<sub-points>" --effort small --issue-refs <id1> <id2>
   104→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "<sub-points>" --effort medium --issue-refs <id1>
   105→{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other-cluster-name>
   106→{cli_command} plan cluster show <name>
   107→{cli_command} plan cluster list --verbose
   108→```
   109→
   110→### Skip/dismiss
   111→```
   112→{cli_command} plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."
   113→```
   114→**Important:** The --attest value MUST contain the words "reviewed" and "not gaming" (exact phrases required by validation).
   115→
   116→### Effort tags
   117→Valid values: trivial, small, medium, large. Set on steps via --effort flag.
   118→"""
   119→
   120→def triage_prompt_preamble(mode: PromptMode) -> str:
   121→    """Return the shared prompt preamble for the requested runner mode."""
   122→    if mode == "output_only":
   123→        return _PREAMBLE_OUTPUT_ONLY
   124→    return _PREAMBLE_SELF_RECORD
   125→
   126→
   127→def render_cli_reference(cli_command: str = "desloppify") -> str:
   128→    """Render the CLI reference with the exact command prefix to execute."""
   129→    return _CLI_REFERENCE_TEMPLATE.format(cli_command=cli_command)
   130→
   131→
   132→__all__ = [

   135→    "_STAGES",
   136→    "render_cli_reference",
   137→    "triage_prompt_preamble",
   138→]

    46→    detail: dict[str, Any] = {
    47→        "holistic": True,
    48→        "dimension": dimension,
    49→        "related_files": related_files,
    50→        "evidence": evidence,
    51→        "suggestion": suggestion,
    52→        "reasoning": reasoning,
    53→    }
    54→    detail["summary_hash"] = content_hash
    55→    return detail
    56→
    57→
    58→def validate_and_build_issues(
    59→    issues_list: list[ReviewIssuePayload],
    60→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    61→    lang_name: str,
    62→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    63→    """Validate raw holistic issues and build state-ready issue dicts.
    64→
    65→    Returns (review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns).
    66→    """
    67→    review_issues: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    68→    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    69→    dismissed_concerns: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    70→    allowed_dimensions = {
    71→        dim for dim in holistic_prompts if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    for idx, raw_issue in enumerate(issues_list):
    75→        issue, issue_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
    76→            raw_issue,
    77→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
    78→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions,
    79→            allow_dismissed=True,
    80→        )
    81→        if issue_errors:
    82→            skipped.append(
    83→                {
    84→                    "index": idx,
    85→                    "missing": issue_errors,
    86→                    "identifier": (
    87→                        raw_issue.get("identifier", "<none>")
    88→                        if isinstance(raw_issue, dict)
    89→                        else "<none>"
    90→                    ),
    91→                }
    92→            )
    93→            continue
    94→        if issue is None:
    95→            raise ValueError(
    96→                "review issue payload missing after validation succeeded"
    97→            )
    98→
    99→        if issue.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed":
   100→            fingerprint = issue.get("concern_fingerprint", "")
   101→            if fingerprint:
   102→                dismissed_concerns.append(
   103→                    {
   104→                        "fingerprint": fingerprint,
   105→                        "concern_type": issue.get("concern_type", ""),
   106→                        "concern_file": issue.get("concern_file", ""),
   107→                        "reasoning": issue.get("reasoning", ""),
   108→                    }
   109→                )
   110→            continue
   111→
   112→        summary_text = str(issue.get("summary", ""))
   113→        if summary_text.lower().startswith(_POSITIVE_PREFIXES):
   114→            skipped.append(
   115→                {
   116→                    "index": idx,
   117→                    "missing": ["positive observation (not a defect)"],
   118→                    "identifier": issue.get("identifier", "<none>"),
   119→                }
   120→            )
   121→            continue
   122→
   123→        dimension = issue["dimension"]
   124→
   125→        is_confirmed_concern = issue.get("concern_verdict") == "confirmed"
   126→        detector = "concerns" if is_confirmed_concern else "review"
   127→
   128→        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(summary_text.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
   129→        detail = _build_holistic_detail(
   130→            content_hash=content_hash,
   131→            dimension=dimension,
   132→            related_files=issue["related_files"],
   133→            evidence=issue["evidence"],
   134→            suggestion=issue.get("suggestion", ""),
   135→            reasoning=issue.get("reasoning", ""),
   136→        )
   137→        if is_confirmed_concern:
   138→            detail["concern_type"] = issue.get("concern_type", "")
   139→            detail["concern_verdict"] = "confirmed"
   140→
   141→        prefix = "concern" if is_confirmed_concern else "holistic"
   142→        issue_file = issue.get("concern_file", "") if is_confirmed_concern else ""
   143→        confidence = normalize_review_confidence(issue.get("confidence", "low"))
   144→        imported = make_issue(
   145→            detector=detector,
   146→            file=issue_file,
   147→            name=f"{prefix}::{dimension}::{issue['identifier']}",
   148→            tier=review_tier(confidence, holistic=True),
   149→            confidence=confidence,
   150→            summary=summary_text,
   151→            detail=detail,
   152→        )
   153→        imported["lang"] = lang_name
   154→        review_issues.append(imported)
   155→
   156→    return review_issues, skipped, dismissed_concerns
   157→
   158→
   159→def collect_imported_dimensions(
   160→    *,
   161→    issues_list: list[ReviewIssuePayload],
   162→    review_issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   163→    assessments: dict[str, Any] | None,
   164→    review_scope: ReviewScopePayload | dict[str, Any] | None,
   165→    valid_dimensions: set[str],
   166→) -> set[str]:
   167→    """Return normalized dimensions this import explicitly covered."""
   168→    imported_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   169→
   170→    if isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   171→        scope_dims = review_scope.get("imported_dimensions")
   172→        if isinstance(scope_dims, list):
   173→            for raw_dim in scope_dims:
   174→                normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   175→                if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   176→                    imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   177→
   178→    for issue in issues_list:
   179→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   180→            continue
   181→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(issue.get("dimension", "")))
   182→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   183→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   184→
   185→    for issue in review_issues:
   186→        detail = issue.get("detail")
   187→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   188→            continue
   189→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   190→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   191→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   192→
   193→    for raw_dim in (assessments or {}):
   194→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   195→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   196→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   197→
   198→    return imported_dimensions
   199→
   200→
   201→def auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   202→    state: StateModel,
   203→    new_ids: set[str],
   204→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   205→    utc_now_fn,
   206→    *,
   207→    imported_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   208→    full_sweep_included: bool | None = None,
   209→) -> None:
   210→    """Auto-resolve open holistic issues not present in the latest import."""
   211→    scope_dimensions = {
   212→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   213→        for dim in (imported_dimensions or set())
   214→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   215→    }
   216→    scoped_reimport = full_sweep_included is False
   217→    if scoped_reimport and not scope_dimensions:
   218→        return
   219→
   220→    def _should_resolve(issue: Issue) -> bool:
   221→        if issue.get("detector") not in ("review", "concerns"):
   222→            return False
   223→        detail = issue.get("detail")
   224→        if not isinstance(detail, dict) or not detail.get("holistic"):
   225→            return False
   226→        if not scoped_reimport:
   227→            return True
   228→        dimension = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   229→        return dimension in scope_dimensions
   230→
   231→    auto_resolve_review_issues(
   232→        state,
   233→        new_ids=new_ids,
   234→        diff=diff,
   235→        note="not reported in latest holistic re-import",
   236→        should_resolve=_should_resolve,
   237→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "auto_resolve_stale_holistic",
   243→    "collect_imported_dimensions",
   244→    "validate_and_build_issues",
   245→]
   246→
```

    28→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    29→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    30→    try:
    31→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    32→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} issues to {output_file}", "green"))
    33→    except OSError as exc:
    34→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    35→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    36→        return False
    37→    return True
    38→
    39→
    40→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    41→    """Group issues by file and sort by descending count."""
    42→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    43→    for issue in matches:
    44→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    45→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    46→
    47→
    48→def _print_single_issue(issue: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    49→    """Render a single issue to terminal."""
    50→    normalized_status = canonical_issue_status(issue.get("status"))
    51→    status_icon = {
    52→        "open": "○",
    53→        "fixed": "✓",
    54→        "wontfix": "—",
    55→        "false_positive": "✗",
    56→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    57→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    58→    zone = issue.get("zone", "production")
    59→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    60→    print(
    61→        f"    {status_icon} T{issue['tier']} [{issue['confidence']}] {issue['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    62→    )
    63→
    64→    detail_parts = format_detail(issue.get("detail", {}))
    65→    if detail_parts:
    66→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    67→    if show_code:
    68→        detail_raw = issue.get("detail", {})
    69→        detail = detail_raw if isinstance(detail_raw, dict) else {}
    70→        target_line = (
    71→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    72→        )
    73→        if target_line and issue["file"] not in (".", ""):
    74→            snippet = read_code_snippet(issue["file"], target_line)
    75→            if snippet:
    76→                print(snippet)
    77→    if issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"      ⟳ reopened {issue['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    81→                "red",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→    if issue.get("note"):
    85→        print(colorize(f"      note: {issue['note']}", "dim"))
    86→    print(colorize(f"      {issue['id']}", "dim"))
    87→
    88→
    89→def render_issues(
    90→    matches: list[dict],
    91→    *,
    92→    pattern: str,
    93→    status_filter: str,
    94→    show_code: bool,
    95→    top: int,

    98→    noise_budget: int,
    99→    global_noise_budget: int,
   100→    budget_warning: str | None,
   101→) -> None:
   102→    """Render grouped issues and rollup summary to terminal."""
   103→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
   104→    print(
   105→        colorize(
   106→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} issues matching '{pattern}'\n",
   107→            "bold",

   109→    )
   110→    if budget_warning:
   111→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   112→    if hidden_total:

   115→        )

   118→        )
   119→        print(

   125→                ),

   127→            )
   128→        )
   129→
   130→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   131→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   132→    remaining_issues = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   133→
   134→    for filepath, issues in shown_files:
   135→        issues.sort(

   139→            )

   142→        print(

   144→            + colorize(f"  ({len(issues)} issues)", "dim")
   145→        )
   146→
   147→        for issue in issues:

   203→                    )

   211→def show_agent_plan(
   212→    narrative: dict, matches: list[dict], *, plan: dict | None = None
   213→) -> None:
   214→    """Render a compact plan from current issues and narrative actions.
   215→
   216→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   217→    """
   218→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
   219→        render_plan_with_living_plan(
   220→            [],
   221→            plan=plan,
   222→            header="  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next` to see your next task):",
   223→        )
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   227→    if not actions and not matches:
   228→        return
   229→
   230→    print(
   231→        colorize(
   232→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   233→            "yellow",
   234→        )
   235→    )
   236→    if actions:
   237→        top = actions[0]
   238→        print(
   239→            colorize(
   240→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}",
   241→                "cyan",
   242→            )
   243→        )
   244→    elif matches:
   245→        first = matches[0]
   246→        print(
   247→            colorize(
   248→                "  Agent focus: `desloppify next --count 20` — "
   249→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   250→                "cyan",
   251→            )
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   255→        print()
   256→
   257→
   258→def show_subjective_followup(
   259→    state: dict, target_strict_score: float, *, objective_backlog: int = 0,
   260→) -> None:
   261→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   262→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   263→    if not dim_scores:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   267→        state,
   268→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   269→    )
   270→    if not subjective:
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   274→        state,
   275→        subjective,
   276→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   277→        max_quality_items=3,
   278→        max_integrity_items=5,
   279→    )
   280→    if print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=objective_backlog):
   281→        print()
   282→

    53→    @classmethod
    54→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ShowOptions:
    55→        return cls(
    56→            pattern_raw=str(getattr(args, "pattern", "") or ""),
    57→            show_code=bool(getattr(args, "code", False)),
    58→            chronic=bool(getattr(args, "chronic", False)),

    64→
    65→def _handle_special_entity_views(
    66→    *,
    67→    entity,
    68→    state: dict,
    69→    config: dict,
    70→    lang_name: str | None,
    71→    pattern_raw: str,
    72→) -> bool:
    73→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    74→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    75→        return True
    76→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    77→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    78→        return True

    80→
    81→

    92→        return pattern, load_matches(
    93→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    94→        )
    95→    matches = _load_dimension_issues(state, entity, status_filter)
    96→    if not matches:
    97→        _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    98→        return None
    99→    return entity.display_name, matches
   100→
   101→
   102→def _active_plan_or_none() -> dict | None:
   103→    try:
   104→        plan = load_plan()
   105→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   106→        logger.debug("Show command skipped active-plan lookup.", exc_info=exc)
   107→        return None
   108→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"):
   109→        return plan
   110→    return None
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Show all issues for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   115→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   116→    state = runtime.state
   117→    config = runtime.config
   118→
   119→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   123→    if skill_warning:
   124→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   125→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   126→    if config_warning:
   127→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   128→
   129→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   130→
   131→    opts = ShowOptions.from_args(args)
   132→
   133→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   134→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   135→        return
   136→
   137→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   138→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   139→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   140→        state,
   141→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   145→    if _handle_special_entity_views(
   146→        entity=entity,
   147→        state=state,
   148→        config=config,
   149→        lang_name=lang_name,
   150→        pattern_raw=opts.pattern_raw,
   151→    ):
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    match_result = _load_entity_matches(
   155→        state=state,
   156→        entity=entity,
   157→        pattern=pattern,
   158→        status_filter=status_filter,
   159→        scope=scope,
   160→        chronic=opts.chronic,
   161→    )
   162→    if match_result is None:
   163→        return
   164→    pattern, matches = match_result
   165→
   166→    if not matches:
   167→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    (
   171→        surfaced_matches,
   172→        hidden_by_detector,
   173→        noise_budget,
   174→        global_noise_budget,
   175→        budget_warning,
   176→    ) = resolve_noise(
   177→        config,
   178→        matches,
   179→        no_budget=opts.no_budget,
   180→    )
   181→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   182→
   183→    payload = build_show_payload(
   184→        surfaced_matches,
   185→        pattern,
   186→        status_filter,
   187→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   188→            total_matches=len(matches),
   189→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   190→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   191→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   192→        ),
   193→    )
   194→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   195→
   196→    if opts.output_file:
   197→        if write_show_output_file(opts.output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   198→            return
   199→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   200→
   201→    render_issues(
   202→        surfaced_matches,
   203→        pattern=pattern,
   204→        status_filter=status_filter,
   205→        show_code=opts.show_code,
   206→        top=opts.top,
   207→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   208→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   209→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   210→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   211→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   212→    )
   213→    plan_active = _active_plan_or_none()
   214→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   215→    show_subjective_followup(
   216→        state,
   217→        target_strict_score_from_config(config),
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)

1→"""Concerns special-view rendering helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine._concerns.generators import generate_concerns
     7→
     8→
     9→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    10→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    11→    concerns = generate_concerns(state)
    12→    if not concerns:
    13→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    14→        return
    15→
    16→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    17→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    18→
    19→    for i, concern in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    20→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{concern.type}] {concern.file}", "cyan"))
    21→        print(f"     {concern.summary}")
    22→        for evidence in concern.evidence:
    23→            print(colorize(f"       - {evidence}", "dim"))
    24→        print(colorize(f"     ? {concern.question}", "yellow"))
    25→
    26→        prev = dismissals.get(concern.fingerprint)
    27→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
    28→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    29→            if reasoning:
    30→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    31→        print()
    32→
    33→
    34→__all__ = ["_show_concerns"]
    35→

1→"""Payload builders for show command query/output serialization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→
    10→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    11→class ShowPayloadMeta:
    12→    total_matches: int | None = None
    13→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int] | None = None
    14→    noise_budget: int | None = None
    15→    global_noise_budget: int | None = None
    16→
    17→
    18→def build_show_payload(
    19→    matches: list[dict[str, Any]],
    20→    pattern: str,
    21→    status_filter: str,
    22→    meta: ShowPayloadMeta | None = None,
    23→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    24→    """Build the structured JSON payload shared by query file and --output."""
    25→    resolved_meta = meta or ShowPayloadMeta()
    26→
    27→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    28→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    29→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
    30→    for issue in matches:
    31→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    32→        by_detector[issue["detector"]] += 1
    33→        by_tier[issue["tier"]] += 1
    34→
    35→    payload = {
    36→        "query": pattern,
    37→        "status_filter": status_filter,
    38→        "total": len(matches),
    39→        "summary": {
    40→            "by_tier": {f"T{tier}": count for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())},
    41→            "by_detector": dict(sorted(by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1])),
    42→            "files": len(by_file),
    43→        },
    44→        "by_file": {
    45→            fp: [
    46→                {
    47→                    "id": f["id"],
    48→                    "tier": f["tier"],
    49→                    "confidence": f["confidence"],
    50→                    "summary": f["summary"],
    51→                    "detail": f.get("detail", {}),
    52→                }
    53→                for f in fs
    54→            ]
    55→            for fp, fs in sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1]))
    56→        },
    57→    }
    58→    if resolved_meta.total_matches is not None:
    59→        payload["total_matching"] = resolved_meta.total_matches
    60→    if resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector:
    61→        payload["hidden"] = {
    62→            "by_detector": resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector,
    63→            "total": sum(resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector.values()),
    64→        }
    65→    if resolved_meta.noise_budget is not None:
    66→        payload["noise_budget"] = resolved_meta.noise_budget
    67→    if resolved_meta.global_noise_budget is not None:
    68→        payload["noise_global_budget"] = resolved_meta.global_noise_budget
    69→    return payload
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = ["ShowPayloadMeta", "build_show_payload"]
    73→
```
